# Test specification — RTK2 v56

This is a source-derived catalogue of every validation entry point in this checkout. It records declared test cases and parameterized groups, not a claimed execution count. Run the scripts to expand seeded, roster, region, viewport and native-vector loops. Every assertion in the linked source is part of the success contract; the table shows the exact assertion expressions. Setup excerpts identify case-specific inputs; shared helpers and full input mutation sequences are accessible at the linked line range. No validation script was removed during this maintenance.

## Running and interpreting results

Run from the `rtk2/` directory after `npm ci` with Node 24 (jsdom 30 requires a compatible modern Node). `npm test` runs the engine fingerprint check plus the core/versioned suites. `npm run test:ui` includes the older UI suites; `npm run test:v56:ui` runs the 24-vector mounted raster comparison and current UI regression suite; `npm run test:audio:ui` is the v55 UI suite. `node tests/asset-layout-tests.mjs` checks the maintenance manifest, media bytes, grouped directory references and retired-file absence. Native Python probes are separate: they require legally supplied private DOS inputs, Python and Unicorn and are not run by npm test.

A case succeeds only if all assertions execute and pass; a thrown assertion or nonzero process exit fails the script. Named helpers print PASS/FAIL plus their group summary; straight-line scripts print their listed PASS checkpoints. Some helpers collect failures until completion; absence of a crash or existence of an output JSON alone is not sufficient. Native comparison files must have the expected case count and all pass flags true. Raster tests validate canvas calls and state/RNG stability, not physical screen layout or human hearing.

The previous v56 verification records remain [verification-v56.json](verification-v56.json). They describe the earlier checkout; the current asset-move validation must use the new CI run. The older all-UI suite has recorded fixed-delay/timing limitations and is not certified by a v56-only UI pass. Existing nine AI profile files are retained unchanged. Tests of tiny optimizer/curriculum fixtures are validation code, not authorization to renew production profiles; no `train:ai` or `train:governance` command is run by maintenance.

## Retention review and coverage limits

Do not infer that a script never caught a bug merely because its filename is old. [bug-validation.md](bug-validation.md) connects reported bugs to behavioral coverage; [pending-test-case-design.md](pending-test-case-design.md) records exceptional missing tests. The v56 before/after commander-capture witnesses and the driver-export truncation report are positive historical detection evidence. Where no historical failure record exists, detection history is unknown, not “never caught”.

Candidates for later review include v9/v28/v29 artwork snapshot/file-size checks, v36 synthetic disaster-file existence checks even where native cues now play, overlapping audio observer suites and older copied UI suites. They remain intact until their behavioral and integration assertions are separated from obsolete asset checks. All 21 native music exports are retained because audio-v50 verifies their bytes and driver capture, despite being inactive BGM. These checks cannot establish original hardware waveform parity, complete original caller coverage, real browser autoplay behavior, physical-phone sound/layout, commercial permissions, or unobserved native AI branches.

Source: [tests/artwork-tests.mjs](../tests/artwork-tests.mjs). Purpose: full portrait, event and weather atlas cells preserve their ratio in thirty wide and tall frames, with centred letterboxing; all 352 officer cells contain distinct raster images and retain their ratio in the battle portrait frame; five new full-dimension artworks and every formation/capital cell scale uniformly; all eight frames retain alpha and distinct content; additional cases below.

## tests/audio-v50-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/audio-v50-tests.mjs](../tests/audio-v50-tests.mjs). Purpose: all 16 catalogued native effect replacements exist and route by bounded ID; province orders use semantic cues independently of dialogue flow; governance events match verified native effect IDs and AI council/order semantic cues; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../assets/sounds/native-effect-${id}.mp3`, `./assets/sounds/march.mp3`, `./assets/sounds/freesound_community-sword-battle-jingle-loop-96983.mp3`, `./assets/sounds/message.mp3`, `./assets/sounds/order.mp3`, `../assets/music/native-music-${id}.mp3`, `./assets/music/council-music.mp3`, `./assets/music/battle-music-v17.mp3`, `./assets/music/triumph-music.mp3`, `../docs/native-opl-driver-v50.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:assert/strict`, `node:fs`, `node:crypto`, `../mjs/audio.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |

## tests/audio-v52-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/audio-v52-tests.mjs](../tests/audio-v52-tests.mjs). Purpose: restored remaster governance/battle loops are stable across province regions; combat without queued animation plays once instead of silently deferring forever; successful/failed fireballs and animated melee use the unlocked cached sound session; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `./assets/music/council-music.mp3`, `./assets/music/battle-music-v17.mp3`, `./assets/music/triumph-music.mp3`, `audiomass-charge_scream.mp3`, `freesound_community-sword-battle-jingle-loop-96983.mp3`, `/fire.mp3`, `/trade.mp3`, `./assets/sounds/trade.mp3`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/audio.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [restored remaster governance/battle loops are stable across province regions](../tests/audio-v52-tests.mjs#L1-L10) | const flush=async()=&gt;{for(let n=0;n&lt;8;n++)await Promise.resolve();}; const listeners=new Map(),media=[],sources=[],chunks=new Map(),fetches=[];let notices=[]; globalThis.document={hidden:false,addEventListener(type,fn){listeners.set(type,fn);},removeEventListener(type){listeners.delete(type);}}; globalThis.Audio=class{constructor(src){this.src=src;this.paused=true;this.currentTime=0;media.push(this);}play(){this.paused=false;return Promise.resolve();}pause(){this.paused=true;}}; globalThis.AudioContext=class{constructor(){this.state='suspended';this.destination={};}resume(){this.state='running';return Promise.resolve();}close(){this.state='closed';return Promise.resolve();}decodeAudioData(da … (complete input/procedure at linked L1–10) | assert.equal(bus.track.src,'./assets/music/council-music.mp3')<br>assert.equal(bus.track,council)<br>assert(council.paused)<br>assert.equal(bus.track.src,'./assets/music/battle-music-v17.mp3')<br>assert(bus.track.loop)<br>assert.equal(bus.track.src,'./assets/music/triumph-music.mp3')<br>assert(!bus.track.loop)<br>assert.equal(bus.track.src,'./assets/music/council-music.mp3') |
| 2. [combat without queued animation plays once instead of silently deferring forever](../tests/audio-v52-tests.mjs#L10-L12) | ; for(const type of ['attack','simultaneous','charge']){const b={lastAction:{type,sequence:1,messageSerial:1,combatUnits:[{id:1}]},messages:[{serial:1,text:'Resolved combat'}]},before=sources.length;bus.playBattle(b,0,false);await flush();;;bus.playBattle(b,0,false);await flush();;} | assert.equal(sources.length,before+1)<br>assert(sources.slice(before).some(s=&gt;s.buffer.src.includes(type==='charge'?'audiomass-charge_scream.mp3':'freesound_community-sword-battle-jingle-loop-96983.mp3')))<br>assert.equal(sources.length,before+1) |
| 3. [successful/failed fireballs and animated melee use the unlocked cached sound session](../tests/audio-v52-tests.mjs#L12-L15) | ; for(const result of ['success','failed']){const b={lastAction:{type:'fireball',sequence:1,messageSerial:1},messages:[{serial:1,text:'Fireball '+result}]},before=sources.length;bus.playBattle(b,0,true);await flush();;;} const b={lastAction:{type:'attack',sequence:1,messageSerial:1,combatUnits:[{id:1}]},messages:[{serial:1,text:'Attacked'}]},before=sources.length;bus.playBattle(b,0,true);await flush();;bus.playCombat(b.lastAction,1);await flush();;; | assert.equal(sources.length,before+1)<br>assert(sources.at(-1).buffer.src.endsWith('/fire.mp3'))<br>assert.equal(sources.length,before)<br>assert.equal(sources.length,before+1)<br>assert.equal(fetches.filter(url=&gt;url.includes('freesound_community-sword-battle-jingle-loop-96983.mp3')).length,1) |
| 4. [overlapping combat voices stay bounded while decoding each asset only once](../tests/audio-v52-tests.mjs#L15-L17) | ; const repeated=sources.length;for(let n=0;n&lt;20;n++)bus.playCombat({type:'simultaneous'},1);await flush();;;; | assert.equal(sources.length,repeated+8)<br>assert(bus.effects.size&lt;=8)<br>assert(sources.slice(0,repeated).every(s=&gt;s.stopped)) |
| 5. [muted/hidden/stopped pending effects cannot start late and playback failures are visible](../tests/audio-v52-tests.mjs#L17-L19) | ; let resolveTrade;globalThis.fetch=url=&gt;url.endsWith('/trade.mp3')?new Promise(resolve=&gt;{resolveTrade=resolve;}):Promise.resolve({ok:false});const pending=sources.length;bus.play('trade');bus.configure({sound:false,music:true});const trade=new ArrayBuffer(4);chunks.set(trade,'./assets/sounds/trade.mp3');resolveTrade({ok:true,arrayBuffer:async()=&gt;trade});await flush();;;bus.configure({sound:true,music:false});;bus.play('fire');document.hidden=true;listeners.get('visibilitychange')();await flush();;document.hidden=false;listeners.get('visibilitychange')();bus.play('recruit');await flush();;;bus.destroy();; | assert.equal(sources.length,pending)<br>assert.equal(bus.effects.size,0)<br>assert(bus.track.paused)<br>assert.equal(sources.length,pending)<br>assert.equal(notices.length,1)<br>assert(bus.audioError)<br>assert.equal(bus.context.state,'closed') |
| 6. [HTML audio fallback reports blocked playback instead of silently swallowing errors](../tests/audio-v52-tests.mjs#L19-L21) | ; delete globalThis.AudioContext;globalThis.fetch=async()=&gt;({ok:false});const oldPlay=globalThis.Audio.prototype.play;globalThis.Audio.prototype.play=function(){if(this.src.includes('native-effect-13'))return Promise.reject(Object.assign(Error('Blocked'),{name:'NotAllowedError'}));return oldPlay.call(this);};const fallback=new AudioBus();notices=[];fallback.notice=text=&gt;notices.push(text);fallback.unlock();fallback.setActive(true);fallback.playNative(13);await flush();;;fallback.destroy(); | assert.equal(notices.length,1)<br>assert.equal(fallback.audioError,'NotAllowedError') |

| 1. [BUG-56-05 unlocked Web Audio plays exact gallop loop even when delayed HTML playback is denied](../tests/audio-v56-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('BUG-56-05 unlocked Web Audio plays exact gallop loop even when delayed HTML playback is denied',async bus=&gt;{const before=media.filter(a=&gt;a.src.endsWith(horse)).reduce((n,a)=&gt;n+a.plays,0);bus.setCourierMoving(true);await flush();const s=sources.at(-1);;;;;bus.setCourierMoving(true);await flush();;bus.setCourierMoving(false);;}) | assert(s?.buffer.src.endsWith(horse))<br>assert(s.loop)<br>assert.equal(s.gain.gain.value,1)<br>assert.equal(media.filter(a=&gt;a.src.endsWith(horse)).reduce((n,a)=&gt;n+a.plays,0),before)<br>assert.equal(sources.at(-1),s)<br>assert(s.stopped) |
| 2. [BUG-56-05 outward/return moving animation clock starts loop and stops at 650ms, interception, proposal, stale route and exit](../tests/audio-v56-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('BUG-56-05 outward/return moving animation clock starts loop and stops at 650ms, interception, proposal, stale route and exit',async bus=&gt;{for(const phase of ['outbound','return']){const g={s:{journey:{phase,legs:[{route:[9,8],index:1}],leg:0}},journeyMotion:{from:9,to:8,start:1000}};for(const [time,moving]of [[999,false],[1000,true],[1325,true],[1649,true],[1650,false]]){const motion=courierMotion(g,time);;bus.setCourierMoving(motion.moving);await flush();;}for(const flag of ['interception','proposal']){g.s.journey[flag]={};;delete g.s.journey[flag];}g.s.journey.legs[0].index=0;;g.s.journey=null;;}bus.setCourierMoving(true);await flush();const s=sources.at(-1);bus.stop();;}) | assert.equal(motion.moving,moving)<br>assert.equal(!!bus.courierEffect,moving)<br>assert(!courierMotion(g,1200).moving)<br>assert(!courierMotion(g,1200).moving)<br>assert(!courierMotion(g,1200).moving)<br>assert(s.stopped&amp;&amp;!bus.courierEffect) |
| 3. [BUG-56-05 delayed decode cannot start after motion cancelled or page hidden](../tests/audio-v56-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('BUG-56-05 delayed decode cannot start after motion cancelled or page hidden',async bus=&gt;{;const before=sources.length;bus.setCourierMoving(true);bus.setCourierMoving(false);decodeReady();await flush();;bus.setCourierMoving(true);document.hidden=true;listeners.get('visibilitychange')();await flush();;}) | assert(decodeReady)<br>assert.equal(sources.length,before)<br>assert.equal(sources.length,before) |
| 4. [BUG-56-05 decode/fetch failure clears pending loop and permits retry without duplicate voices](../tests/audio-v56-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('BUG-56-05 decode/fetch failure clears pending loop and permits retry without duplicate voices',async bus=&gt;{bus.buffers.delete('gallop');failFetch=true;bus.setCourierMoving(true);await flush();;bus.setCourierMoving(true);await flush();;const s=bus.courierEffect.source;bus.configure({sound:false,music:true});;}) | assert.equal(bus.courierEffect,null)<br>assert(bus.courierEffect?.source?.loop)<br>assert(s.stopped) |
| 5. [BUG-56-05 HTML fallback uses exact moving gallop; mute/background/destroy pause it](../tests/audio-v56-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('BUG-56-05 HTML fallback uses exact moving gallop; mute/background/destroy pause it',async bus=&gt;{bus.setCourierMoving(true);const a=media.at(-1);;bus.configure({sound:false});;bus.configure({sound:true});const next=media.at(-1);document.hidden=true;listeners.get('visibilitychange')();;document.hidden=false;listeners.get('visibilitychange')();const final=media.at(-1);bus.destroy();;}) | assert(a.src.endsWith(horse)&amp;&amp;a.loop&amp;&amp;!a.paused)<br>assert(a.paused)<br>assert(next.paused)<br>assert(final.paused) |

<a id="tests-battle-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/battle-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/battle-tests.mjs](../tests/battle-tests.mjs). Purpose: Hex geometry provides six vertical and diagonal directions for both column parities; All 41 original province terrain layouts are retained exactly; Deployment highlights passable edge hexes for attackers and rejects illegal placements atomically; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `../mjs/geography.mjs`, `../mjs/battle-presentation.mjs`, `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Hex geometry provides six vertical and diagonal directions for both column parities](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Hex geometry provides six vertical and diagonal directions for both column parities',()=&gt;{for(const q of [5,6]){const ns=neighbors(q,6);;;for(const n of ns);};}) | assert.equal(ns.length,6)<br>assert.deepEqual(ns,DIRECTIONS.map((_,i)=&gt;direction(q,6,i)))<br>assert.equal(distance({q,r:6},n),1)<br>assert.equal(distance({q:6,r:6},{q:7,r:7}),2) |
| 2. [All 41 original province terrain layouts are retained exactly](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('All 41 original province terrain layouts are retained exactly',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();for(const p of g.s.provinces){const source=g.province(p.neighbors[0]);if(source.owner===255)source.owner=0;if(p.owner===255)p.owner=1;const id=source.officers[0]??0;;}}) | assert.deepEqual(spawnBattle(g,source,p,[id],20000,terrains[p.id-1]).terrain,terrains[p.id-1]) |
| 3. [Deployment highlights passable edge hexes for attackers and rejects illegal placements atomically](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Deployment highlights passable edge hexes for attackers and rejects illegal placements atomically',()=&gt;{const g=begin(fresh(),[0,34],false),b=g.s.battle,u=b.units[0],before=JSON.stringify(b);;;for(const cell of placementCells(b,'attack')){;;};;;const cell=placementCells(b,'attack')[0];g.battleAction('place',{unit:u.id,...cell});;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains);}) | assert.equal(b.phase,'deployment')<br>assert(b.units.every(u=&gt;!u.placed&amp;&amp;u.mobility===dailyMobility(g.officer(u.id).training)))<br>assert(entryEdge(cell.q,cell.r,b.entryDirection,b.target))<br>assert(Number.isFinite(terrainCost(b.terrain[cell.r*13+cell.q])))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.battleAction('place',{unit:u.id,q:6,r:6}))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.battleAction('deploy'))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(b),before)<br>assert(u.placed)<br>assert.equal(u.mobility,dailyMobility(g.officer(u.id).training)) |
| 4. [Both armies finish placement before defender-first combat; deployment saves restore](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Both armies finish placement before defender-first combat; deployment saves restore',()=&gt;{const g=begin(fresh(),[0,34],false);while(g.s.battle.phase==='deployment'&amp;&amp;g.s.battle.side==='attack')aiBattle(g,60);;;;const h=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains),{},g.terrains);;while(g.s.battle.phase==='deployment')aiBattle(g,60);;;;;}) | assert.equal(g.s.battle.side,'defend')<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle.phase,'deployment')<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle.messages.at(-1).text,'Defender’s turn')<br>assert.deepEqual(h.s.battle,g.s.battle)<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle.day,1)<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle.side,'defend')<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle.messages.at(-1).text,'Defender’s turn')<br>assert(g.s.battle.units.every(u=&gt;u.placed)) |
| 5. [Defender-first days update stores, fire and weather only after both armies finish](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L15-L19) | test('Defender-first days update stores, fire and weather only after both armies finish',()=&gt;{ const g=begin(),b=g.s.battle,u=b.units[0];b.food={attack:1000000,defend:1000000};b.weather='sunny';b.fire={[u.r*13+u.q]:3};b.wind=0;g.random=()=&gt;.5; const food={...b.food},men=u.soldiers;g.battleAction('end');;;;;;;; g.battleAction('end');;;;;;;;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains); }) | assert.equal(b.side,'attack')<br>assert.equal(b.day,1)<br>assert.equal(b.completedDays,0)<br>assert.deepEqual(b.food,food)<br>assert.equal(u.soldiers,men)<br>assert.equal(b.fire[u.r*13+u.q],3)<br>assert.equal(b.messages.at(-1).text,'Attacker’s turn')<br>assert.equal(b.side,'defend')<br>assert.equal(b.day,2)<br>assert.equal(b.completedDays,1)<br>assert(b.food.attack&lt;food.attack&amp;&amp;b.food.defend&lt;food.defend)<br>assert(u.soldiers&lt;men)<br>assert.equal(b.fire[u.r*13+u.q],2)<br>assert.equal(b.messages.at(-1).text,'Defender’s turn')<br>assert(living(b).filter(u=&gt;u.side==='defend').every(u=&gt;!u.ordered))<br>assert(living(b).filter(u=&gt;u.side==='attack').every(u=&gt;u.ordered)) |
| 6. [Defender-first warfare suspends after 30 full days and resumes with the defender](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L20-L24) | test('Defender-first warfare suspends after 30 full days and resumes with the defender',()=&gt;{ const g=begin(),b=g.s.battle;b.food={attack:1000000,defend:1000000};g.s.monthlyReview=null;g.random=()=&gt;.5; for(let day=1;day&lt;=30;day++){;;g.battleAction('end');;;;g.battleAction('end');} ;;;b.resumeMonth=g.s.year*12+g.s.month-1;;;;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains); }) | assert.equal(b.side,'defend')<br>assert.equal(b.day,day)<br>assert.equal(b.side,'attack')<br>assert.equal(b.day,day)<br>assert(!b.suspended)<br>assert(b.suspended)<br>assert.equal(b.completedDays,30)<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle,null)<br>assert(g.resumeWars())<br>assert.equal(b.side,'defend')<br>assert.equal(b.day,1)<br>assert.equal(b.completedDays,0)<br>assert(living(b).every(u=&gt;!u.ordered)) |
| 7. [Mid-combat legacy saves retain their attacker-first cycle; invalid turn order is rejected](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L25-L27) | test('Mid-combat legacy saves retain their attacker-first cycle; invalid turn order is rejected',()=&gt;{ const {g,b}=arena();delete b.firstSide;const h=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains),{},g.terrains);h.battleAction('end');;;h.battleAction('end');;;validateSave(h.s,scenarios,h.terrains);const bad=structuredClone(h.s);bad.battle.firstSide='unknown';; }) | assert.equal(h.s.battle.side,'defend')<br>assert.equal(h.s.battle.day,1)<br>assert.equal(h.s.battle.side,'attack')<br>assert.equal(h.s.battle.day,2)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(bad,scenarios,h.terrains),/turn order/) |
| 8. [Read-only status playback names the acting general before action and casualty messages](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L28-L32) | test('Read-only status playback names the acting general before action and casualty messages',()=&gt;{ const {g,b,u,v}=arena();Object.assign(u,{q:6,r:5,int:89});Object.assign(v,{q:6,r:7});b.terrain[v.r*13+v.q]=1;const serial=b.eventSerial;g.random=()=&gt;.5;g.battleAction('move',{unit:u.id,q:6,r:6});const before=JSON.stringify(g.s),items=battleStatusMessages(b,serial,id=&gt;g.officer(id).name);;;;;; const next=b.eventSerial;g.battleAction('end');const ended=battleStatusMessages(b,next,id=&gt;g.officer(id).name,true);for(const m of b.messages.filter(m=&gt;m.serial&gt;next&amp;&amp;m.turnUnit!==undefined)){const index=ended.findIndex(x=&gt;x.text===m.text);;};; const rejected=JSON.stringify(g.s);;; }) | assert.equal(items[0].text,g.officer(u.id).name+'’s turn')<br>assert(items.some(x=&gt;x.text.includes('ambushed')))<br>assert(items.some(x=&gt;x.text.includes('moved')))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.deepEqual(battleStatusMessages(b,b.eventSerial,id=&gt;g.officer(id).name),[])<br>assert.equal(ended[index-1].text,g.officer(m.turnUnit).name+'’s turn')<br>assert.equal(ended.at(-2).text,'Defender’s turn')<br>assert.equal(ended.at(-1).text,g.officer(b.selected).name+'’s turn')<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.battleAction('move',{unit:b.selected,q:-1,r:-1}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),rejected) |
| 9. [Personal challenge status announces the responding general before the result](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L33-L35) | test('Personal challenge status announces the responding general before the result',()=&gt;{ const {g,b,u,v}=arena();u.war=100;const serial=b.eventSerial;g.battleAction('challenge',{unit:u.id,target:v.id});;const response=b.eventSerial;g.battleAction('challengeResponse',{accept:false});; }) | assert.equal(battleStatusMessages(b,serial,id=&gt;g.officer(id).name)[0].text,g.officer(u.id).name+'’s turn')<br>assert.equal(battleStatusMessages(b,response,id=&gt;g.officer(id).name)[0].text,g.officer(v.id).name+'’s turn') |

[AI status names the policy-selected general without announcing a different selected unit](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L36-L39) | test('AI status names the policy-selected general without announcing a different selected unit',()=&gt;{ const opening=begin(),start=opening.s.battle;; const {g,b,u,f}=arena();u.war=20;f.war=100;b.selected=u.id;const serial=b.eventSerial;aiBattle(g,90);;const snapshot=JSON.stringify(g.s),items=battleStatusMessages(b,serial,id=&gt;g.officer(id).name,false);;; }) | assert.deepEqual(battleStatusMessages(start,start.eventSerial-1,id=&gt;opening.officer(id).name,false).map(x=&gt;x.text),['Defender’s turn'])<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.unit,f.id)<br>assert.equal(items[0].text,g.officer(f.id).name+'’s turn')<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),snapshot) |

[Army equipment changes actual combat losses and purchasing equips a recipient without creating soldiers](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L83-L90) | test('Army equipment changes actual combat losses and purchasing equips a recipient without creating soldiers',()=&gt;{ const bare=arena(),armed=arena(),partial=arena();bare.u.weapons=0;partial.u.weapons=2500; for(const x of [bare,partial,armed]){x.g.random=()=&gt;.5;x.g.battleAction('attack',{unit:x.u.id,target:x.v.id});} ;; const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),buyer=g.officer(34),recipient=g.officer(33);g.s.monthlyReview=null;p.merchant=true;buyer.acted=false;recipient.weapons=0;const men=recipient.soldiers,gold=p.gold; g.execute('trade',{province:p.id,officer:buyer.id,recipient:recipient.id,mode:'arms',amount:17}); ;;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains); }) | assert(bare.v.soldiers&gt;partial.v.soldiers)<br>assert(partial.v.soldiers&gt;armed.v.soldiers)<br>assert.equal(p.gold,gold-17)<br>assert.equal(recipient.weapons,1700)<br>assert.equal(recipient.soldiers,men)<br>assert(buyer.acted) |

[Battle HUD weapons validate atomically and persist through settlement and suspended wars](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L91-L95) | test('Battle HUD weapons validate atomically and persist through settlement and suspended wars',()=&gt;{ const x=arena();x.g.cheat('battle',x.u.id,{weapons:1234});;const before=JSON.stringify(x.g.s);;; const y=arena();y.u.weapons=2345;y.b.suspended=true;y.g.suspendBattle();;; const z=arena();z.u.weapons=3456;z.b.outcome={winner:'attack',reason:'Test victory'};z.g.resolveBattle();;validateSave(z.g.s,scenarios,z.g.terrains); }) | assert.equal(x.u.weapons,1234)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;x.g.cheat('battle',x.u.id,{weapons:10001}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(x.g.s),before)<br>assert.equal(y.g.officer(y.u.id).weapons,2345)<br>assert.equal(validateSave(y.g.s,scenarios,y.g.terrains).wars[0].units.find(u=&gt;u.id===y.u.id).weapons,2345)<br>assert.equal(z.g.officer(z.u.id).weapons,3456) |

[A terminal commander charge keeps the occupation coordinate in the victory report](../tests/battle-tests.mjs#L96-L99) | test('A terminal commander charge keeps the occupation coordinate in the victory report',()=&gt;{ const {g,b,u,f,v,king}=arena();Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:0});Object.assign(v,{q:0,r:2});Object.assign(king,{q:7,r:6,soldiers:1});b.leaders.defend=king.id;const target={q:king.q,r:king.r}; g.battleAction('charge',{unit:u.id,target:king.id});;;;;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains); }) | assert.equal(king.soldiers,0)<br>assert.deepEqual({q:u.q,r:u.r},target)<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle,null)<br>assert(g.s.triumph)<br>assert.equal(g.s.lastBattle.chargeLanding.kind,'occupation')<br>assert.deepEqual({q:g.s.lastBattle.chargeLanding.q,r:g.s.lastBattle.chargeLanding.r},target) |

## tests/completion-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/completion-tests.mjs](../tests/completion-tests.mjs). Purpose: Custom ruler/follower use separate stable IDs; historical officers and source scenarios remain unchanged after load; All four delegation policies and supply/attack targets persist; unused generals follow the selected policy; Province Rest preserves the faction until its remaining directly ruled provinces finish; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:assert/strict`, `node:fs`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/campaign-completion.mjs`, `../mjs/province-rules.mjs`, `../mjs/advice-topics.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-politics.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`.

Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

[Supply convoy deducts cargo at departure, loses only carried goods to bandits and delivers the remainder after save/load](../tests/completion-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Supply convoy deducts cargo at departure, loses only carried goods to bandits and delivers the remainder after save/load',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.execute('moveParty',{province:9,target:17,officers:[34],gold:0,food:0});g.random=()=&gt;0;const source=g.province(9),dest=g.province(17),gold=source.gold,before=dest.gold;g.execute('transport',{province:9,target:17,officer:33,gold:100,food:1000});;g.s=valid(g);g.advanceJourney();;g.random=()=&gt;.999;travel(g);;;valid(g);}) | assert.equal(source.gold,gold-100)<br>assert.equal(g.s.journey.cargo.gold,90)<br>assert.equal(g.province(17).gold,before+90)<br>assert.equal(g.province(9).gold,gold-100) |

[Protect/plunder and unique item recipient decisions persist and cannot repeat rewards](../tests/completion-tests.mjs#L18-L18) | test('Protect/plunder and unique item recipient decisions persist and cannot repeat rewards',()=&gt;{for(const mode of ['protect','plunder']){const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),trust=g.ruler().trust,war=g.officer(34).war;g.s.spoils=[{owner:0,province:9,item:'luminous'}];g.s=valid(g);g.random=()=&gt;.375;g.decideSpoils(mode,34);;;;;;;valid(g);}}) | assert.equal(g.officer(34).war,Math.min(100,war+8))<br>assert.equal(g.officer(34).loyalty,100)<br>assert.equal(g.s.items.length,1)<br>assert.equal(g.s.spoils.length,0)<br>assert.equal(g.ruler().trust,mode==='protect'?Math.min(100,trust+1):Math.max(0,trust-10))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.decideSpoils(mode,34)) |

<a id="tests-decisions-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/decisions-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/decisions-tests.mjs](../tests/decisions-tests.mjs). Purpose: No empty adjacent province captures every defending officer, including ruler/governor; checkpoint retains decisions; With an empty escape province, native WAR/training/mobility distinguish strong and weak field escape; Native ordinary captive recruitment completes once; beheading another captive removes membership permanently; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:assert/strict`, `node:fs`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/terrain-mask.mjs`, `../mjs/geography.mjs`, `../mjs/campaign-decisions.mjs`, `../mjs/game-ui.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [No empty adjacent province captures every defending officer, including ruler/governor; checkpoint retains decisions](../tests/decisions-tests.mjs#L18-L21) | test('No empty adjacent province captures every defending officer, including ruler/governor; checkpoint retains decisions',()=&gt;{ const {g,ids}=win();;;;; const restored=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrain),{},terrain);;check(restored); }) | assert.deepEqual(new Set(g.s.captiveDecisions.map(c=&gt;c.officer)),new Set(ids))<br>assert(g.s.captiveDecisions.some(c=&gt;c.role==='Ruler'))<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle,null)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.finishFactionTurn(),/captives/)<br>assert.deepEqual(restored.s.captiveDecisions,g.s.captiveDecisions) |
| 2. [With an empty escape province, native WAR/training/mobility distinguish strong and weak field escape](../tests/decisions-tests.mjs#L22-L25) | test('With an empty escape province, native WAR/training/mobility distinguish strong and weak field escape',()=&gt;{ const strong=win(true,true),weak=win(true,false);;; ;check(strong.g);check(weak.g); }) | assert.equal(strong.g.s.captiveDecisions.length,0)<br>assert.equal(weak.g.s.captiveDecisions.length,weak.ids.length)<br>assert(strong.ids.every(id=&gt;strong.g.s.provinces.some(p=&gt;p.id!==8&amp;&amp;p.officers.includes(id)))) |
| 3. [Native ordinary captive recruitment completes once; beheading another captive removes membership permanently](../tests/decisions-tests.mjs#L26-L29) | test('Native ordinary captive recruitment completes once; beheading another captive removes membership permanently',()=&gt;{ const {g,ids}=win(),ordinary=ids.filter(id=&gt;g.s.captiveDecisions.find(c=&gt;c.officer===id).role!=='Ruler');g.random=()=&gt;.999;;;; const id=ordinary[1];;;;;check(g); }) | assert.equal(g.decideCaptive(ordinary[0],'recruit'),true)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(ordinary[0]).prisonerOf,undefined)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.decideCaptive(ordinary[0],'recruit'),/already/)<br>assert(g.decideCaptive(id,'behead'))<br>assert(g.officer(id).dead)<br>assert(!g.s.provinces.some(p=&gt;['officers','hidden','unclaimed'].some(k=&gt;p[k].includes(id))))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.decideCaptive(id,'free'),/already/) |
| 4. [A captured ruler cannot be recruited and rejection does not spend RNG or alter the campaign](../tests/decisions-tests.mjs#L30-L32) | test('A captured ruler cannot be recruited and rejection does not spend RNG or alter the campaign',()=&gt;{ const {g}=win();const c=g.s.captiveDecisions.find(c=&gt;c.role==='Ruler'),before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;g.decideCaptive(c.officer,'free');check(g); }) | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.decideCaptive(c.officer,'recruit'),/only be set free or beheaded/)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before) |
| 5. [A released landless ruler continues in exile, moves through connected provinces and settles](../tests/decisions-tests.mjs#L33-L36) | test('A released landless ruler continues in exile, moves through connected provinces and settles',()=&gt;{ const {g,former}=win();g.s.humanRulers.push(former);for(const c of [...g.s.captiveDecisions])g.decideCaptive(c.officer,'free');const party=g.s.roaming.find(p=&gt;p.owner===former);;;check(g); g.s.player=former;g.s.selected=party.province;const empty=g.s.provinces.find(p=&gt;p.owner===255);;for(const id of realmRoute(g,party.province,empty.id).slice(1))g.roam('move',id);g.roam('settle');;;check(g); }) | assert(party)<br>assert(activeRulers(g.s).some(r=&gt;r.id===former))<br>assert(empty)<br>assert.equal(g.province(empty.id).owner,former)<br>assert.equal(g.roaming(),undefined) |
| 6. [Defeated human realms relinquish control after decisions; the last human gets a clear ended campaign](../tests/decisions-tests.mjs#L37-L39) | test('Defeated human realms relinquish control after decisions; the last human gets a clear ended campaign',()=&gt;{ for(const last of [false,true]){const {g,former}=win();g.s.humanRulers=last?[former]:[0,former];g.s.player=former;const c=g.s.captiveDecisions.find(c=&gt;c.role==='Ruler');g.decideCaptive(c.officer,'behead');for(const next of [...g.s.captiveDecisions])g.decideCaptive(next.officer,'free');;if(last){;;}else{;;}check(g);} }) | assert(!g.s.humanRulers.includes(former))<br>assert(g.s.campaignEnded&amp;&amp;g.s.paused)<br>assert.equal(g.aiStep(),false)<br>assert(!g.s.campaignEnded)<br>assert.notEqual(g.s.player,former) |
| 7. [A dispatched gift has no early effect, blocks extra orders and resumes after save/load](../tests/decisions-tests.mjs#L41-L44) | test('A dispatched gift has no early effect, blocks extra orders and resumes after save/load',()=&gt;{ const g=trip(),gold=g.province(9).gold,other=g.province(g.ruler(3).home).gold,seed=g.s.seed;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'gift',target:3,officer:34,amount:100});;;;;;; const loaded=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrain),{},terrain);loaded.random=()=&gt;0;let steps=0;while(loaded.s.journey){if(loaded.s.journey.proposal)loaded.answerProposal(true);else if(loaded.s.journey.interception)loaded.interceptJourney('free');else loaded.advanceJourney();;check(loaded);};;;check(loaded); }) | assert(g.s.journey)<br>assert.equal(g.province(9).gold,gold-100)<br>assert.equal(g.province(g.ruler(3).home).gold,other)<br>assert.equal(g.s.seed,seed)<br>assert(g.officer(34).acted&amp;&amp;g.officer(34).inTransit)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('develop',{province:9,officer:33,amount:10}),/journey/)<br>assert(++steps&lt;150)<br>assert.equal(loaded.province(9).gold,gold-100)<br>assert.equal(loaded.province(loaded.ruler(3).home).gold,other+100)<br>assert(!loaded.officer(34).inTransit) |

[Intercepted envoy can be captured or beheaded; cancelled missions transfer no gift and clear transit](../tests/decisions-tests.mjs#L45-L47) | test('Intercepted envoy can be captured or beheaded; cancelled missions transfer no gift and clear transit',()=&gt;{ for(const choice of ['capture','behead']){const g=trip(),gold=g.province(9).gold;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'gift',target:3,officer:34,amount:100});for(let n=0;n&lt;40&amp;&amp;!g.s.journey.interception;n++)g.advanceJourney();;g.interceptJourney(choice);;;;if(choice==='behead');else ;check(g);} }) | assert(g.s.journey.interception)<br>assert.equal(g.s.journey,null)<br>assert.equal(g.province(9).gold,gold-100)<br>assert(!g.officer(34).inTransit)<br>assert(g.officer(34).dead)<br>assert(g.s.captiveDecisions.some(c=&gt;c.officer===34)) |

[Invalid dispatch and invalid saved route reject atomically](../tests/decisions-tests.mjs#L48-L50) | test('Invalid dispatch and invalid saved route reject atomically',()=&gt;{ const g=trip(),before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'gift',target:3,officer:34,amount:100});const broken=structuredClone(g.s);broken.journey.legs[0].route=[9,41];; }) | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'gift',target:3,officer:34,amount:99}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(broken,scenarios,terrain),/route/) |

## tests/expansion-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/expansion-tests.mjs](../tests/expansion-tests.mjs). Purpose: General retreat requires a valid choice and rejects occupied, remote and missing destinations atomically; Empty neighbour retreat reserves the province and settles surviving troops after restored defeat; Defenders exclude the attacker source and can settle an empty connected province after conquest; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/monthly-events.mjs`, `../mjs/officer-roster.mjs`, `../mjs/portraits.mjs`, `../mjs/events.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [General retreat requires a valid choice and rejects occupied, remote and missing destinations atomically](../tests/expansion-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('General retreat requires a valid choice and rejects occupied, remote and missing destinations atomically',()=&gt;{const {g,b,f}=arena();const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);for(const province of [undefined,b.target,36]){;;}g.battleAction('flee',{unit:f.id,province:b.source});;;;const s=validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains);;}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.battleAction('flee',{unit:f.id,province}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert(f.fled)<br>assert.equal(f.retreatProvince,b.source)<br>assert(!b.units.find(x=&gt;x.id===b.leaders.attack).fled)<br>assert.equal(s.battle.units.find(x=&gt;x.id===f.id).retreatProvince,b.source) |
| 2. [Empty neighbour retreat reserves the province and settles surviving troops after restored defeat](../tests/expansion-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Empty neighbour retreat reserves the province and settles surviving troops after restored defeat',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena(),p=neutral(g,b);g.battleAction('flee',{unit:f.id,province:p.id});;const saved=validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains),loaded=new Game(saved,{},g.terrains);loaded.battleAction('flee',{unit:u.id,confirmArmy:true});;;;;validateSave(loaded.s,scenarios,g.terrains);}) | assert(!retreatOptions(g,v).some(q=&gt;q.id===p.id))<br>assert.equal(loaded.province(p.id).owner,b.attacker)<br>assert(loaded.province(p.id).officers.includes(f.id))<br>assert.equal(loaded.officer(f.id).soldiers,5000)<br>assert.equal(loaded.province(p.id).governor,f.id) |
| 3. [Defenders exclude the attacker source and can settle an empty connected province after conquest](../tests/expansion-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Defenders exclude the attacker source and can settle an empty connected province after conquest',()=&gt;{const {g,b,v,king}=arena(),p=neutral(g,b);b.side='defend';;;;g.battleAction('flee',{unit:v.id,province:p.id});g.battleAction('flee',{unit:king.id,confirmArmy:true});;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains);}) | assert(!retreatOptions(g,v).some(q=&gt;q.id===b.source))<br>assert(retreatOptions(g,v).some(q=&gt;q.id===p.id))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.battleAction('flee',{unit:v.id,province:b.source}))<br>assert.equal(g.province(p.id).owner,b.defender)<br>assert(g.province(p.id).officers.includes(v.id))<br>assert.equal(g.province(b.target).owner,b.attacker) |

[Forged retreat reservations and destinations cannot be restored](../tests/expansion-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Forged retreat reservations and destinations cannot be restored',()=&gt;{const {g,b,f}=arena();f.fled=true;f.retreatProvince=b.target;;f.retreatProvince=b.source;f.fled=false;;}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains)) |
| 5. [Four weather states preserve native dry-weather parity and rain blocks fire](../tests/expansion-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Four weather states preserve native dry-weather parity and rain blocks fire',()=&gt;{;;;;for(const weather of Object.keys(WEATHER)){const {g,b,u}=arena();g.random=()=&gt;.65;setWeather(b,weather);if(weather==='rain'){b.fire={3:2};setWeather(b,weather);;const before=JSON.stringify(b);;;}else{g.battleAction('fireball',{unit:u.id,q:6,r:4});;}}}) | assert.deepEqual(Object.values(WEATHER),['Clear','Few clouds','Cloudy','Rain'])<br>assert.equal(fireChance(80,'sunny'),fireChance(80,'fewclouds'))<br>assert.equal(fireChance(80,'fewclouds'),fireChance(80,'cloudy'))<br>assert.equal(fireChance(100,'rain'),0)<br>assert.deepEqual(b.fire,{})<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.battleAction('fireball',{unit:u.id,q:6,r:4}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(b),before)<br>assert.equal(!!b.fire[4*13+6],true) |

[Old stormy battlefield saves migrate to cloudy while retaining wind and units](../tests/expansion-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Old stormy battlefield saves migrate to cloudy while retaining wind and units',()=&gt;{const {g,b}=arena();b.rulesVersion=4;b.weather='stormy';b.wind=6;b.windStrength=3;const s=validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains);;;;;;}) | assert.equal(s.battle.rulesVersion,6)<br>assert.equal(s.battle.weather,'cloudy')<br>assert.equal(s.battle.wind,6)<br>assert.equal(s.battle.windStrength,1)<br>assert.deepEqual(s.battle.units,b.units) |

| 10. [Hua Tuo book changes three-month sickness and injury to one month and persists](../tests/expansion-tests.mjs#L19-L19) | test('Hua Tuo book changes three-month sickness and injury to one month and persists',()=&gt;{for(const key of ['sick','injured']){const ordinary=fresh(),medical=fresh();for(const g of [ordinary,medical]){g.officer(34)[key]=true;g.officer(34).illMonths=3;}const r=report();;;;const saved=validateSave(medical.s,scenarios,terrains);;for(let month=2;month&lt;=4;month++){for(const g of [ordinary,medical]){g.s.month=month;runMonthlyEvents(g,report());};;}}}) | assert(triggerEvent(medical,'medical',r,{officer:34}))<br>assert.equal(medical.officer(34).illMonths,1)<br>assert.equal(r.details[0].art,'medical')<br>assert(saved.officers[34].medicalBook)<br>assert.equal(!!medical.officer(34)[key],false)<br>assert.equal(!!ordinary.officer(34)[key],month&lt;4) |

<a id="tests-fidelity-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/fidelity-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/fidelity-tests.mjs](../tests/fidelity-tests.mjs). Purpose: All 412 future records have canonical identities and valid province/year fields; Every future identity appears once in every scenario and survives save/load without overwriting native officers; Future arrivals respect their dates and never resurrect a deceased identity; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/future-officers.mjs`, `../mjs/campaign-fidelity.mjs`, `../mjs/monthly-events.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/geography.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. ## tests/monthly-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/monthly-tests.mjs](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs). Purpose: New campaigns begin with a dated historical council and a restorable review; Every month resolves its council before the new factions begin; Applying the same monthly events twice does not charge or damage anything twice; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/monthly-events.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [New campaigns begin with a dated historical council and a restorable review](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('New campaigns begin with a dated historical council and a restorable review',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();;;const loaded=validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);;;}) | assert.equal(g.s.lastReport.details[0].kind,'history')<br>assert.equal(g.s.monthlyReview.month,1)<br>assert.deepEqual(loaded.monthlyReview,g.s.monthlyReview)<br>assert.deepEqual(loaded.lastReport,g.s.lastReport) |
| 2. [Every month resolves its council before the new factions begin](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Every month resolves its council before the new factions begin',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.random=()=&gt;.99;for(let month=2;month&lt;=12;month++){g.s.completed=g.s.rulers.map(r=&gt;r.id).filter(id=&gt;id!==g.s.player);const r=g.finishFactionTurn();;;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}}) | assert(r)<br>assert.equal(r.month,month)<br>assert.equal(g.s.monthlyReview.month,month)<br>assert(r.details.length) |
| 3. [Applying the same monthly events twice does not charge or damage anything twice](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Applying the same monthly events twice does not charge or damage anything twice',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.s.month=5;g.random=()=&gt;0;runMonthlyEvents(g,report());const snapshot=JSON.stringify(g.s);runMonthlyEvents(g,report());;}) | assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),snapshot) |
| 4. [Historical cards occur once and preserve the campaign ownership](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Historical cards occur once and preserve the campaign ownership',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.s.year=192;g.s.month=1;g.random=()=&gt;.99;const owners=g.s.provinces.map(p=&gt;p.owner),r=report();runMonthlyEvents(g,r);;;const next=report();g.s.month=2;runMonthlyEvents(g,next);;}) | assert(r.details.some(e=&gt;e.title.includes('192')))<br>assert.deepEqual(g.s.provinces.map(p=&gt;p.owner),owners)<br>assert(!next.details.some(e=&gt;e.kind==='history')) |
| 5. [Hidden adults remain searchable; young historical generals come of age in January](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Hidden adults remain searchable; young historical generals come of age in January',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.s.provinces.find(p=&gt;p.hidden.length),id=p.hidden[0],adult=p.hidden[1];g.officer(id).birth=175;g.s.year=190;g.s.month=1;g.random=()=&gt;.99;const r=report();runMonthlyEvents(g,r);;;if(adult!==undefined);;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert(p.unclaimed.includes(id))<br>assert(!p.hidden.includes(id))<br>assert(p.hidden.includes(adult))<br>assert(r.details.some(e=&gt;e.officer===id)) |
| 6. [Old generals can die and rulers pass to a valid saved successor](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Old generals can die and rulers pass to a valid saved successor',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),r=g.ruler(),old=g.officer(r.leader);old.birth=100;g.s.year=190;g.s.month=1;g.random=()=&gt;0;runMonthlyEvents(g,report());;;g.normalize();validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);;}) | assert(old.dead)<br>assert.notEqual(r.leader,old.id)<br>assert(g.s.provinces.some(p=&gt;p.owner===r.id&amp;&amp;p.officers.includes(r.leader))) |
| 7. [Flood losses are mitigated by dikes and remain in range](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs#L16-L16) | test('Flood losses are mitigated by dikes and remain in range',()=&gt;{const a=fresh(),z=fresh(),p=a.province(9),q=z.province(9);p.flood=0;q.flood=100;const old=p.food,protectedRice=q.food;a.random=z.random=()=&gt;0;disaster(a,p,'flood',report());disaster(z,q,'flood',report());;;;a.normalize();validateSave(a.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert.equal(p.food,old)<br>assert.equal(q.food,protectedRice)<br>assert(p.population&lt;q.population) |
| 8. [Locusts, typhoons, epidemics and uprisings affect the specified campaign attributes](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs#L17-L17) | test('Locusts, typhoons, epidemics and uprisings affect the specified campaign attributes',()=&gt;{for(const type of ['locust','typhoon','epidemic','uprising']){const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),before=structuredClone(p),soldiers=g.officer(0).soldiers,r=report();g.random=()=&gt;0;disaster(g,p,type,r);;if(type==='locust'\|\|type==='uprising');if(type==='epidemic'\|\|type==='uprising');if(type==='typhoon');g.normalize();validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}}) | assert(r.details[0].title.includes(p.name))<br>assert(p.food&lt;before.food)<br>assert(g.officer(0).soldiers&lt;soldiers)<br>assert(p.land&lt;before.land) |
| 9. [Seasonal swarms spread only to fixed neighbouring provinces and disappear by winter](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs#L18-L18) | test('Seasonal swarms spread only to fixed neighbouring provinces and disappear by winter',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.random=()=&gt;0;disaster(g,g.province(9),'locust',report());g.s.month=4;const r=report();runMonthlyEvents(g,r);for(const id of g.province(9).neighbors);g.s.month=10;runMonthlyEvents(g,report());;}) | assert(r.details.some(e=&gt;e.province===id&amp;&amp;e.title.includes('Locust')))<br>assert(!g.s.monthlyState.disasters.some(d=&gt;d.type==='locust')) |
| 10. [Imprisoned officers remain in their province while free generals may wander](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs#L19-L19) | test('Imprisoned officers remain in their province while free generals may wander',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.s.provinces.find(p=&gt;p.hidden.length),id=p.hidden.shift();p.unclaimed.push(id);g.officer(id).prisonerOf=0;g.random=()=&gt;0;g.s.month=2;runMonthlyEvents(g,report());;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert(p.unclaimed.includes(id)) |
| 11. [Rebellion updates officer lists and governor references with a valid save](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs#L20-L20) | test('Rebellion updates officer lists and governor references with a valid save',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(10),ids=[...p.officers],r=report();disaster(g,p,'rebellion',r);g.normalize();;;;;;g.s.player=p.owner;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert.notEqual(p.owner,0)<br>assert.notEqual(p.owner,255)<br>assert.equal(g.ruler(p.owner).leader,p.governor)<br>assert(ids.every(id=&gt;p.officers.includes(id)\|\|p.unclaimed.includes(id)))<br>assert.equal(g.officer(p.governor).owner,p.owner) |
| 12. [Invalid council checkpoints and active-disaster records are rejected](../tests/monthly-tests.mjs#L21-L21) | test('Invalid council checkpoints and active-disaster records are rejected',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),a=structuredClone(g.s);a.monthlyReview.index=900;;const b=structuredClone(g.s);b.monthlyState.disasters=[{province:42,type:'locust',born:0,lastSpread:0}];;}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(a,scenarios,terrains))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(b,scenarios,terrains)) |

<a id="tests-province-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/province-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/province-tests.mjs](../tests/province-tests.mjs). Purpose: Hire uses hundreds, original population reserve and shared capacity; resources and assignments are conserved; Reassignment rejects unconfirmed remaining men and overspending, then returns confirmed disbands to population; Train improves every local army and consumes only the chosen instructor action; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `../mjs/ruler-family.mjs`, `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/province-rules.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Hire uses hundreds, original population reserve and shared capacity; resources and assignments are conserved](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Hire uses hundreds, original population reserve and shared capacity; resources and assignments are conserved',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),cap=hireCapacity(g,p),gold=p.gold,food=p.food,pop=p.population,alloc=assignments(g,p);;alloc[34]+=100;g.execute('hireArmy',{province:9,officer:33,hundreds:1,allocations:alloc});;;;;;valid(g);const h=fresh();h.province(9).population=50000;;atomic(h,()=&gt;h.execute('hireArmy',{province:9,officer:33,hundreds:1,allocations:assignments(h,h.province(9))}));}) | assert(cap&gt;=1)<br>assert.equal(p.gold,gold-10)<br>assert.equal(p.food,food-100)<br>assert.equal(p.population,pop-100)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(34).soldiers,2100)<br>assert(g.officer(33).acted)<br>assert.equal(hireCapacity(h,h.province(9)),0) |
| 2. [Reassignment rejects unconfirmed remaining men and overspending, then returns confirmed disbands to population](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Reassignment rejects unconfirmed remaining men and overspending, then returns confirmed disbands to population',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),pop=p.population,alloc=assignments(g,p);alloc[34]-=100;atomic(g,()=&gt;g.execute('reassignArmy',{province:9,officer:33,allocations:alloc}));const p2=g.province(9);g.execute('reassignArmy',{province:9,officer:33,allocations:alloc,disband:true});;;valid(g);const h=fresh(),a=assignments(h,h.province(9));a[34]=10001;atomic(h,()=&gt;h.execute('reassignArmy',{province:9,officer:33,allocations:a,disband:true}));}) | assert.equal(p2.population,pop+100)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(34).soldiers,1900) |
| 3. [Train improves every local army and consumes only the chosen instructor action](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Train improves every local army and consumes only the chosen instructor action',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),before=p.officers.map(id=&gt;g.officer(id).training);g.execute('trainArmy',{province:9,officer:34});p.officers.forEach((id,i)=&gt;);;;valid(g);}) | assert(g.officer(id).training&gt;before[i])<br>assert(g.officer(34).acted)<br>assert(!g.officer(33).acted) |
| 4. [Reward respects a ready governor; horse and writings alter stock, loyalty and INT without gold for horse or writings](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Reward respects a ready governor; horse and writings alter stock, loyalty and INT without gold for horse or writings',()=&gt;{for(const kind of ['rewardGold','rewardHorse','rewardWritings']){const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);p.gold=1000;p.horses=2;g.ruler().advisor=33;g.officer(33).int=95;g.s.monthlyAdvice.intelligence[33]=100;g.officer(34).int=60;g.officer(34).loyalty=70;g.execute(kind,{province:9,officer:34,amount:50});;;if(kind==='rewardHorse'){;;}if(kind==='rewardWritings');else ;valid(g);g.officer(0).acted=true;atomic(g,()=&gt;g.execute(kind,{province:9,officer:34,amount:1}));}}) | assert.equal(p.gold,kind==='rewardGold'?950:1000)<br>assert(g.officer(p.governor).acted)<br>assert.equal(p.horses,1)<br>assert(!g.officer(34).hasHorse)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(34).int,61)<br>assert(g.officer(34).loyalty&gt;70) |
| 5. [Advice requires a local advisor, caches one intelligence roll, and rumours/healing require travelling visitors](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L16-L16) | test('Advice requires a local advisor, caches one intelligence roll, and rumours/healing require travelling visitors',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);g.ruler().advisor=33;g.officer(33).int=100;g.s.monthlyAdvice.intelligence[33]=100;p.food=1;;const seed=g.s.seed;;;g.s.visitors={month:g.s.year*12+g.s.month-1,huaTuo:9,scholar:9,critic:1};;g.officer(34).sick=true;g.officer(34).illMonths=3;g.execute('healing',{province:9});;;valid(g);g.s.month++;g.random=()=&gt;.99;covertUpkeep(g);;}) | assert(provinceAdvice(g,p).includes('only 1 food'))<br>assert.equal(provinceAdvice(g,p),provinceAdvice(g,p))<br>assert.equal(g.s.seed,seed)<br>assert(provinceRumours(g,p).startsWith('Sima Hui:'))<br>assert(!g.officer(34).sick)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(34).illMonths,0)<br>assert.equal(updateVisitors(g).month,g.s.year*12+g.s.month-1) |
| 6. [Alliance, gift, marriage and cancellation change reciprocal diplomatic state and preserve save invariants](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L17-L17) | test('Alliance, gift, marriage and cancellation change reciprocal diplomatic state and preserve save invariants',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),r=g.ruler(3);g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'alliance',target:3,officer:33});;;const gold=p.gold;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'gift',target:3,officer:34,amount:100});;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'marriage',target:3,officer:35});;;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'cancel',target:3});;;valid(g);}) | assert(g.ruler().alliances.includes(3))<br>assert(r.alliances.includes(0))<br>assert.equal(p.gold,gold-100)<br>assert.equal(g.ruler().daughterGivenTo,3)<br>assert(r.daughtersReceived.includes(0))<br>assert(!g.ruler().alliances.includes(3))<br>assert(!r.alliances.includes(0)) |
| 7. [Refused missions consume the envoy action, but invalid targets and gifts are atomic](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L18-L18) | test('Refused missions consume the envoy action, but invalid targets and gifts are atomic',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();atomic(g,()=&gt;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'gift',target:3,officer:34,amount:99}));g.random=()=&gt;.99;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'alliance',target:3,officer:34});;;valid(g);}) | assert(g.officer(34).acted)<br>assert(!g.ruler().alliances.includes(3)) |
| 8. [Joint invasion requires an ally, brings their formations from their own approach and returns survivors to their realm](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L19-L19) | test('Joint invasion requires an ally, brings their formations from their own approach and returns survivors to their realm',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();atomic(g,()=&gt;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'joint',target:10,enemy:8,officer:33}));g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'alliance',target:10,officer:33});g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'joint',target:10,enemy:8,officer:34});g.invade({province:9,target:8,governor:33,officers:[0,35],food:10000});const b=g.s.battle,allies=b.units.filter(u=&gt;u.allyOwner===10);;;;valid(g);while(b.phase==='deployment')aiBattle(g);g.battleAction('end');g.battleAction('flee',{unit:b.leaders.attack,confirmArmy:true});for(const u of  … (complete input/procedure at linked L19–19) | assert.equal(allies.length,2)<br>assert(allies.every(u=&gt;u.placed&amp;&amp;u.origin===16&amp;&amp;unitOwner(b,u)===10))<br>assert.equal(g.s.jointPlans.length,0)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(u.id).owner,10)<br>assert(g.province(16).officers.includes(u.id)) |
| 9. [An impossible invasion restores allied provisions, actions, plans and RNG atomically](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L20-L20) | test('An impossible invasion restores allied provisions, actions, plans and RNG atomically',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.ruler().alliances.push(10);g.ruler(10).alliances.push(0);g.s.jointPlans=[{ruler:0,ally:10,enemy:8,expires:g.s.year*12+g.s.month}];g.terrains[7]=Array(156).fill(3);g.terrains[7][80]=6;atomic(g,()=&gt;g.invade({province:9,target:8,governor:33,officers:[0,34],food:10000}));valid(g);}) | This checkpoint logs completion; assertions may be shared with preceding setup. Use the full linked block when judging independent coverage. |
| 10. [Betrayal and forged letters lower subordinate loyalty; a saved valid pact switches sides during invasion](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L21-L21) | test('Betrayal and forged letters lower subordinate loyalty; a saved valid pact switches sides during invasion',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),target=g.officer(115),loy=target.loyalty;g.execute('spyMission',{province:9,mode:'betrayal',officer:33,target:115});;;g.execute('spyMission',{province:9,mode:'forged',officer:34,target:125});valid(g);g.invade({province:9,target:8,governor:33,officers:[0,35],food:10000});;;valid(g);}) | assert.equal(target.betrayalFor,0)<br>assert(target.loyalty&lt;loy)<br>assert(g.s.battle.units.find(u=&gt;u.id===115).betrayed)<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle.units.find(u=&gt;u.id===115).side,'attack') |
| 11. [Expired betrayal and joint plans cannot affect later wars](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L22-L22) | test('Expired betrayal and joint plans cannot affect later wars',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),month=g.s.year*12+g.s.month-1;g.officer(115).betrayalFor=0;g.officer(115).betrayalUntil=month-1;g.s.jointPlans=[{ruler:0,ally:10,enemy:8,expires:month-1}];covertUpkeep(g);;;valid(g);}) | assert.equal(g.officer(115).betrayalFor,undefined)<br>assert.equal(g.s.jointPlans.length,0) |
| 12. [Infiltration moves a fully loyal subordinate as a free general, then undermines the enemy once recruited](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L23-L23) | test('Infiltration moves a fully loyal subordinate as a free general, then undermines the enemy once recruited',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),o=g.officer(34);o.loyalty=100;g.execute('spyMission',{province:9,mode:'infiltrate',officer:34,target:8});;;;g.s.player=12;g.execute('recruit',{province:8,officer:12,target:34});;const before=g.officer(115).loyalty;covertUpkeep(g);;valid(g);}) | assert.equal(o.owner,255)<br>assert(g.province(8).unclaimed.includes(34))<br>assert(!g.province(9).officers.includes(34))<br>assert.equal(o.owner,12)<br>assert(g.officer(115).loyalty&lt;before) |
| 13. [Rival tigers uses two messengers and creates hostility between rivals](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L24-L24) | test('Rival tigers uses two messengers and creates hostility between rivals',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),before=g.ruler(3).relations[10]??50;g.execute('spyMission',{province:9,mode:'rival',target:3,other:10,officer:33,second:34});;;valid(g);}) | assert.equal(g.ruler(3).relations[10],Math.min(100,before+20))<br>assert(g.officer(33).acted&amp;&amp;g.officer(34).acted) |
| 14. [Tiger and wolf creates an independent realm for a rival governor](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L25-L25) | test('Tiger and wolf creates an independent realm for a rival governor',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(16);p.governor=87;g.officer(87).loyalty=1;g.execute('spyMission',{province:9,mode:'wolf',officer:33,target:87});;;;valid(g);}) | assert.notEqual(p.owner,10)<br>assert.notEqual(p.owner,255)<br>assert.equal(g.ruler(p.owner).leader,87) |
| 15. [Only the ready ruler can delegate another own province; direct and self rule persist](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L26-L26) | test('Only the ready ruler can delegate another own province; direct and self rule persist',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.execute('move',{province:9,target:17,officer:34,gold:0,food:0});const p=g.province(17);g.execute('delegateRealm',{province:9,target:17,mode:'self'});;valid(g);atomic(g,()=&gt;g.execute('delegateRealm',{province:9,target:17,mode:'direct'}));g.officer(0).acted=false;g.execute('delegateRealm',{province:9,target:17,mode:'direct'});;valid(g);}) | assert.equal(p.delegate,'domestic')<br>assert.equal(g.province(17).delegate,'manual') |
| 16. [Dismiss conceals a subordinate nearby; self-exile requires both choices and preserves campaign control](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L27-L27) | test('Dismiss conceals a subordinate nearby; self-exile requires both choices and preserves campaign control',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.execute('dismiss',{province:9,officer:34});;;valid(g);atomic(g,()=&gt;g.execute('selfExile',{province:9}));atomic(g,()=&gt;g.execute('selfExile',{province:9,confirm:true}));g.execute('selfExile',{province:9,confirm:true,takeAll:false});;;;;valid(g);atomic(g,()=&gt;g.execute('search',{province:9,officer:33}));}) | assert.equal(g.officer(34).owner,255)<br>assert(g.province(9).neighbors.some(id=&gt;g.province(id).hidden.includes(34)))<br>assert(!g.s.campaignEnded)<br>assert(g.s.humanRulers.includes(0))<br>assert(g.roaming())<br>assert(!g.s.provinces.some(p=&gt;p.owner===0)) |

[Forged covert, visitor and marriage save fields are rejected](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L28-L28) | test('Forged covert, visitor and marriage save fields are rejected',()=&gt;{for(const corrupt of [g=&gt;g.officer(115).spyFor=99,g=&gt;g.s.visitors.huaTuo=42,g=&gt;g.ruler().marriedTo=3]){const g=fresh();corrupt(g);;}}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;valid(g)) |
| 18. [Accepted threats transfer a rival realm, while source service years advance and new recruitment starts at one](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L29-L29) | test('Accepted threats transfer a rival realm, while source service years advance and new recruitment starts at one',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),since=g.officer(34).serviceSince;;g.s.year++;;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'threat',target:12,officer:33});;;;valid(g);}) | assert(Number.isInteger(since))<br>assert.equal(g.s.year-since+1,scenarios[0].year-since+2)<br>assert.equal(g.province(8).owner,0)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(12).owner,0)<br>assert(!g.s.provinces.some(p=&gt;p.owner===12)) |
| 19. [Actual faction rollover expires covert pacts and refreshes travelling visitors once per month](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L30-L30) | test('Actual faction rollover expires covert pacts and refreshes travelling visitors once per month',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),month=g.s.year*12+g.s.month-1;g.officer(115).betrayalFor=0;g.officer(115).betrayalUntil=month;g.s.jointPlans=[{ruler:0,ally:10,enemy:8,expires:month}];let safety=0;while(g.s.month===1){g.finishFactionTurn();;};;;valid(g);}) | assert(++safety&lt;=20)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(115).betrayalFor,undefined)<br>assert.equal(g.s.jointPlans.length,0)<br>assert.equal(g.s.visitors.month,month+1) |
| 20. [Joint invasion rejects allies of either partner before consuming an action or provisions](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L31-L31) | test('Joint invasion rejects allies of either partner before consuming an action or provisions',()=&gt;{for(const partner of [0,10]){const g=fresh();g.ruler().alliances.push(10);g.ruler(10).alliances.push(0);g.ruler(partner).alliances.push(12);g.ruler(12).alliances.push(partner);atomic(g,()=&gt;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'joint',target:10,enemy:8,officer:33}));;;valid(g);}}) | assert(!g.officer(33).acted)<br>assert.equal(g.s.jointPlans.length,0) |
| 21. [A new alliance cancels pending joint plans against that realm without charging the invasion](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L32-L32) | test('A new alliance cancels pending joint plans against that realm without charging the invasion',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'alliance',target:10,officer:33});g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'joint',target:10,enemy:8,officer:34});;g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'alliance',target:12,officer:35});;valid(g);}) | assert.equal(g.s.jointPlans.length,1)<br>assert.equal(g.s.jointPlans.length,0) |
| 22. [Self-exile remains playable through rest, saved movement and settlement](../tests/province-tests.mjs#L33-L33) | test('Self-exile remains playable through rest, saved movement and settlement',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.execute('selfExile',{province:9,confirm:true,takeAll:true});const party=g.roaming();;valid(g);const target=g.province(9).neighbors.find(id=&gt;g.province(id).owner===255);if(target){g.roam('move',target);g.roam('settle');;}else g.roam('rest');valid(g);}) | assert(party.officers.includes(g.ruler().leader))<br>assert.equal(g.province(target).owner,0) |
