## tests/strategy-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/strategy-tests.mjs](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs). Purpose: Launch supports zero, twelve distinct humans and scenario-specific limits; All six legacy scenarios migrate without losing domestic progress; Hotseat visits every faction once before monthly upkeep; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/engine.mjs`, `../mjs/portraits.mjs`, `../mjs/events.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Launch supports zero, twelve distinct humans and scenario-specific limits](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L3-L3) | test('Launch supports zero, twelve distinct humans and scenario-specific limits',()=&gt;{for(const s of scenarios){const ids=activeRulers(s).map(r=&gt;r.id).slice(0,12);;validateSave(createCampaign(s,ids),scenarios,terrains);};;;}) | assert.equal(createCampaign(s,[]).humanRulers.length,0)<br>assert.equal(createCampaign(scenarios[0],activeRulers(scenarios[0]).slice(0,12).map(r=&gt;r.id)).humanRulers.length,12)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;createCampaign(scenarios[5],[0,0]))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;createCampaign(scenarios[5],[14])) |
| 2. [All six legacy scenarios migrate without losing domestic progress](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L4-L4) | test('All six legacy scenarios migrate without losing domestic progress',()=&gt;{for(const s of scenarios){const old=oldCreate(s,activeRulers(s)[0].id);old.provinces[8].gold=1234;const migrated=validateSave(old,scenarios,terrains);;;;;}}) | assert.equal(migrated.version,2)<br>assert.deepEqual(migrated.humanRulers,[old.player])<br>assert.equal(migrated.provinces[8].gold,1234)<br>assert(migrated.provinces.every(p=&gt;p.geo.length===2&amp;&amp;!/ \d+$/.test(p.name))) |
| 3. [Hotseat visits every faction once before monthly upkeep](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L5-L5) | test('Hotseat visits every faction once before monthly upkeep',()=&gt;{const g=fresh([0,1]),count=activeRulers(g.s).length,month=g.s.month;;const seen=[];for(let i=0;i&lt;count;i++){;seen.push(g.s.player);g.finishFactionTurn();if(i&lt;count-1);};;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert(!g.aiStep())<br>assert(!seen.includes(g.s.player))<br>assert.equal(g.s.month,month)<br>assert.equal(g.s.month,month+1)<br>assert.deepEqual(g.s.completed,[]) |
| 4. [Intelligence changes actual officer choices and aggression can suppress war](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L6-L6) | test('Intelligence changes actual officer choices and aggression can suppress war',()=&gt;{const high=fresh([]),low=fresh([]);for(const g of [high,low]){g.s.seed=123;g.s.ai.aggression=0;}high.s.ai.intelligence=100;low.s.ai.intelligence=0;high.aiStep();low.aiStep();const a=high.s.officers.find(o=&gt;o.acted),b=low.s.officers.find(o=&gt;o.acted);;;;}) | assert(a&amp;&amp;b)<br>assert.notEqual(a.id,b.id)<br>assert(!high.s.battle&amp;&amp;!low.s.battle) |
| 5. [Invasions validate before costs, reserve commanders and restore battle saves](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L7-L7) | test('Invasions validate before costs, reserve commanders and restore battle saves',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;begin(g);;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);const h=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains),{},terrains);;const corrupt=structuredClone(g.s);corrupt.battle.units[0].q=99;;}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.invade({province:9,target:4,officers:[0],food:20000}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert(g.s.battle)<br>assert.equal(g.province(9).food,20000)<br>assert(g.officer(0).acted)<br>assert.deepEqual(h.s.battle,g.s.battle)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(corrupt,scenarios,terrains)) |
| 6. [Hex movement respects mobility, terrain, occupancy and atomic rejection](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L8-L8) | test('Hex movement respects mobility, terrain, occupancy and atomic rejection',()=&gt;{const g=begin(),b=g.s.battle,u=b.units[0];u.mobility=6;const moves=reachable(b,u);;for(const m of moves){;;}const bad=JSON.stringify(b);;;g.battleAction('move',{unit:u.id,...moves[0]});;;;}) | assert(moves.length)<br>assert(m.cost&lt;=6)<br>assert(!at(b,m.q,m.r))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.battleAction('move',{unit:u.id,q:99,r:99}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(b),bad)<br>assert.equal(u.mobility,6-moves[0].cost)<br>assert(u.ordered)<br>assert.equal(neighbors(6,6).length,6) |
| 7. [Wait banks mobility and rain prevents fireballs without using the daily order](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Wait banks mobility and rain prevents fireballs without using the daily order',()=&gt;{const g=begin(),b=g.s.battle,u=b.units[0];g.battleAction('wait',{unit:u.id});;;u.ordered=false;b.weather='rain';const before=JSON.stringify(b);const dry=neighbors(u.q,u.r).find(p=&gt;![3,4].includes(b.terrain[p.r*13+p.q]));;;}) | assert.equal(u.mobility,3)<br>assert(u.ordered)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.battleAction('fireball',{unit:u.id,...dry}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(b),before) |
| 8. [Retreat and conquest settle armies and ownership with valid governors](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Retreat and conquest settle armies and ownership with valid governors',()=&gt;{const retreat=begin(),rice=retreat.province(9).food;retreat.battleAction('retreat');;;validateSave(retreat.s,scenarios,terrains);const g=begin(),target=g.s.battle.target;g.s.battle.side='defend';g.battleAction('retreat');;;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert(!retreat.s.battle)<br>assert.equal(retreat.province(9).food,rice)<br>assert.equal(g.province(target).owner,0)<br>assert(g.province(target).officers.includes(0))<br>assert(!g.province(9).officers.includes(0))<br>assert.equal(g.ruler(0).home,target) |
| 9. [Cheat edits are bounded, atomic, affect AI and preserve ownership](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Cheat edits are bounded, atomic, affect AI and preserve ownership',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.cheat('ai','global',{intelligence:100,aggression:0});;g.cheat('ai',1,{intelligence:10});;g.cheat('officer',0,{war:100,acted:true});g.cheat('province',9,{gold:30000,food:900000,resetActions:false});;g.cheat('province',9,{resetActions:true});;const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;g.cheat('province',9,{owner:1});;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert.deepEqual(g.aiFor(),{intelligence:100,aggression:0})<br>assert.equal(g.aiFor(1).intelligence,10)<br>assert(g.officer(0).acted)<br>assert(!g.officer(0).acted)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.cheat('province',9,{gold:100,land:999}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert(g.province(9).officers.every(id=&gt;g.officer(id).owner===1)) |
| 10. [Battle HUD resolves routing and blocks conflicting campaign edits](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Battle HUD resolves routing and blocks conflicting campaign edits',()=&gt;{const g=begin(),b=g.s.battle,before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;g.cheat('battle',b.units[0].id,{morale:80,mobility:5,weather:'rain',attackFood:1000});;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.cheat('officer',0,{soldiers:1}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle.weather,'rain') |
| 11. [AI attacking a human pauses for the defending human army](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('AI attacking a human pauses for the defending human army',()=&gt;{const g=begin();g.s.settings.battleView='all';g.s.humanRulers=[g.s.battle.defender];g.s.battle.side='defend';;const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;}) | assert(g.battleHuman())<br>assert.equal(g.aiStep(),false)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before) |
| 12. [All scenario AI campaigns survive 6,000 actions each with valid checkpoints](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('All scenario AI campaigns survive 6,000 actions each with valid checkpoints',()=&gt;{let wars=0,months=0;for(const s of scenarios){const g=new Game(createCampaign(s,[]),{},terrains);g.s.seed=42;g.s.settings.battleView='all';g.s.ai.aggression=80;for(let i=0;i&lt;6000;i++){if(g.s.battle)wars++;g.aiStep();if(i%1000===0)validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);;months+=g.s.turn;};console.log('Simulation:',wars,'battle actions;',months,'combined campaign turns');}) | assert(g.s.turn&gt;1)<br>assert(wars&gt;0) |
| 13. [Named portraits and reserved frames cover all officer slots](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Named portraits and reserved frames cover all officer slots',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();;;for(const o of g.s.officers);;}) | assert.equal(portraitIndex(g.officer(0)),0)<br>assert(portraitAssigned(g.officer(0)))<br>assert(portraitIndex(o)&gt;=0&amp;&amp;portraitIndex(o)&lt;=352)<br>assert.equal(portraitIndex({name:'Unassigned officer'}),352) |
| 14. [Audio settings and real events survive save/load; previews remain transient](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L16-L16) | test('Audio settings and real events survive save/load; previews remain transient',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.s.settings.sound=false;g.s.settings.music=true;g.execute('develop',{province:9,officer:0,amount:100});;;g.s.eventPreview={kind:'prison',title:'Preview',text:'No campaign change'};;const loaded=validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);;;;;const corrupt=structuredClone(loaded);corrupt.lastEvent.kind='unrecognized';;}) | assert(g.s.lastEvent.text.includes('cultivated'))<br>assert.equal(eventCell(eventFor(g).kind),3)<br>assert(eventFor(g).preview)<br>assert.equal(loaded.settings.sound,false)<br>assert.equal(loaded.settings.music,true)<br>assert(!loaded.eventPreview)<br>assert(loaded.lastEvent.text.includes('cultivated'))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(corrupt,scenarios,terrains)) |
| 15. [Battle action records supply a raster animation cue without changing rule outcomes](../tests/strategy-tests.mjs#L17-L17) | test('Battle action records supply a raster animation cue without changing rule outcomes',()=&gt;{const g=begin(),u=g.s.battle.units[0];u.mobility=6;const m=reachable(g.s.battle,u)[0];g.battleAction('move',{unit:u.id,...m});;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert.equal(g.s.battle.lastAction.type,'move')<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle.lastAction.unit,u.id)<br>assert(g.s.battle.lastAction.sequence&gt;1) |

| 1. [Every playable ruler in all six scenarios can save and load](../tests/tests.mjs#L4-L4) | test('Every playable ruler in all six scenarios can save and load',()=&gt;{for(const s of scenarios)for(const r of s.rulers){if(s.provinces.some(p=&gt;p.owner===r.id))validateSave(createCampaign(s,r.id),scenarios);}}) | This checkpoint logs completion; assertions may be shared with preceding setup. Use the full linked block when judging independent coverage. |

| 3. [Source cultivation and dike formulas, resource costs, and action limits](../tests/tests.mjs#L6-L6) | test('Source cultivation and dike formulas, resource costs, and action limits',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),o=g.officer(p.governor),old=p.land,expected=g.preview('develop',9,o.id,100),gold=p.gold;g.execute('develop',{province:9,officer:o.id,amount:100});;;const snap=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;}) | assert.equal(p.land,old+expected)<br>assert.equal(p.gold,gold-100)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('flood',{province:9,officer:o.id,amount:100}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),snap) |
| 4. [Invalid orders and insufficient resources are atomic](../tests/tests.mjs#L7-L7) | test('Invalid orders and insufficient resources are atomic',()=&gt;{for(const args of [{amount:100.5},{amount:-10},{amount:101},{amount:'NaN'}]){const g=fresh(),before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;}const g=fresh();g.province(9).gold=0;const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('develop',{province:9,officer:0,...args}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('reward',{province:9,officer:33,amount:10}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before) |
| 5. [Relief formula increases loyalty and consumes rice](../tests/tests.mjs#L8-L8) | test('Relief formula increases loyalty and consumes rice',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);p.loyalty=50;const gain=g.preview('relief',9,0,3000),food=p.food;g.execute('relief',{province:9,officer:0,amount:3000});;;}) | assert.equal(p.loyalty,50+gain)<br>assert.equal(p.food,food-3000) |
| 6. [Rice trade does not create free rice or exceed storage](../tests/tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Rice trade does not create free rice or exceed storage',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);p.merchant=true;const before=p.gold,food=p.food;g.execute('trade',{province:9,mode:'buy',amount:1});;;p.gold=30000;;}) | assert.equal(p.gold,before-1)<br>assert.equal(p.food,food+1)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('trade',{province:9,mode:'sell',amount:100})) |
| 7. [Rewards charge resources and teaching cannot exceed adviser intelligence](../tests/tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Rewards charge resources and teaching cannot exceed adviser intelligence',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),o=g.officer(33);o.loyalty=50;const gold=p.gold;g.execute('reward',{province:9,officer:33,amount:50});;;g.officer(p.governor).acted=false;g.execute('advisor',{province:9,officer:33});const pupil=g.officer(p.officers[2]);const before=pupil.int;g.execute('book',{province:9,officer:pupil.id});;;}) | assert.equal(p.gold,gold-50)<br>assert(g.officer(p.governor).acted)<br>assert.equal(pupil.int,before+1)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('book',{province:9,officer:pupil.id})) |
| 8. [Peaceful settlement, transport route, and governor consistency](../tests/tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Peaceful settlement, transport route, and governor consistency',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),target=p.neighbors.find(id=&gt;g.province(id).owner===255);;const o=p.officers[2];g.execute('move',{province:9,officer:o,target,gold:100,food:1000});;;;;g.execute('transport',{province:9,officer:p.officers[3],target,gold:50,food:500});;validateSave(g.s,scenarios);}) | assert(target)<br>assert.equal(g.province(target).owner,0)<br>assert.equal(g.province(target).governor,o)<br>assert(!p.officers.includes(o))<br>assert.deepEqual(g.route(9,target),[9,target])<br>assert.equal(g.province(target).gold,scenarios[0].provinces[target-1].gold+150) |
| 9. [Tax applies loyalty and trust penalties and blocks repeat and summer collection](../tests/tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Tax applies loyalty and trust penalties and blocks repeat and summer collection',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),loyalty=p.loyalty,trust=g.ruler().trust;g.execute('tax',{province:9});;;;const h=fresh();h.s.month=7;;}) | assert.equal(p.loyalty,Math.max(0,loyalty-10))<br>assert.equal(g.ruler().trust,trust-5)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('tax',{province:9}))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;h.execute('tax',{province:9})) |
| 10. [Recruitment and diplomacy complete without duplicate officers](../tests/tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Recruitment and diplomacy complete without duplicate officers',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),id=p.hidden[0];if(id!==undefined){p.hidden.shift();p.unclaimed.push(id);g.execute('recruit',{province:9,officer:0,target:id});}g.execute('diplomacy',{province:9,officer:p.officers[2],target:1,amount:100,mode:'gift'});;validateSave(g.s,scenarios);}) | assert.equal(g.ruler(0).relations[1],g.ruler(1).relations[0]) |
| 11. [Garrison recruitment checks capacity, spends resources, and trains](../tests/tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Garrison recruitment checks capacity, spends resources, and trains',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),o=g.officer(p.officers[2]);const before=o.soldiers;p.population=900000;g.execute('enlist',{province:9,officer:o.id,amount:100});;g.endMonth();g.execute('train',{province:9,officer:o.id});;}) | assert.equal(o.soldiers,before+100)<br>assert(o.acted) |
| 12. [Exile preserves governor, adviser and officer-location invariants and rejects ruler dismissal atomically](../tests/tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Exile preserves governor, adviser and officer-location invariants and rejects ruler dismissal atomically',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),o=g.officer(p.officers[1]);const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;p.governor=o.id;g.ruler().advisor=o.id;const pop=p.population,soldiers=o.soldiers;g.execute('exile',{province:p.id,officer:o.id});;;;;;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios);const remaining=p.officers.slice(1);for(const id of remaining){p.officers=p.officers.filter(n=&gt;n!==id);p.unclaimed.push(id);g.officer(id).owner=255;}const last=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('exile',{province:p.id,officer:g.ruler().leader}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert(!p.officers.includes(o.id))<br>assert(p.unclaimed.includes(o.id))<br>assert(p.officers.includes(p.governor))<br>assert.equal(g.ruler().advisor,null)<br>assert.equal(o.owner,255)<br>assert.equal(o.soldiers,0)<br>assert.equal(p.population,Math.min(3000000,pop+soldiers))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('exile',{province:p.id,officer:p.officers[0]}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),last) |
| 13. <a id="tests-ui-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/ui-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/ui-tests.mjs](../tests/ui-tests.mjs). Purpose: live renderer loads corrected Liu Bei portrait and all common event atlases; original launch menu appears before a campaign; six scenarios, era limits, twelve distinct humans and AI choice in order; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `package.json`, `index.html`, `style.css`, `./assets/artwork/fire-v42.webp`, `-v31.webp`, `-v30.webp`, `config/province-terrain.json`, `.json`, `external-campaign.json`, `before-enemy-move.json`, `before-fireball.json`, `before-end-turn.json`, `before-charge.json`, `before-retreat.json`, `slow-governance.json`, `tests/fixtures/v19-hard-second-battle.json`, `hard-second-battle.json`, `config/scenarios.json`, `-audience.json`, `forced-retreat.json`, `combat-v42.json`, `/freesound_community-sword-battle-jingle-loop-96983.mp3`, `/audiomass-charge_scream.mp3`, `/fire.mp3`, `tile-v48.json`, `double-ambush-v48.json`, `display-v47.json`, `conceal-v53.json`, `interception-v53.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `../mjs/raster-world.mjs`, `../mjs/original-deployment.mjs`, `../mjs/geography.mjs`, `node:fs`, `node:path`, `node:assert/strict`, `jsdom`, `@napi-rs/canvas`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |

<a id="tests-v15-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v15-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v15-tests.mjs](../tests/v15-tests.mjs). Purpose: War monthly estimate follows actual daily deductions and adjusts for selected troop strength; Victory persists before captive decisions and blocks orders until acknowledged; Original saves retain their one-slot rules; Expanded seeds only the native gameplay daughter; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`, `/battle-music-v17.mp3`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/ruler-family.mjs`, `../mjs/game-ui.mjs`, `../mjs/war-provisions.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [War monthly estimate follows actual daily deductions and adjusts for selected troop strength](../tests/v15-tests.mjs#L10-L12) | test('War monthly estimate follows actual daily deductions and adjusts for selected troop strength',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh(false);g.s.settings.battleView='all';g.invade({province:9,target:8,governor:33,officers:[0,34],food:30000,gold:500});while(g.s.battle.phase==='deployment')aiBattle(g);const b=g.s.battle,men=living(b).filter(u=&gt;u.side==='attack').reduce((n,u)=&gt;n+u.soldiers,0),before=b.food.attack,e=warProvisions(men,before);g.battleAction('end');g.battleAction('end');;;;; }) | assert.equal(b.food.attack,before-e.daily)<br>assert.equal(e.monthly,e.daily*30)<br>assert.equal(warProvisions(20000,30000).days,45)<br>assert.equal(warProvisions(0,1000).monthly,0) |
| 2. [Victory persists before captive decisions and blocks orders until acknowledged](../tests/v15-tests.mjs#L13-L15) | test('Victory persists before captive decisions and blocks orders until acknowledged',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh(false);g.s.settings.battleView='all';g.invade({province:9,target:8,governor:33,officers:[0,34],food:30000,gold:500});g.s.battle.outcome={winner:'attack',reason:'Enemy army defeated'};g.resolveBattle();;;const saved=valid(g);;;;g.acknowledgeTriumph();;;valid(g); }) | assert(g.s.triumph)<br>assert(g.s.captiveDecisions.length)<br>assert.deepEqual(saved.triumph,g.s.triumph)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('search',{province:8,officer:34}),/victory screen/)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.finishFactionTurn(),/victory screen/)<br>assert.equal(g.s.triumph,null)<br>assert(g.s.captiveDecisions.length) |
| 3. [Original saves retain their one-slot rules; Expanded seeds only the native gameplay daughter](../tests/v15-tests.mjs#L16-L18) | test('Original saves retain their one-slot rules; Expanded seeds only the native gameplay daughter',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh(false);;;g.s.familyMode='expanded';initializeRoyalFamilies(g.s);const f=g.ruler().family;;;;;valid(g); }) | assert.equal(g.s.familyMode,'original')<br>assert(!g.ruler().family)<br>assert.equal(f.children.length,1)<br>assert.equal(childAge(g.s,f.children[0]),18)<br>assert(f.children[0].nativeSlot)<br>assert.equal(g.s.officers.length,255) |
| 4. [Court marriage consumes the ruler action and exactly 100 gold, and cannot be duplicated](../tests/v15-tests.mjs#L19-L21) | test('Court marriage consumes the ruler action and exactly 100 gold, and cannot be duplicated',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),before=p.gold;g.execute('courtMarriage',{province:9});;;;const state=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;valid(g); }) | assert.equal(p.gold,before-100)<br>assert.equal(g.ruler().family.spouse.kind,'court')<br>assert(g.officer(g.ruler().leader).acted)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('courtMarriage',{province:9}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),state) |
| 5. [Royal proposals enforce age 16, ruler sex, unmarried recipient, and reciprocal family links](../tests/v15-tests.mjs#L22-L25) | test('Royal proposals enforce age 16, ruler sex, unmarried recipient, and reciprocal family links',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh(),r=g.ruler(),target=g.ruler(3),child=r.family.children[0];child.birthMonth=g.s.year*12+g.s.month-1-15*12;;child.birthMonth-=12;;acceptRoyalProposal(g,r,target,child.id);;;;valid(g); const h=fresh(),source=h.ruler(),woman=h.ruler(3);h.officer(woman.leader).sex='female';;const id=source.family.nextChild++,prince={id:source.family.id+'-c'+id,name:'Prince Test',sex:'male',birthMonth:h.s.year*12+h.s.month-1-16*12,marriedTo:null,marriedLeader:null,eligibleNotified:true};source.family.children.push(prince);acceptRoyalProposal(h,source,woman,prince.id);;valid(h); }) | assert.throws(()=&gt;checkRoyalProposal(g,r,target,child.id),/16/)<br>assert.equal(checkRoyalProposal(g,r,target,child.id),child)<br>assert.equal(target.family.spouse.child,child.id)<br>assert.equal(eligibleRoyalChildren(g.s,r).length,0)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;checkRoyalProposal(g,r,target,child.id),/unmarried/)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;checkRoyalProposal(h,source,woman,source.family.children[0].id))<br>assert.equal(woman.family.spouse.sex,'male') |
| 6. [Royal births use saved gestation, cooldown and gender; offspring do not inflate the officer roster](../tests/v15-tests.mjs#L26-L28) | test('Royal births use saved gestation, cooldown and gender; offspring do not inflate the officer roster',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh();g.execute('courtMarriage',{province:9});g.random=()=&gt;0;const start=g.s.year*12+g.s.month-1,first=report();royalFamilyUpkeep(g,first);;g.s=valid(g);setMonth(g.s,start+8);royalFamilyUpkeep(g,report());;setMonth(g.s,start+9);const birth=report();royalFamilyUpkeep(g,birth);;;;;royalFamilyUpkeep(g,report());;setMonth(g.s,start+20);royalFamilyUpkeep(g,report());;setMonth(g.s,start+21);royalFamilyUpkeep(g,report());;valid(g); }) | assert.equal(g.ruler().family.pregnancy.due,start+9)<br>assert.equal(g.ruler().family.children.length,1)<br>assert.equal(g.ruler().family.children.length,2)<br>assert.equal(birth.details[0].title,'A royal child is born')<br>assert.equal(g.ruler().family.children[1].sex,'female')<br>assert.equal(g.s.officers.length,255)<br>assert.equal(g.ruler().family.pregnancy,null)<br>assert.equal(g.ruler().family.pregnancy,null)<br>assert(g.ruler().family.pregnancy) |
| 7. [An actual monthly council includes a royal birth and survives save validation](../tests/v15-tests.mjs#L29-L29) | test('An actual monthly council includes a royal birth and survives save validation',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.execute('courtMarriage',{province:9});g.ruler().family.pregnancy={due:g.s.year*12+g.s.month,sex:'male'};const result=g.endMonth();;;}) | assert(result.details.some(e=&gt;e.title==='A royal child is born'))<br>assert(valid(g).lastReport.details.some(e=&gt;e.title==='A royal child is born')) |
| 8. [Coming-of-age notices occur once; no expected birth for unmarried or age-ineligible families](../tests/v15-tests.mjs#L30-L32) | test('Coming-of-age notices occur once; no expected birth for unmarried or age-ineligible families',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh(),f=g.ruler().family,c=f.children[0],stamp=g.s.year*12+g.s.month-1;c.birthMonth=stamp-16*12;c.eligibleNotified=false;g.random=()=&gt;0;const a=report();royalFamilyUpkeep(g,a);;;const b=report();royalFamilyUpkeep(g,b);;g.execute('courtMarriage',{province:9});f.spouse.birthMonth=stamp-46*12;royalFamilyUpkeep(g,report());;valid(g); }) | assert.equal(a.details.length,1)<br>assert.equal(f.pregnancy,null)<br>assert.equal(b.details.length,0)<br>assert.equal(f.pregnancy,null) |
| 9. [Ruler retirement preserves genealogy and frees their royal spouse for remarriage](../tests/v15-tests.mjs#L33-L35) | test('Ruler retirement preserves genealogy and frees their royal spouse for remarriage',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh(),parent=g.ruler(),target=g.ruler(3),child=parent.family.children[0];acceptRoyalProposal(g,parent,target,child.id);retireRulerFamily(g.s,target);;;;target.leader=g.province(target.home).officers.find(id=&gt;id!==target.leader);target.name=g.officer(target.leader).name;initializeRoyalFamilies(g.s);;;; }) | assert.equal(child.marriedTo,null)<br>assert(child.widowed)<br>assert(g.s.familyArchives.length)<br>assert.equal(target.family.spouse,null)<br>assert.equal(g.s.familyArchives[0].children.length,1)<br>assert(eligibleRoyalChildren(g.s,parent).includes(child)) |
| 10. [Family validation rejects malformed age, pregnancy, duplicate child and marriage links](../tests/v15-tests.mjs#L36-L38) | test('Family validation rejects malformed age, pregnancy, duplicate child and marriage links',()=&gt;{ for(const mutate of [s=&gt;s.rulers[0].family.children[0].birthMonth=s.year*12+100,s=&gt;s.rulers[0].family.pregnancy={due:s.year*12+100,sex:'female'},s=&gt;s.rulers[0].family.children.push(structuredClone(s.rulers[0].family.children[0])),s=&gt;s.rulers[0].family.children[0].marriedTo=3]){const g=fresh();mutate(g.s);;} }) | assert.throws(()=&gt;valid(g)) |
| 11. [Advisor reward estimates identify useless gold, full loyalty, horse stock, and writings prerequisites](../tests/v15-tests.mjs#L39-L41) | test('Advisor reward estimates identify useless gold, full loyalty, horse stock, and writings prerequisites',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh(false),p=g.province(9);g.ruler().advisor=33;g.officer(33).int=100;g.s.monthlyAdvice.intelligence[33]=100;g.officer(0).charm=80;g.officer(34).loyalty=50;;;g.officer(34).loyalty=100;;g.officer(34).loyalty=50;p.horses=0;;g.officer(34).int=99;;g.officer(34).int=60;;; }) | assert(orderInsight(g,'Reward · Gold',34,{amount:1}).includes('Best affordable result this order'))<br>assert(orderInsight(g,'Reward · Gold',34,{amount:50}).includes('50 → 60–61'))<br>assert(orderInsight(g,'Reward · Gold',34,{amount:50}).includes('already has loyalty 100'))<br>assert(orderInsight(g,'Reward · Horse',34,{amount:1}).includes('no horse'))<br>assert(orderInsight(g,'Reward · Writings',34,{amount:1}).includes('two fewer'))<br>assert(orderInsight(g,'Reward · Writings',34,{amount:1}).includes('60 → 61'))<br>assert(orderInsight(g,'Give food to the people',34,{amount:1000}).includes('Popular loyalty')) |

## tests/v16-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v16-tests.mjs](../tests/v16-tests.mjs). Purpose: Captured field commander is last for either winning side and remains last after saving; Monthly order availability accounts for governor, ruler, season, visitors, and ready officers without changing the campaign; Map numbers default on in old saves and persist independently of ownership; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:assert/strict`, `node:fs`, `@napi-rs/canvas`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/order-availability.mjs`, `../mjs/battle-presentation.mjs`, `../mjs/portraits.mjs`, `../mjs/officer-roster.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Captured field commander is last for either winning side and remains last after saving](../tests/v16-tests.mjs#L13-L20) | test('Captured field commander is last for either winning side and remains last after saving',()=&gt;{ for(const winner of ['attack','defend']){ const g=fresh();g.invade({province:9,target:8,governor:33,officers:[0,34],food:30000,gold:500});g.s.humanRulers.push(g.s.battle.defender);const b=g.s.battle,loser=winner==='attack'?'defend':'attack',leader=b.leaders[loser]; if(winner==='defend')for(const u of b.units.filter(u=&gt;u.side==='attack'))u.captured=true; b.outcome={winner,reason:'Enemy commander defeated'};g.resolveBattle();const owner=winner==='attack'?b.attacker:b.defender,queue=g.s.captiveDecisions.filter(c=&gt;c.owner===owner);;;; const saved=validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);; } }) | assert(queue.length&gt;1)<br>assert.equal(queue.at(-1).officer,leader)<br>assert.equal(g.s.lastBattle.captured.at(-1),leader)<br>assert.equal(saved.captiveDecisions.filter(c=&gt;c.owner===owner).at(-1).officer,leader) |
| 2. [Monthly order availability accounts for governor, ruler, season, visitors, and ready officers without changing the campaign](../tests/v16-tests.mjs#L21-L27) | test('Monthly order availability accounts for governor, ruler, season, visitors, and ready officers without changing the campaign',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;; g.officer(p.governor).acted=true;;;;;g.officer(p.governor).acted=false; g.s.month=7;;g.s.month=1;p.merchant=false;; for(const id of p.officers)g.officer(id).acted=true;;;;; g.s.provinceCompleted.push(p.id);; }) | assert.equal(orderUnavailable(g,p,'develop'),null)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.match(orderUnavailable(g,p,'reward'),/already acted/)<br>assert.match(orderUnavailable(g,p,'tax'),/already acted/)<br>assert.match(orderUnavailable(g,p,'exile'),/already acted/)<br>assert.equal(orderUnavailable(g,p,'develop'),null)<br>assert.match(orderUnavailable(g,p,'tax'),/July/)<br>assert.match(orderUnavailable(g,p,'trade'),/merchant/)<br>assert.match(orderUnavailable(g,p,'develop'),/available/)<br>assert.equal(orderUnavailable(g,p,'view'),null)<br>assert.equal(orderUnavailable(g,p,'map'),null)<br>assert.equal(orderUnavailable(g,p,'rest'),null)<br>assert.match(orderUnavailable(g,p,'develop'),/finished/) |
| 3. [Map numbers default on in old saves and persist independently of ownership](../tests/v16-tests.mjs#L28-L31) | test('Map numbers default on in old saves and persist independently of ownership',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh();; for(const ownership of [false,true]){g.s.settings.ownership=ownership;g.s.settings.showNumbers=false;const s=validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);;;} }) | assert.equal(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains).settings.showNumbers,true)<br>assert.equal(s.settings.showNumbers,false)<br>assert.equal(s.settings.ownership,ownership) |
| 4. [Only the selected living, unspent unit on the acting side blinks; unavailable tactics explain why](../tests/v16-tests.mjs#L32-L36) | test('Only the selected living, unspent unit on the acting side blinks; unavailable tactics explain why',()=&gt;{ const b={phase:'battle',side:'attack',selected:4,weather:'rain'},u={id:4,side:'attack',placed:true,ordered:false,soldiers:500};; for(const patch of [{id:5},{side:'defend'},{ordered:true},{soldiers:0},{routed:true},{fled:true},{placed:false}]); ;;;;; }) | assert(activeBattleUnit(b,u))<br>assert(!activeBattleUnit(b,{...u,...patch}))<br>assert(!activeBattleUnit({...b,phase:'deployment'},u))<br>assert(!activeBattleUnit({...b,outcome:{winner:'attack'}},u))<br>assert.match(battleOrderUnavailable(b,{...u,ordered:true},'wait'),/already/)<br>assert.match(battleOrderUnavailable(b,u,'fireball'),/rain/)<br>assert.equal(battleOrderUnavailable(b,{...u,ordered:true},'view'),null) |
| 5. [Fifteen historical identity overrides retain 352 unique assignments and date the Xiahou Dun eye injury](../tests/v16-tests.mjs#L37-L41) | test('Fifteen historical identity overrides retain 352 unique assignments and date the Xiahou Dun eye injury',()=&gt;{ const seen=new Set(OFFICER_ROSTER.map(o=&gt;JSON.stringify(portraitFrame(o,189))));; for(const name of HISTORIC_PORTRAITS){const officer=OFFICER_ROSTER.find(o=&gt;o.name===name);;;} const dun=OFFICER_ROSTER.find(o=&gt;o.name==='Xiahou Dun');;; }) | assert.equal(seen.size,352)<br>assert(officer)<br>assert.equal(portraitFrame(officer).atlas,'officersHistoric')<br>assert.equal(portraitFrame(dun,197).cell,15)<br>assert.equal(portraitFrame(dun,198).cell,5) |

<a id="tests-v17-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v17-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v17-tests.mjs](../tests/v17-tests.mjs). Purpose: all 41 unique paintings load, retain aspect ratio and match the original terrain identity and renderer registration; battlefield rendering has no terrain autotiling or texture blending path; the faster battlefield composition and separately preloaded victory artwork are present.

Shared inputs: `config/battlefield-paintings.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`, `assets/music/battle-music-v17.mp3`, `assets/music/triumph-music.mp3`, `assets/artwork/triumph-v17.webp`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:assert/strict`, `node:fs`, `node:crypto`, `@napi-rs/canvas`, `../mjs/battlefield-art.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [all 41 unique paintings load, retain aspect ratio and match the original terrain identity and renderer registration](../tests/v17-tests.mjs#L1-L21) | const sha=data=&gt;crypto.createHash('sha256').update(data).digest('hex'); const manifest=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('config/battlefield-paintings.json')); const terrains=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('config/province-terrain.json')); ;; const hashes=new Set(); for(const map of manifest.maps){ const bytes=fs.readFileSync(map.asset);;;hashes.add(map.sha256); ;; ;; const image=await loadImage(bytes);;;; const screen=p=&gt;({x:p.x*10+100,y:p.z*10+104}),rect=paintingRect(image,10,screen); ;;; const canvas=createCanvas(200,220),ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');paintBattlefield(ctx,image,10,screen);const pixels=ctx.getImageData(0,0,200,220).data; ; } | assert.equal(manifest.maps.length,41)<br>assert.deepEqual(manifest.maps.map(m=&gt;m.province),Array.from({length:41},(_,i)=&gt;i+1))<br>assert.equal(sha(bytes),map.sha256)<br>assert(!hashes.has(map.sha256))<br>assert.deepEqual(map.terrain,terrains[map.province-1])<br>assert.equal(map.terrainSha256,sha(Buffer.from(terrains[map.province-1])))<br>assert.equal(map.asset,'assets/artwork/'+battlefieldFile(map.province))<br>assert.equal(battlefieldKey(map.province),'battlefield'+String(map.province).padStart(2,'0'))<br>assert.equal(image.width,map.width)<br>assert.equal(image.height,map.height)<br>assert(Math.abs(image.width/image.height-1024/1109)&lt;.002)<br>assert(Math.abs(rect.width/rect.height-image.width/image.height)&lt;1e-12)<br>assert.equal(rect.x,0)<br>assert.equal(rect.y,screen({x:0,z:BATTLEFIELD_BOUNDS.top}).y)<br>assert(pixels.some((v,i)=&gt;i%4!==3&amp;&amp;v&gt;70)) |
| 2. [battlefield rendering has no terrain autotiling or texture blending path](../tests/v17-tests.mjs#L21-L24) | ; const code=fs.readFileSync('mjs/raster-world.mjs','utf8');;;; ; | assert(!code.includes('createTerrainPainter'))<br>assert(!code.includes('terrainVariant'))<br>assert(code.includes('paintBattlefield'))<br>assert(!fs.existsSync('mjs/terrain-art.mjs')) |
| 3. [the faster battlefield composition and separately preloaded victory artwork are present](../tests/v17-tests.mjs#L24-L27) | ; const audio=fs.readFileSync('tools/build-battle-audio.py','utf8');;;; const victory=await loadImage('assets/artwork/triumph-v17.webp');; | assert(audio.includes('BATTLE_BPM=144'))<br>assert(fs.statSync('assets/music/battle-music-v17.mp3').size&gt;100000)<br>assert(fs.statSync('assets/music/triumph-music.mp3').size&gt;100000)<br>assert.equal(victory.width/victory.height,16/9) |

<a id="tests-v18-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v18-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v18-tests.mjs](../tests/v18-tests.mjs). Purpose: March requires a remaining governor, rejects invalid choices atomically and preserves appointment through battle save/load; Candidate advice changes with loyalty and describes military/economic risk without consuming RNG; Province 9 map routes share the native graph with War/Move and exclude visually close Province 18; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/game-ui.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [March requires a remaining governor, rejects invalid choices atomically and preserves appointment through battle save/load](../tests/v18-tests.mjs#L1-L4) | const scenarios=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('config/scenarios.json')),terrains=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('config/province-terrain.json')),fresh=()=&gt;new Game(createCampaign(scenarios[0],[0]),{},terrains); const g=fresh(),args={province:9,target:8,officers:[0,34],food:20000,gold:500},before=JSON.stringify(g.s); ;;;;g.invade({...args,governor:35});;;const h=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains),{},terrains);h.normalize();;h.s.battle.outcome={winner:'defend',reason:'Withdrawn'};h.resolveBattle();; | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.invade(args),/Choose who will govern/)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.invade({...args,governor:34}),/remains/)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.equal(g.province(9).governor,35)<br>assert(!g.officer(35).acted)<br>assert.equal(h.province(9).governor,35)<br>assert.equal(h.province(9).governor,0) |
| 2. [Candidate advice changes with loyalty and describes military/economic risk without consuming RNG](../tests/v18-tests.mjs#L4-L5) | ; const a=fresh();a.ruler().advisor=33;a.officer(33).int=95;a.s.monthlyAdvice.intelligence[33]=100;const o=a.officer(35);o.loyalty=35;o.soldiers=0;const seed=a.s.seed,low=orderInsight(a,'Appoint governor',35);;;;o.loyalty=99;const high=orderInsight(a,'Appoint governor',35);;;; | assert(low.includes('defection/rebellion'))<br>assert(low.includes('LOY 35'))<br>assert(low.includes('vulnerable'))<br>assert(high.includes('Strong loyalty'))<br>assert.notEqual(high,low)<br>assert.equal(a.s.seed,seed) |
| 3. [Province 9 map routes share the native graph with War/Move and exclude visually close Province 18](../tests/v18-tests.mjs#L5-L6) | ; const p=a.province(9);;;;const code=fs.readFileSync('mjs/raster-world.mjs','utf8');;; | assert.deepEqual([...p.neighbors].sort((x,y)=&gt;x-y),[6,7,8,10,16,17])<br>assert.deepEqual(p.neighbors.filter(id=&gt;[255,p.owner].includes(a.province(id).owner)),[17])<br>assert(!p.neighbors.includes(18))<br>assert(code.includes('new Set(selectedProvince.neighbors)'))<br>assert(code.includes('connected.has(p.id)')) |
| 4. [Rival Tigers uses distinct envoys and destinations; malformed assignments are rejected and diplomacy uses one envoy](../tests/v18-tests.mjs#L6-L7) | ; const r=fresh(),raw=JSON.stringify(r.s),mission={province:9,mode:'rival',target:3,other:10,officer:33};;;r.execute('spyMission',{...mission,second:34});const j=r.s.journey;;;;;validateSave(r.s,scenarios,terrains);const bad=structuredClone(r.s);bad.journey.legs.pop();;const s=fresh();s.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'alliance',target:3,officer:33});; | assert.throws(()=&gt;r.execute('spyMission',{...mission,second:33}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(r.s),raw)<br>assert.equal(j.legs.length,2)<br>assert.deepEqual(j.legs.map(l=&gt;l.officer),[33,34])<br>assert.equal(j.legs[0].route.at(-1),r.ruler(3).home)<br>assert.equal(j.legs[1].route.at(-1),r.ruler(10).home)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(bad,scenarios,terrains),/messenger/)<br>assert.equal(s.s.journey.legs.length,1) |

<a id="tests-v19-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v19-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v19-tests.mjs](../tests/v19-tests.mjs). Purpose: every order is rolled before presentation, INT80 has independent available/unavailable orders and retries/save-load preserve decisions without RNG; changing candidates, amounts or midmonth intelligence cannot reroll an unavailable order; council advice shares the same monthly gate; monthly rollover refreshes capability, INT100/0 are exact and malformed decisions reject while old saves migrate; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/monthly-advice.mjs`, `../mjs/game-ui.mjs`, `../mjs/province-rules.mjs`, `../mjs/advice-topics.mjs`, `../mjs/province-choice.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [every order is rolled before presentation, INT80 has independent available/unavailable orders and retries/save-load preserve decisions without RNG](../tests/v19-tests.mjs#L1-L4) | const scenarios=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('config/scenarios.json')),terrains=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('config/province-terrain.json'));const s=createCampaign(scenarios[0],[0]),g=new Game(s,{},terrains),r=g.ruler(),a=g.officer(33);r.advisor=33;s.monthlyAdvice.intelligence[33]=80;a.int=80; const rolls=s.monthlyAdvice.rolls[r.id];;const good=ADVICE_ORDERS.find(k=&gt;rolls[k]&lt;.8),bad=ADVICE_ORDERS.find(k=&gt;rolls[k]&gt;=.8);;;;const frozen=JSON.stringify(s.monthlyAdvice),seed=s.seed; for(let i=0;i&lt;20;i++){;;};;const restored=new Game(validateSave(s,scenarios,terrains),{},terrains);;; | assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(rolls),ADVICE_ORDERS)<br>assert(good&amp;&amp;bad)<br>assert(canAdvise(s,r,a,good))<br>assert(!canAdvise(s,r,a,bad))<br>assert(!canAdvise(s,r,a,bad))<br>assert(canAdvise(s,r,a,good))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(s.monthlyAdvice),frozen)<br>assert.equal(s.seed,seed)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(restored.s.monthlyAdvice),frozen)<br>assert(!canAdvise(restored.s,restored.ruler(),restored.officer(33),bad)) |
| 2. [changing candidates, amounts or midmonth intelligence cannot reroll an unavailable order; council advice shares the same monthly gate](../tests/v19-tests.mjs#L4-L5) | ; s.monthlyAdvice.rolls[r.id].cultivate=.9;const unavailable=orderInsight(g,'Cultivate',34,{amount:10});;;a.int=100;;s.monthlyAdvice.rolls[r.id].general=.9;s.monthlyAdvice.rolls[r.id].war=.9;;; | assert(unavailable.includes(ADVICE_UNAVAILABLE))<br>assert(orderInsight(g,'Cultivate',35,{amount:90}).includes(ADVICE_UNAVAILABLE))<br>assert(orderInsight(g,'Cultivate',34).includes(ADVICE_UNAVAILABLE))<br>assert(provinceAdvice(g,g.province(9)).includes(ADVICE_UNAVAILABLE))<br>assert(adviceTopic(g,g.province(9),'war').includes(ADVICE_UNAVAILABLE)) |
| 3. [monthly rollover refreshes capability, INT100/0 are exact and malformed decisions reject while old saves migrate](../tests/v19-tests.mjs#L5-L6) | ; const oldMonth=s.monthlyAdvice.month;g.endMonth();;;;s.monthlyAdvice.intelligence[33]=0;;const broken=structuredClone(s);broken.monthlyAdvice.rolls[0].war=2;;const legacy=structuredClone(s);delete legacy.monthlyAdvice;; | assert.equal(s.monthlyAdvice.month,oldMonth+1)<br>assert.equal(s.monthlyAdvice.intelligence[33],100)<br>assert(ADVICE_ORDERS.every(k=&gt;canAdvise(s,r,a,k)))<br>assert(ADVICE_ORDERS.every(k=&gt;!canAdvise(s,r,a,k)))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(broken,scenarios,terrains),/advice/)<br>assert(validateSave(legacy,scenarios,terrains).monthlyAdvice) |
| 4. [province choice metadata and both sort labels preserve city numbers, names and rulers](../tests/v19-tests.mjs#L6-L7) | ; ;;;;for(const p of s.provinces){const choice=provinceChoice(g,p);;;;;;} | assert.equal(adviceOrder('Reward · Gold'),'reward:gold')<br>assert.equal(adviceOrder('Cultivate'),'cultivate')<br>assert.equal(adviceOrder('War invasion'),'war')<br>assert.equal(adviceOrder('Spy · Rival tigers'),'spy:rival')<br>assert.equal(choice[2].province,p.id)<br>assert(choice[1].includes('#'+p.id))<br>assert(choice[1].includes(p.name))<br>assert(choice[1].includes(p.owner===255?'Unclaimed':g.ruler(p.owner).name))<br>assert(provinceLabel(g,p,true).startsWith(p.name)) |

<a id="tests-v20-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v20-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v20-tests.mjs](../tests/v20-tests.mjs). Purpose: Hard AI crosses a distant mountain gap, including initial sideways/away steps, and reaches the palace; Route planning respects mountains, active fires and occupied hexes, returning no route for a sealed destination; Defender plans toward reachable enemy contact rather than trying to enter an occupied enemy hex; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`, `tests/fixtures/v19-hard-second-battle.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Hard AI crosses a distant mountain gap, including initial sideways/away steps, and reaches the palace](../tests/v20-tests.mjs#L1-L7) | const scenarios=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('config/scenarios.json')),terrains=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('config/province-terrain.json')); const fresh=()=&gt;{const state=createCampaign(scenarios[0],[],3);state.seed=6;state.ai={intelligence:90,aggression:75};state.settings.battleView='all';state.familyMode='expanded';return new Game(state,{},structuredClone(terrains));}; function arena(){const g=fresh();g.invade({province:9,target:8,officers:[0,34],governor:33,food:20000});while(g.s.battle.phase==='deployment')aiBattle(g,60);const b=g.s.battle,u=b.units.find(u=&gt;u.id===0);b.terrain=Array(156).fill(0);b.palace={q:8,r:2};b.terrain[2*13+8]=6;b.weather='sunny';b.fire={};b.challengeIssued=[34];b.food … (complete input/procedure at linked L1–7) | assert(route)<br>assert(route.path.some(p=&gt;p.q===4&amp;&amp;p.r===11))<br>assert(route.path.every(p=&gt;b.terrain[p.r*13+p.q]!==3))<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,'move')<br>assert(distance(u,b.palace)&gt;=before,'First detour step must not need to reduce straight-line distance')<br>assert.equal(u.q,b.palace.q)<br>assert.equal(u.r,b.palace.r) |
| 2. [Route planning respects mountains, active fires and occupied hexes, returning no route for a sealed destination](../tests/v20-tests.mjs#L7-L10) | ; } { const {b,u}=arena();for(let r=0;r&lt;12;r++)b.terrain[r*13+4]=3;b.units=b.units.filter(v=&gt;v.side===u.side);;for(const n of neighbors(u.q,u.r))b.fire[n.r*13+n.q]=3;;const x=arena();const ally=x.b.units.find(v=&gt;v.side===x.u.side&amp;&amp;v!==x.u);Object.assign(ally,{q:4,r:2});x.b.fire[3*13+4]=3;const route=planBattleRoute(x.b,x.u);;;; | assert.equal(planBattleRoute(b,u),null)<br>assert.equal(planBattleRoute(b,u),null)<br>assert(route)<br>assert(route.path.every(p=&gt;!x.b.fire[p.r*13+p.q]&amp;&amp;at(x.b,p.q,p.r)?.id!==ally.id))<br>assert(reachable(x.b,x.u).some(p=&gt;p.q===route.path[0].q&amp;&amp;p.r===route.path[0].r)) |
| 3. [Defender plans toward reachable enemy contact rather than trying to enter an occupied enemy hex](../tests/v20-tests.mjs#L10-L13) | ; } { const {g,b,u}=arena();const enemy=b.units.find(v=&gt;v.side!==u.side);Object.assign(enemy,{q:4,r:2});b.side='defend';for(const v of b.units.filter(v=&gt;v.side==='defend'))Object.assign(v,{ordered:true});Object.assign(enemy,{ordered:false,mobility:2,war:79});const route=planBattleRoute(b,enemy);;;; | assert(route)<br>assert.equal(distance(route, u),1)<br>assert(route.path.every(p=&gt;at(b,p.q,p.r)?.id!==u.id)) |
| 4. [V19 second-battle Hard checkpoint advances past day one without attempting another challenge, including save/load](../tests/v20-tests.mjs#L13-L16) | ; } { const raw=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('tests/fixtures/v19-hard-second-battle.json')),g=new Game(validateSave(raw,scenarios,terrains),{},terrains),b=g.s.battle;;;;;;;for(let n=0;n&lt;80&amp;&amp;g.s.battle===b&amp;&amp;b.day===1;n++);;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains); | assert.equal(g.s.difficulty,3)<br>assert.deepEqual(g.s.humanRulers,[])<br>assert.equal(g.s.ai.intelligence,90)<br>assert.equal(g.s.settings.battleView,'all')<br>assert.equal(b.day,1)<br>assert(b.challengeIssued.length)<br>assert(g.aiStep())<br>assert(!g.s.battle\|\|g.s.battle!==b\|\|b.day&gt;1,'Previously stalled second battle must advance beyond day one')<br>assert.equal(b.challengeIssued.length,1) |
| 5. [Zero-player Hard/all-battles campaigns complete 15,000 actions across six scenarios (${completed} battle starts) with valid saves and no tactical exceptions](../tests/v20-tests.mjs#L16-L19) | ; } { let completed=0,days=0;for(const scenario of scenarios){const state=createCampaign(scenario,[],3);state.seed=6;state.ai={intelligence:90,aggression:75};state.settings.battleView='all';state.familyMode='expanded';const g=new Game(state,{},terrains);let previous=null,seen=0;for(let i=0;i&lt;2500;i++){if(g.s.battle&amp;&amp;g.s.battle!==previous){previous=g.s.battle;seen++;}g.aiStep();if(g.s.battle?.day&gt;1)days++;if(i%500===0)validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);;completed+=seen;}; | assert(seen&gt;=2,'Every scenario must exercise consecutive visible AI battles')<br>assert(days&gt;0) |

## tests/v21-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v21-tests.mjs](../tests/v21-tests.mjs). Purpose: Lone defender holds the palace against multiple distant attackers at every AI level; Palace guard attacks adjacent enemies in place and does not charge onto a defeated noncommander tile; Defender prioritises a guaranteed attacking-commander kill and wins without abandoning palace; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Lone defender holds the palace against multiple distant attackers at every AI level](../tests/v21-tests.mjs#L4-L4) | test('Lone defender holds the palace against multiple distant attackers at every AI level',()=&gt;{for(const intelligence of [25,60,90]){const {g,b,guard}=arena();for(let day=0;day&lt;8;day++){guard.ordered=false;b.side='defend';aiBattle(g,intelligence);;;;}}}) | assert.deepEqual({q:guard.q,r:guard.r},b.palace)<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,'wait')<br>assert.equal(palaceGuard(b).id,guard.id) |
| 2. [Palace guard attacks adjacent enemies in place and does not charge onto a defeated noncommander tile](../tests/v21-tests.mjs#L5-L5) | test('Palace guard attacks adjacent enemies in place and does not charge onto a defeated noncommander tile',()=&gt;{const {g,b,f,guard}=arena();Object.assign(f,direction(guard.q,guard.r,5),{soldiers:100});Object.assign(guard,{soldiers:10000,weapons:10000,war:99});aiBattle(g,90);;;;;}) | assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,'attack')<br>assert.equal(f.soldiers,0)<br>assert.deepEqual({q:guard.q,r:guard.r},b.palace)<br>assert.equal(at(b,b.palace.q,b.palace.r).id,guard.id) |
| 3. [Defender prioritises a guaranteed attacking-commander kill and wins without abandoning palace](../tests/v21-tests.mjs#L6-L6) | test('Defender prioritises a guaranteed attacking-commander kill and wins without abandoning palace',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,guard}=arena();Object.assign(u,direction(guard.q,guard.r,5),{soldiers:1});Object.assign(f,direction(guard.q,guard.r,2),{soldiers:1});;;aiBattle(g,90);;;;}) | assert.equal(minimumStrikeLoss(b,guard,u),u.soldiers)<br>assert.equal(aiAttackTarget(b,guard,[f,u]).id,u.id)<br>assert.equal(u.soldiers,0)<br>assert.equal(b.outcome.winner,'defend')<br>assert.deepEqual({q:guard.q,r:guard.r},b.palace) |
| 4. [Multiple defenders retain a palace garrison while another unit intercepts approaching enemies](../tests/v21-tests.mjs#L7-L7) | test('Multiple defenders retain a palace garrison while another unit intercepts approaching enemies',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,guard,reserve}=arena(true);guard.ordered=true;Object.assign(u,{q:6,r:2});Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:1});aiBattle(g,90);;;;;}) | assert.equal(b.lastAction.unit,reserve.id)<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,'move')<br>assert.equal(at(b,b.palace.q,b.palace.r).id,guard.id)<br>assert(distance(reserve,u)&lt;5) |
| 5. [An out-of-position sole defender returns toward an empty palace instead of pursuing distant attackers](../tests/v21-tests.mjs#L8-L8) | test('An out-of-position sole defender returns toward an empty palace instead of pursuing distant attackers',()=&gt;{const {g,b,guard}=arena();Object.assign(guard,{q:6,r:8});aiBattle(g,90);;;}) | assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,'move')<br>assert(distance(guard,b.palace)&lt;2) |
| 6. [Attackers advance on an empty palace and prioritise its guard over unrelated weak neighbours](../tests/v21-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Attackers advance on an empty palace and prioritise its guard over unrelated weak neighbours',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,guard}=arena();b.side='attack';guard.placed=true;Object.assign(guard,{q:12,r:11});aiBattle(g,90);;;const x=arena(true);x.b.side='attack';Object.assign(x.u,{q:5,r:6});Object.assign(x.reserve,direction(x.u.q,x.u.r,4),{soldiers:1});;}) | assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,'move')<br>assert(distance(u,b.palace)&lt;distance({q:0,r:0},b.palace))<br>assert.equal(aiAttackTarget(x.b,x.u,[x.reserve,x.guard]).id,x.guard.id) |
| 7. [Palace guard refuses uncertain personal combat but can challenge a commander it is certain to defeat](../tests/v21-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Palace guard refuses uncertain personal combat but can challenge a commander it is certain to defeat',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,guard}=arena();b.day=1;b.completedDays=0;Object.assign(u,{war:95});Object.assign(guard,{war:95});b.challenge={challenger:u.id,target:guard.id};b.challengeIssued=[u.id];aiBattle(g,90);;;;const x=arena();x.b.day=1;x.b.completedDays=0;x.u.war=50;x.guard.war=90;aiBattle(x.g,90);;}) | assert.equal(b.challenge,null)<br>assert(!guard.captured)<br>assert.deepEqual({q:guard.q,r:guard.r},b.palace)<br>assert.equal(x.b.challenge.target,x.u.id) |

<a id="tests-v22-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v22-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v22-tests.mjs](../tests/v22-tests.mjs). Purpose: Outnumbered palace guard uses safe fire without abandoning palace; Fire refuses rain and wind spreading into own commander; Defender selects nearby jungle concealment and holds an ambush; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Outnumbered palace guard uses safe fire without abandoning palace](../tests/v22-tests.mjs#L5-L5) | test('Outnumbered palace guard uses safe fire without abandoning palace',()=&gt;{const {g,b,f,guard}=arena();b.wind=0;b.windStrength=0;Object.assign(guard,{soldiers:1000,weapons:1000});Object.assign(f,direction(guard.q,guard.r,5));aiBattle(g,90);;;}) | assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,'fireball')<br>assert.deepEqual({q:guard.q,r:guard.r},b.palace) |
| 2. [Fire refuses rain and wind spreading into own commander](../tests/v22-tests.mjs#L6-L6) | test('Fire refuses rain and wind spreading into own commander',()=&gt;{const {b,f,guard}=arena();Object.assign(f,direction(guard.q,guard.r,5));b.weather='rain';;b.weather='sunny';for(const c of neighbors(guard.q,guard.r))b.terrain[c.r*13+c.q]=4;b.terrain[f.r*13+f.q]=0;b.wind=Array.from({length:6},(_,i)=&gt;i).find(i=&gt;distance(direction(f.q,f.r,i),guard)===0)+1;b.windStrength=3;;}) | assert.equal(safeFireTarget(b,guard,[f]),null)<br>assert.equal(safeFireTarget(b,guard,[f]),null) |
| 3. [Defender selects nearby jungle concealment and holds an ambush](../tests/v22-tests.mjs#L7-L7) | test('Defender selects nearby jungle concealment and holds an ambush',()=&gt;{const {b,u,reserve}=arena(true);Object.assign(u,{q:5,r:2});const c={q:5,r:4};b.terrain[c.r*13+c.q]=1;reserve.mobility=6;const choice=tacticalPosition(b,reserve,[u],[c],true);;;;Object.assign(reserve,c);;}) | assert(choice)<br>assert.equal(choice.q,c.q)<br>assert.equal(choice.r,c.r)<br>assert.equal(tacticalPosition(b,reserve,[u],[],true).wait,true) |
| 4. [Weak attacker selects dry river bank, never water, and resumes after bounded waiting](../tests/v22-tests.mjs#L8-L8) | test('Weak attacker selects dry river bank, never water, and resumes after bounded waiting',()=&gt;{const {b,u,guard}=arena();b.side='attack';Object.assign(u,{q:3,r:3});Object.assign(guard,{q:6,r:3});b.terrain[3*13+5]=4;const c={q:4,r:3};;Object.assign(u,c);;b.day=3;;}) | assert.equal(tacticalPosition(b,u,[guard],[c],true).q,4)<br>assert.equal(tacticalPosition(b,u,[guard],[],true).wait,true)<br>assert.equal(tacticalPosition(b,u,[guard],[],true),null) |
| 5. [Attacking commander refuses uncertain duel with army still intact](../tests/v22-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Attacking commander refuses uncertain duel with army still intact',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,guard}=arena();b.day=1;b.completedDays=0;b.side='defend';u.war=90;guard.war=89;b.challenge={challenger:guard.id,target:u.id};b.challengeIssued=[guard.id];aiBattle(g,90);;;}) | assert(!u.captured)<br>assert.equal(b.challenge,null) |
| 6. [Remaining soldiers do not disguise morale routing as empty army](../tests/v22-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Remaining soldiers do not disguise morale routing as empty army',()=&gt;{const {b}=arena();for(const v of b.units.filter(v=&gt;v.side==='defend')){v.routed=true;v.morale=0;};}) | assert.match(outcome(b).reason,/remaining soldiers/) |

<a id="tests-v23-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v23-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v23-tests.mjs](../tests/v23-tests.mjs). Purpose: Every Message Speed lasts exactly 3, 2, 1 or 0.5 seconds without dropped queued messages; Message pause retains unread time, resume does not restart it and cancellation prevents stale callbacks; AI tuning validates and merges partial groups while preserving default battlefield policy; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`, `config/ai-config.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-parameters.mjs`, `../mjs/message-playback.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Every Message Speed lasts exactly 3, 2, 1 or 0.5 seconds without dropped queued messages](../tests/v23-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Every Message Speed lasts exactly 3, 2, 1 or 0.5 seconds without dropped queued messages',()=&gt;{for(const [ms]of MESSAGE_SPEEDS){const c=clock(),seen=[],done=[];const p=createMessagePlayer({clock:c,duration:()=&gt;messageDuration({settings:{messageSpeed:ms}}),show:x=&gt;seen.push(x.text),hide:x=&gt;done.push(x.text)});p.enqueue('first');p.enqueue('second');;c.tick(ms-1);;c.tick(1);;c.tick(ms);;;}}) | assert.deepEqual(seen,['first'])<br>assert.deepEqual(done,[])<br>assert.deepEqual(seen,['first','second'])<br>assert.deepEqual(done,['first','second'])<br>assert(!p.busy) |
| 2. [Message pause retains unread time, resume does not restart it and cancellation prevents stale callbacks](../tests/v23-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Message pause retains unread time, resume does not restart it and cancellation prevents stale callbacks',()=&gt;{const c=clock(),seen=[],p=createMessagePlayer({clock:c,duration:()=&gt;2000,show:x=&gt;seen.push(x.text)});p.enqueue({text:'monthly',onDone:()=&gt;seen.push('orders')});c.tick(800);p.pause();c.tick(9000);;p.resume();c.tick(1199);;c.tick(1);;p.enqueue({text:'old save',onDone:()=&gt;});p.clear();c.tick(3000);;}) | assert(p.busy)<br>assert.deepEqual(seen,['monthly'])<br>assert.deepEqual(seen,['monthly','orders'])<br>assert.fail('Stale timer')<br>assert(!p.busy) |
| 3. [AI tuning validates and merges partial groups while preserving default battlefield policy](../tests/v23-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('AI tuning validates and merges partial groups while preserving default battlefield policy',()=&gt;{;const t=validateAIConfig({battle:{outnumberedPowerRatio:.95}});;;;;;;}) | assert.deepEqual(validateAIConfig(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('config/ai-config.json'))),DEFAULT_AI)<br>assert.equal(t.battle.outnumberedPowerRatio,.95)<br>assert.equal(t.battle.safeDuelWarMargin,20)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateAIConfig({battle:{ambushAdvanceEveryDays:0}}))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateAIConfig({governance:{maxInvaders:11}}))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateAIConfig({governance:{politicsChance:1.1}}))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateAIConfig({unknown:{}})) |
| 4. [Both armies lose immediately at zero food while their morale and soldiers remain high](../tests/v23-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Both armies lose immediately at zero food while their morale and soldiers remain high',()=&gt;{for(const side of ['attack','defend']){const {b}=arena();b.food[side]=0;const men=living(b).filter(u=&gt;u.side===side).reduce((n,u)=&gt;n+u.soldiers,0);;;;;}}) | assert(men&gt;0)<br>assert(living(b).filter(u=&gt;u.side===side).every(u=&gt;u.morale===100))<br>assert.equal(outcome(b).winner,side==='attack'?'defend':'attack')<br>assert.match(b.outcome.reason,/food/) |
| 5. [Food input accepts all available province stock through 3,000,000 and rejects excess atomically](../tests/v23-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Food input accepts all available province stock through 3,000,000 and rejects excess atomically',()=&gt;{const s=createCampaign(scenarios[0],[0]);s.settings.battleView='all';const g=new Game(s,{},terrains);g.s.monthlyReview=null;g.province(9).food=3000000;const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;g.invade({province:9,target:8,officers:[0,34],governor:33,food:3000000});;;const zero=new Game(createCampaign(scenarios[0],[0]),{},terrains);zero.s.monthlyReview=null;zero.s.settings.battleView='all';zero.invade({province:9,target:8,officers:[0,34],governor:33,food:0});;zero.s.battle.phase='battle';;}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.invade({province:9,target:8,officers:[0,34],governor:33,food:3000001}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle.food.attack,3000000)<br>assert.equal(g.province(9).food,0)<br>assert.equal(zero.s.battle.food.attack,0)<br>assert.equal(outcome(zero.s.battle).winner,'defend') |
| 6. [Fire success/failure and simultaneous attacks identify initiating generals and retain ordered event serials](../tests/v23-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Fire success/failure and simultaneous attacks identify initiating generals and retain ordered event serials',()=&gt;{for(const fail of [false,true]){const {g,b,u,defender}=arena();g.random=()=&gt;fail?.99:0;const t=g.battleAction('fireball',{unit:u.id,q:defender.q,r:defender.r});;;;}const {g,b,u,v,defender}=arena();Object.assign(v,direction(defender.q,defender.r,1));const text=g.battleAction('simultaneous',{unit:u.id,target:defender.id});;;;const restored=validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains);;}) | assert(t.includes(g.officer(u.id).name))<br>assert.match(t,fail?/failed/:/success/)<br>assert.equal(b.messages.at(-1).text,t)<br>assert(text.includes(g.officer(u.id).name))<br>assert.match(text,/simultaneous/)<br>assert(b.messages.every((m,i)=&gt;!i\|\|m.serial&gt;b.messages[i-1].serial))<br>assert.deepEqual(restored.battle.messages,b.messages) |
| 7. [Legacy saves receive Message Speed from their former speed and malformed message queues are rejected](../tests/v23-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Legacy saves receive Message Speed from their former speed and malformed message queues are rejected',()=&gt;{for(const [old,ms]of [[1200,3000],[600,2000],[300,1000],[100,500]]){const s=createCampaign(scenarios[0],[0]);delete s.settings.messageSpeed;s.speed=old;;}const {g,b}=arena();b.messages.push({serial:b.eventSerial+1,text:'future',day:2});;}) | assert.equal(validateSave(s,scenarios,terrains).settings.messageSpeed,ms)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains),/battle messages/) |

<a id="tests-v24-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v24-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v24-tests.mjs](../tests/v24-tests.mjs). Purpose: Native daily/monthly rations use floor division by thirty with a minimum of one for an army; AI carries food based on actual troops and refuses less than a full month plus one reserve; Defending undeployed reserves eat alongside the field army while the attacker source garrison does not; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-governance.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-parameters.mjs`, `../mjs/war-provisions.mjs`, `../mjs/message-playback.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Native daily/monthly rations use floor division by thirty with a minimum of one for an army](../tests/v24-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Native daily/monthly rations use floor division by thirty with a minimum of one for an army',()=&gt;{for(const [men,daily]of [[0,0],[1,1],[29,1],[30,1],[31,1],[2999,99],[3000,100],[3872,129],[10000,333]]){;;};}) | assert.equal(battleDailyFood(men),daily)<br>assert.equal(warProvisions(men).monthly,daily*30)<br>assert.equal(warProvisions(3872,242).days,1) |
| 2. [AI carries food based on actual troops and refuses less than a full month plus one reserve](../tests/v24-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('AI carries food based on actual troops and refuses less than a full month plus one reserve',()=&gt;{;;;const g=fresh();g.s.ai.aggression=100;g.s.ai.intelligence=100;g.random=()=&gt;.99;g.s.journey=null;g.s.familyMode='original';g.s.player=0;const p=g.province(9);for(const id of p.officers){g.officer(id).soldiers=10000;g.officer(id).training=0;g.officer(id).acted=false;}p.food=100000;let chosen=null;g.invade=args=&gt;{chosen=args;};g.random=()=&gt;.5;aiGovernance(g);;const men=chosen.officers.reduce((n,id)=&gt;n+g.officer(id).soldiers,0);;;}) | assert.equal(invasionFoodPlan(10000,100000),333*75+1)<br>assert.equal(invasionFoodPlan(10000,9990),null)<br>assert.equal(invasionFoodPlan(10000,9991),9991)<br>assert(chosen)<br>assert.equal(chosen.food,invasionFoodPlan(men,100000,g.rules.ai.governance))<br>assert(chosen.food&gt;warProvisions(men).monthly) |
| 3. [Defending undeployed reserves eat alongside the field army while the attacker source garrison does not](../tests/v24-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Defending undeployed reserves eat alongside the field army while the attacker source garrison does not',()=&gt;{const g=pending(),b=g.s.battle,def=b.units.filter(u=&gt;u.side==='defend').reduce((n,u)=&gt;n+u.soldiers,0),att=b.units.filter(u=&gt;u.side==='attack').reduce((n,u)=&gt;n+u.soldiers,0);;const reserve=g.province(b.target).officers.find(id=&gt;!b.units.some(u=&gt;u.id===id));if(reserve!==undefined){g.officer(reserve).soldiers=9000;;}else{const id=g.province(b.source).officers.find(id=&gt;!b.units.some(u=&gt;u.id===id));g.province(b.source).officers=g.province(b.source).officers.filter(v=&gt;v!==id);g.province(b.target).officers.push(id);g.officer(id).owner=b.defender;g.officer(id).soldiers=9000;;}}) | assert.equal(battleRationMen(g,b,'attack'),att)<br>assert(battleRationMen(g,b,'defend')&gt;=def+9000)<br>assert.equal(battleRationMen(g,b,'defend'),def+9000) |
| 4. [Spectator personal-only invasion resolves by equation with no tactical actions, placement or suspended war](../tests/v24-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Spectator personal-only invasion resolves by equation with no tactical actions, placement or suspended war',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.invade({province:9,target:8,officers:[0,34],food:20000});;;;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert.equal(g.s.battle,null)<br>assert.equal(g.s.lastBattle.resolution,'equation')<br>assert(g.s.lastBattle.abstractRounds&lt;=6)<br>assert.equal(g.s.wars.length,0)<br>assert(!g.s.log.some(x=&gt;/moved to\|Day \d+:\|waited as army/.test(x.text))) |
| 5. [Loading an active hidden battle and resuming a suspended hidden war never exposes a tactical step](../tests/v24-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Loading an active hidden battle and resuming a suspended hidden war never exposes a tactical step',()=&gt;{const g=pending(),b=g.s.battle;const initial=b.actionSerial;g.s.settings.battleView='personal';const loaded=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains),{},terrains);;;;const other=pending(),war=other.s.battle;while(war.phase==='deployment')aiBattle(other);war.suspended=true;other.s.monthlyReview=null;other.s.settings.battleView='personal';other.s.battle=null;war.resumeMonth=other.s.year*12+other.s.month-1;other.s.wars.push(war);const serial=war.actionSerial;;;;;;validateSave(other.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert.equal(loaded.s.battle,null)<br>assert.equal(loaded.s.lastBattle.resolution,'equation')<br>assert.equal(b.actionSerial,initial)<br>assert(other.resumeWars())<br>assert.equal(other.s.battle,null)<br>assert.equal(other.s.wars.length,0)<br>assert.equal(war.actionSerial,serial)<br>assert.equal(other.s.lastBattle.resolution,'equation') |
| 6. [All-battles mode retains real tactical deployment and human personally-present battles remain detailed](../tests/v24-tests.mjs#L16-L16) | test('All-battles mode retains real tactical deployment and human personally-present battles remain detailed',()=&gt;{const g=pending();;;const b=g.s.battle;g.s.humanRulers=[0];g.s.settings.battleView='personal';;;;}) | assert(g.s.battle)<br>assert(g.battleDetailed())<br>assert(g.battleDetailed())<br>assert(!g.resolveUnwatchedBattle())<br>assert.equal(g.s.battle,b) |
| 7. [Changing Message Speed retimes the current message, including a paused council report](../tests/v24-tests.mjs#L17-L17) | test('Changing Message Speed retimes the current message, including a paused council report',()=&gt;{let now=0,timer=null,id=0,ms=1000,done=0;const clock={now:()=&gt;now,setTimeout:(fn,delay)=&gt;(timer={fn,at:now+delay},++id),clearTimeout:()=&gt;{timer=null;}};const p=createMessagePlayer({clock,duration:()=&gt;ms,show:()=&gt;{},onIdle:()=&gt;done++});p.enqueue('report');now=400;p.pause();ms=3000;p.retime();now=9000;;p.resume();;now=11600;timer.fn();;p.enqueue('action');now=11700;ms=500;p.retime();;}) | assert.equal(done,0)<br>assert.equal(timer.at,11600)<br>assert.equal(done,1)<br>assert.equal(timer.at,12100) |
| 8. [Governance messages survive atomic rollback cloning without duplicating old Chronicle entries](../tests/v24-tests.mjs#L18-L18) | test('Governance messages survive atomic rollback cloning without duplicating old Chronicle entries',()=&gt;{const old=[{year:189,month:1,turn:1,type:'order',text:'prior'}],fresh={year:189,month:1,turn:1,type:'order',text:'new'};;;;}) | assert.deepEqual(newLogEntries(old,[fresh,...structuredClone(old)]),[fresh])<br>assert.deepEqual(newLogEntries(old,structuredClone(old)),[])<br>assert.equal(newLogEntries(old,[...structuredClone(old),...structuredClone(old)]).length,1) |
| 9. [Local defending reinforcements cannot duplicate committed food; reserve days require positive integers](../tests/v24-tests.mjs#L19-L19) | test('Local defending reinforcements cannot duplicate committed food; reserve days require positive integers',()=&gt;{const g=pending(),b=g.s.battle,p=g.province(b.target);const id=g.province(b.source).officers.find(id=&gt;!b.units.some(u=&gt;u.id===id));;g.province(b.source).officers=g.province(b.source).officers.filter(v=&gt;v!==id);p.officers.push(id);Object.assign(g.officer(id),{owner:b.defender,acted:false,soldiers:9000});const option=reinforcementOptions(g,'defend').find(o=&gt;o.officer===id);;;;;}) | assert.notEqual(id,undefined)<br>assert(option)<br>assert.equal(option.food,0)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateAIConfig({governance:{warProvisionDays:0}}))<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;validateAIConfig({governance:{warProvisionDays:1.5}})) |
| 10. [Saved unified speed survives loading and overrides a conflicting legacy Message Speed](../tests/v24-tests.mjs#L20-L20) | test('Saved unified speed survives loading and overrides a conflicting legacy Message Speed',()=&gt;{for(const messageSpeed of [500,1000,2000,3000]){const g=fresh();g.s.speed=messageSpeed;g.s.settings.messageSpeed=500;;}}) | assert.equal(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains).settings.messageSpeed,messageSpeed) |

## tests/v25-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v25-tests.mjs](../tests/v25-tests.mjs). Purpose: A spent move restores two next turn; Wait then banks three, four and up to six; Water costs five points in reachability and the actual Move order; Native training allowance has correct thresholds and retains banked mobility; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/message-playback.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass.
