| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [A spent move restores two next turn; Wait then banks three, four and up to six](../tests/v25-tests.mjs#L8-L8) | test('A spent move restores two next turn; Wait then banks three, four and up to six',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u}=arena();const move=reachable(b,u).find(p=&gt;p.q===u.q&amp;&amp;p.r===u.r-1);g.battleAction('move',{unit:u.id,...move});;g.battleAction('end');g.battleAction('end');;for(const expected of [3,4,5,6,6]){g.battleAction('wait',{unit:u.id});;g.battleAction('end');g.battleAction('end');;}}) | assert.equal(u.mobility,0)<br>assert.equal(u.mobility,2)<br>assert.equal(u.mobility,expected)<br>assert.equal(u.mobility,expected) |
| 2. [Water costs five points in reachability and the actual Move order](../tests/v25-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Water costs five points in reachability and the actual Move order',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u}=arena();b.terrain[(u.r-1)*13+u.q]=4;u.mobility=4;;u.mobility=5;const move=reachable(b,u).find(p=&gt;p.q===u.q&amp;&amp;p.r===u.r-1);;g.battleAction('move',{unit:u.id,...move});;;}) | assert(!reachable(b,u).some(p=&gt;p.q===u.q&amp;&amp;p.r===u.r-1))<br>assert(move)<br>assert.equal(u.mobility,0)<br>assert.equal(terrainCost(4),5) |
| 3. [Native training allowance has correct thresholds and retains banked mobility](../tests/v25-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Native training allowance has correct thresholds and retains banked mobility',()=&gt;{;}) | assert.deepEqual([0,1,20,21,40,41,80,81,100].map(dailyMobility),[2,2,2,3,3,4,5,6,6]) |
| 4. [Native fire is random below thirty percent, without mandatory hundred or morale damage](../tests/v25-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Native fire is random below thirty percent, without mandatory hundred or morale damage',()=&gt;{;;;;const {g,b,u}=arena();b.fire[u.r*13+u.q]=3;b.side='attack';g.random=()=&gt;.5;g.battleAction('end');;;}) | assert.equal(fireLoss(5000,0),0)<br>assert.equal(fireLoss(5000,.5),750)<br>assert.equal(fireLoss(5000,.999999),1499)<br>assert.equal(fireLoss(99,0),99)<br>assert.equal(u.soldiers,4250)<br>assert.equal(u.morale,100) |
| 5. [One saved speed controls scheduler and message durations, including legacy migration](../tests/v25-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('One saved speed controls scheduler and message durations, including legacy migration',()=&gt;{for(const speed of [500,1000,2000,3000]){const s=createCampaign(scenarios[0],[]);s.speed=speed;s.settings.messageSpeed=1000;const v=validateSave(s,scenarios,terrains);;;;}}) | assert.equal(v.speed,speed)<br>assert.equal(messageDuration(v),speed)<br>assert.equal(v.settings.messageSpeed,speed) |

Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Horse and writings need no gold, consume only the appropriate resource and use governor action](../tests/v26-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Horse and writings need no gold, consume only the appropriate resource and use governor action',()=&gt;{for(const kind of ['rewardHorse','rewardWritings']){const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);p.gold=0;p.horses=2;g.ruler().advisor=33;g.officer(33).int=95;g.officer(34).int=60;g.officer(34).loyalty=50;;g.execute(kind,{province:9,officer:34});;;if(kind==='rewardHorse'){;;;}else{;;;}validateSave(structuredClone(g.s),scenarios,terrains);}}) | assert.equal(orderUnavailable(g,p,kind==='rewardHorse'?'reward:horse':'reward:writings'),null)<br>assert.equal(p.gold,0)<br>assert(g.officer(p.governor).acted)<br>assert.equal(p.horses,1)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(34).loyalty,50+Math.floor(g.officer(0).charm*100/400))<br>assert(!g.officer(34).hasHorse)<br>assert.equal(p.horses,2)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(34).int,61)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(34).loyalty,50) |
| 2. [Native small gold accepts both random outcomes, deducts amount once and caps loyalty](../tests/v26-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Native small gold accepts both random outcomes, deducts amount once and caps loyalty',()=&gt;{for(const rng of [0,.999]){const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);g.random=()=&gt;rng;g.officer(0).charm=80;g.officer(34).loyalty=50;const gold=p.gold;g.execute('rewardGold',{province:9,officer:34,amount:1});;;}const g=fresh();g.officer(34).loyalty=99;g.execute('rewardHorse',{province:9,officer:34});;}) | assert.equal(p.gold,gold-1)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(34).loyalty,rng?51:50)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(34).loyalty,100) |
| 3. [Reward failure is atomic and study requires a two-point INT gap](../tests/v26-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Reward failure is atomic and study requires a two-point INT gap',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.ruler().advisor=33;g.officer(33).int=81;g.officer(34).int=80;const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('rewardWritings',{province:9,officer:34}),/two more/)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before) |
| 4. [Hire capacity is independent of actor attributes and rounds native partial army room upward](../tests/v26-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Hire capacity is independent of actor attributes and rounds native partial army room upward',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),before=hireCapacity(g,p);for(const id of p.officers)Object.assign(g.officer(id),{war:1,int:1,charm:1});;for(const id of p.officers)g.officer(id).soldiers=10000;g.officer(34).soldiers=9999;p.gold=30000;p.food=3000000;p.population=3000000;;}) | assert.equal(hireCapacity(g,p),before)<br>assert.equal(hireCapacity(g,p),1) |
| 5. [Move estimate includes all destination generals and troops through the July payment](../tests/v26-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Move estimate includes all destination generals and troops through the July payment',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),dest=g.province(17);dest.officers=[];dest.gold=0;dest.food=0;g.s.month=6;let estimate=moveForecast(g,p,dest,[34]);;;;g.s.month=7;;dest.gold=10000;dest.food=3000000;estimate=moveForecast(g,p,dest,[34]);;;}) | assert.equal(estimate.payroll,3+Math.floor(g.officer(34).soldiers/200))<br>assert.equal(estimate.months,1)<br>assert.equal(estimate.food,Math.floor(g.officer(34).soldiers/3))<br>assert.equal(moveForecast(g,p,dest,[34]).months,12)<br>assert.equal(estimate.goldNeeded,0)<br>assert.equal(estimate.foodNeeded,0) |
| 6. [Move team advice obeys the saved monthly gate and reflects destination condition](../tests/v26-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Move team advice obeys the saved monthly gate and reflects destination condition',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),dest=g.province(17);g.ruler().advisor=33;g.officer(33).int=100;g.s.monthlyAdvice.intelligence[33]=100;dest.land=10;dest.flood=10;dest.loyalty=10;;g.s.monthlyAdvice.rolls[0].move=.9;g.s.monthlyAdvice.intelligence[33]=80;;;}) | assert(moveTeamInsight(g,p,dest,[34]).includes('needs attention'))<br>assert(moveTeamInsight(g,p,dest,[34]).includes('more info this month'))<br>assert.equal(moveTeamInsight(g,p,dest,[34]),moveTeamInsight(g,p,dest,[34])) |
| 7. [Ruler ID zero is governor after self appointment, normalization and reload](../tests/v26-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Ruler ID zero is governor after self appointment, normalization and reload',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.execute('appointRealm',{province:9,target:9,officer:0,role:'governor'});;g.normalize();g.s=validateSave(structuredClone(g.s),scenarios,terrains);;}) | assert.equal(g.province(9).governor,0)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(g.province(9).governor).name,'Cao Cao') |

## tests/v27-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v27-tests.mjs](../tests/v27-tests.mjs). Purpose: A war has one dated summary even after hundreds of tactical messages; Extended January is replaced by the current February result for the same war; Legacy refused captive can join; final disposition updates once and survives load; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/event-chronicle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [A war has one dated summary even after hundreds of tactical messages](../tests/v27-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('A war has one dated summary even after hundreds of tactical messages',()=&gt;{const g=battle(),row=g.s.warChronicle[0];for(let i=0;i&lt;250;i++)g.record(&#96;Day ${i%30+1}: Defending army’s orders.&#96;,'war');;;;;}) | assert.equal(g.s.log.length,160)<br>assert.equal(acrossChinaChronology(g).filter(e=&gt;e.text.includes('attacked')).length,1)<br>assert(warReportText(g.s,row).includes('Jan 189 AD: Cao Cao #9 attacked Kong Rong #8. In progress.'))<br>assert.equal(beginWarReport(g,g.s.battle),row) |
| 2. [Extended January is replaced by the current February result for the same war](../tests/v27-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Extended January is replaced by the current February result for the same war',()=&gt;{const g=battle(),b=g.s.battle;extendWarReport(g,b);g.s.month=2;beginWarReport(g,b);;;b.outcome={winner:'attack',reason:'Palace occupied'};g.resolveBattle();;;;}) | assert.equal(g.s.warChronicle.length,1)<br>assert.equal(g.s.warChronicle[0].month,2)<br>assert.equal(g.s.warChronicle.length,1)<br>assert.equal(g.s.warChronicle[0].status,'won')<br>assert(acrossChinaChronology(g)[0].text.startsWith('Feb 189 AD:')) |
| 3. [Legacy refused captive can join; final disposition updates once and survives load](../tests/v27-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Legacy refused captive can join; final disposition updates once and survives load',()=&gt;{const g=victory(),row=g.s.warChronicle[0];;g.acknowledgeTriumph();const c=g.s.captiveDecisions.find(c=&gt;c.role!=='Ruler');;c.recruitTried=true;g.random=()=&gt;.999;;;;;const restored=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrain),{},terrain);;}) | assert(row.fates.length)<br>assert(c)<br>assert.equal(g.decideCaptive(c.officer,'recruit'),true)<br>assert.equal(row.fates.filter(f=&gt;f.officer===c.officer).length,1)<br>assert.equal(row.fates.find(f=&gt;f.officer===c.officer).action,'recruit')<br>assert(warReportText(g.s,row).includes('recruited: '+g.officer(c.officer).name))<br>assert.deepEqual(restored.s.warChronicle,g.s.warChronicle) |
| 4. [Recruit, behead and ruler release are distinct, including snapshots after succession](../tests/v27-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Recruit, behead and ruler release are distinct, including snapshots after succession',()=&gt;{const g=victory();g.acknowledgeTriumph();const row=g.s.warChronicle[0],ruler=g.s.captiveDecisions.find(c=&gt;c.role==='Ruler'),others=g.s.captiveDecisions.filter(c=&gt;c.role!=='Ruler');;g.random=()=&gt;0;g.decideCaptive(others[0].officer,'recruit');g.decideCaptive(others[1].officer,'behead');g.decideCaptive(ruler.officer,'free');const text=warReportText(g.s,row);;;;const names=[row.attacker.name,row.defender.name];g.ruler(row.defender.id).name='Changed ruler';;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrain);}) | assert(ruler&amp;&amp;others.length&gt;=2)<br>assert(text.includes('opposing ruler was set free'))<br>assert(text.includes('recruited: '+g.officer(others[0].officer).name))<br>assert(text.includes('beheaded — '+g.officer(others[1].officer).name))<br>assert(names.every(name=&gt;warReportText(g.s,row).includes(name))) |
| 5. [A defeated invasion reports an unharmed returning ruler without pretending they were captured](../tests/v27-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('A defeated invasion reports an unharmed returning ruler without pretending they were captured',()=&gt;{const g=battle();g.s.battle.outcome={winner:'defend',reason:'Invaders withdrew'};g.resolveBattle();const text=warReportText(g.s,g.s.warChronicle[0]);;;;;}) | assert(text.includes('Defeated. The attacking ruler returned unharmed.'))<br>assert(!text.includes('generals were recruited'))<br>assert(!text.includes('none'))<br>assert(!text.includes('Result:')) |
| 6. [Current province list has all 41 live provinces, local events only, and no battle/captive spam](../tests/v27-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Current province list has all 41 live provinces, local events only, and no battle/captive spam',()=&gt;{const g=battle();g.record('Chen Deng moved to 8,5, spending 3 mobility.','war');g.record('Mi Zhu: set free.','prison',{battleOutcome:true,province:17});g.record(g.province(9).name+': cultivated land.');const list=provinceCurrentEvents(g);;;;;g.province(17).owner=0;;}) | assert.equal(list.length,41)<br>assert.equal(list[8].events.length,1)<br>assert(list[8].events[0].text.includes('cultivated'))<br>assert.equal(list[16].events.length,0)<br>assert.equal(provinceCurrentEvents(g)[16].province.owner,0) |
| 7. [Legacy tactical logs are hidden, and old suspended battles migrate only known dates and provinces](../tests/v27-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Legacy tactical logs are hidden, and old suspended battles migrate only known dates and provinces',()=&gt;{const g=battle(),b=g.s.battle;b.suspended=true;g.suspendBattle();delete b.chronicleWar;delete g.s.warChronicle;delete g.s.warSerial;g.s.month=2;const restored=new Game(g.s,{},terrain);;;;g.record('Tao Qian refused recruitment. Decide whether to set them free or behead them.','prison');;}) | assert.equal(restored.s.warChronicle[0].month,2)<br>assert.equal(restored.s.warChronicle[0].status,'ongoing')<br>assert.equal(restored.s.warChronicle[0].source,9)<br>assert(!acrossChinaChronology(g).some(x=&gt;x.text.includes('refused'))) |
| 8. [Malformed history, duplicate outcomes and forged captive links reject during save validation](../tests/v27-tests.mjs#L16-L16) | test('Malformed history, duplicate outcomes and forged captive links reject during save validation',()=&gt;{const g=victory();for(const mutate of [s=&gt;s.warChronicle[0].month=13,s=&gt;s.warChronicle.push(structuredClone(s.warChronicle[0])),s=&gt;s.warChronicle[0].fates.push(structuredClone(s.warChronicle[0].fates[0])),s=&gt;s.captiveDecisions[0].warId=9999]){const s=structuredClone(g.s);mutate(s);;}validateWarHistory(g.s);}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(s,scenarios,terrain),/chronicle/) |

<a id="tests-v28-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v28-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v28-tests.mjs](../tests/v28-tests.mjs). Purpose: Every scheduled historical entry has its own artwork, including legacy cards; Two epidemics retain both province numbers and independent current reports; Treasure awards are national; plunder, alliance refusals and courier reports retain province cards; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`, `../assets/artwork/history-v28.webp`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/monthly-events.mjs`, `../mjs/events.mjs`, `../mjs/event-chronicle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Every scheduled historical entry has its own artwork, including legacy cards](../tests/v28-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Every scheduled historical entry has its own artwork, including legacy cards',()=&gt;{const frames=new Set();for(const [year] of HISTORY){const g=fresh();g.s.year=year;g.s.monthlyState.applied=null;g.s.monthlyState.history=[];const report={details:[],events:[]};runMonthlyEvents(g,report,{initial:true});const e=report.details.find(e=&gt;e.kind==='history');;const f=eventFrame(e);;;frames.add(f.atlas);;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrain);};;}) | assert(e)<br>assert.equal(f.atlas,'history'+year)<br>assert.equal(f.cell,0)<br>assert.deepEqual(eventFrame(presentEvent(g,{kind:'history',title:&#96;Historical chronicle · ${year}&#96;,text:'Legacy'})),f)<br>assert.equal(frames.size,15)<br>assert(fs.statSync(new URL('../assets/artwork/history-v28.webp',import.meta.url)).size&gt;10000) |
| 2. [Two epidemics retain both province numbers and independent current reports](../tests/v28-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Two epidemics retain both province numbers and independent current reports',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),report={details:[],events:[],year:g.s.year,month:g.s.month};for(const id of [6,41])disaster(g,g.province(id),'epidemic',report);g.s.lastReport=report;g.record('A new month begins. '+report.events.join(' '),'month');const cards=provinceCurrentEvents(g);for(const id of [6,41]){const epidemic=cards[id-1].events.filter(e=&gt;e.art==='epidemic');;const e=epidemic[0];;;;;;};}) | assert.equal(epidemic.length,1)<br>assert.equal(e.province,id)<br>assert(!e.text.startsWith('#'))<br>assert(!e.text.includes('瘟疫'))<br>assert(e.text.includes('Impact:'))<br>assert.equal(eventFrame(e).atlas,'omens')<br>assert(!acrossChinaChronology(g).some(e=&gt;e.text.startsWith('A new month'))) |
| 3. [Treasure awards are national; plunder, alliance refusals and courier reports retain province cards](../tests/v28-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Treasure awards are national; plunder, alliance refusals and courier reports retain province cards',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.s.log=[];for(const [text,type]of [['Province 3: Han Fu received Black Lightning; mounted mobility +1.','war'],['Province 19: The army plundered 500 gold and 20000 food. Popular loyalty fell by 20; trust fell by 10.','war'],[g.province(10).name+': Liu Bei refused Li Ru’s alliance proposal.','diplomacy'],['Li Ru departed from Province 10 on horseback.','diplomacy'],['Province 31: The victorious army protected the inhabitants. Gao Pei received Luminous sword; WAR +8.','war'],['Yuan Shang was captured in Province 28. The mission was cancelled.','prison']])g.record(text … (complete input/procedure at linked L11–11) | assert.equal(acrossChinaChronology(g).length,2)<br>assert(acrossChinaChronology(g).every(e=&gt;e.text.includes('received')))<br>assert(list[id-1].events.length)<br>assert.equal(list[2].events.length,0)<br>assert.equal(list[30].events.length,0)<br>assert(list[9].events.some(e=&gt;e.text.includes('as a messenger')))<br>assert(acrossChinaChronology(g).some(e=&gt;e.text.includes('Luminous sword')))<br>assert.equal(provinceCurrentEvents(g)[15].events.length,0) |
| 4. [Dated war messages render exactly one date in both short and full month formats](../tests/v28-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Dated war messages render exactly one date in both short and full month formats',()=&gt;{for(const text of ['Jan 189 AD: Yuan Shu #19 attacked Sun Jian #21. Won.','January 189 AD: Jan 189 AD: Yuan Shu #19 attacked Sun Jian #21. Won.']){const t=datedEventText({year:189,month:1,text});;;}}) | assert.equal((t.match(/189 AD:/g)\|\|[]).length,1)<br>assert(t.startsWith('Jan 189 AD: Yuan Shu')) |
| 5. [A defending ruler without a fighting unit still counts as present; all subordinate roles are generals](../tests/v28-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('A defending ruler without a fighting unit still counts as present; all subordinate roles are generals',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(21),b={source:19,target:21,attacker:g.province(19).owner,defender:p.owner,rulers:{attack:4,defend:2},units:[{id:4,side:'attack'}],outcome:{winner:'attack'}};;const row=beginWarReport(g,b);;finishWarReport(g,b,[],new Map());;row.fates=[{officer:54,name:'Cheng Pu',zh:'程普',formerOwner:row.losingOwner,role:'Governor',action:'free'},{officer:55,name:'Sun Ce',zh:'孫策',formerOwner:row.losingOwner,role:'General',action:'recruit'}];;;row.rulerResult='absent';;}) | assert(p.officers.includes(2))<br>assert(row.rulerPresent.defend)<br>assert(warReportText(g.s,row).includes('opposing ruler returned unharmed'))<br>assert(warReportText(g.s,row).includes('generals were set free — Cheng Pu'))<br>assert(warReportText(g.s,row).includes('generals were recruited: Sun Ce'))<br>assert(!warReportText(g.s,row).includes('not personally present')) |
| 6. [Real Yuan Shu invasion captures or returns Sun Jian rather than reporting him absent](../tests/v28-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Real Yuan Shu invasion captures or returns Sun Jian rather than reporting him absent',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.s.player=4;g.s.humanRulers=[4];g.s.settings.battleView='all';g.random=()=&gt;0;g.invade({province:19,target:21,officers:[4,60],governor:43,food:20000});;;g.s.battle.outcome={winner:'attack',reason:'Palace occupied'};g.resolveBattle();const row=g.s.warChronicle[0];;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrain);}) | assert(g.s.battle)<br>assert(g.s.warChronicle[0].rulerPresent.defend)<br>assert.notEqual(row.rulerResult,'absent')<br>assert(!warReportText(g.s,row).includes('not personally present')) |
| 7. [New messenger departures identify mission, origin and destination without an exile ambiguity](../tests/v28-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('New messenger departures identify mission, origin and destination without an exile ambiguity',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,mode:'alliance',target:3,officer:33});const e=g.s.log[0];;;;;;;}) | assert(e.text.includes('as messengers for alliance proposal'))<br>assert(e.text.includes('#9'))<br>assert(e.text.includes('#'+g.ruler(3).home))<br>assert.equal(e.province,9)<br>assert(!acrossChinaChronology(g).some(x=&gt;x.text===e.text))<br>assert(provinceCurrentEvents(g)[8].events.some(x=&gt;x.text===e.text)) |

<a id="tests-v29-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v29-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v29-tests.mjs](../tests/v29-tests.mjs). Purpose: A non-ruler commander returns unharmed without putting the ruler on two fronts; A province defended by a governor reports that commander, not an absent ruler; Old reports replace unsupported absent-ruler wording without inventing commander identities; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`, `assets/artwork/events.webp`, `-v29.webp`, `assets/artwork/officers-historic-v29.webp`, `assets/artwork/officers-historic-v16.webp`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `@napi-rs/canvas`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/campaign-decisions.mjs`, `../mjs/campaign-fidelity.mjs`, `../mjs/fidelity-orders.mjs`, `../mjs/event-chronicle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [A non-ruler commander returns unharmed without putting the ruler on two fronts](../tests/v29-tests.mjs#L5-L5) | test('A non-ruler commander returns unharmed without putting the ruler on two fronts',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.invade({province:9,target:8,officers:[33,34],food:20000});;;g.s.battle.outcome={winner:'defend',reason:'Invaders withdrew'};g.resolveBattle();const row=g.s.warChronicle[0],text=warReportText(g.s,row);;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrain);}) | assert.equal(g.s.warChronicle[0].commanders.attack.id,33)<br>assert.equal(g.s.warChronicle[0].rulerPresent.attack,false)<br>assert(text.includes('The attacking commander returned unharmed.'))<br>assert(!text.includes('attacking ruler')) |
| 2. [A province defended by a governor reports that commander, not an absent ruler](../tests/v29-tests.mjs#L6-L6) | test('A province defended by a governor reports that commander, not an absent ruler',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),target=g.province(8),lord=g.ruler(target.owner).leader,home=g.s.provinces.find(p=&gt;p.owner===255);target.officers=target.officers.filter(id=&gt;id!==lord);home.owner=target.owner;home.officers=[lord];home.governor=lord;g.ruler(target.owner).home=home.id;target.governor=target.officers[0];g.invade({province:9,target:8,officers:[0,34],governor:33,food:20000});const b=g.s.battle,commander=b.leaders.defend;b.outcome={winner:'attack',reason:'Palace occupied'};const row=finishWarReport(g,b,[],new Map([[commander,{province:home,owner:b.defender}]]));;;;;}) | assert(!row.rulerPresent.defend)<br>assert.equal(row.commanders.defend.id,commander)<br>assert(warReportText(g.s,row).includes('The opposing commander was not captured.'))<br>assert(!warReportText(g.s,row).includes('opposing ruler')) |
| 3. [Old reports replace unsupported absent-ruler wording without inventing commander identities](../tests/v29-tests.mjs#L7-L7) | test('Old reports replace unsupported absent-ruler wording without inventing commander identities',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),row={year:189,month:1,source:11,target:20,attacker:{name:'Dong Zhuo'},defender:{name:'Liu Biao'},status:'defeated',rulerResult:'absent',losingOwner:3,losingRuler:3,fates:[]};;row.status='won';;row.rulerResult='free';row.rulerPresent={attack:true,defend:true};;}) | assert(warReportText(g.s,row).includes('attacking commander returned unharmed'))<br>assert(warReportText(g.s,row).includes('opposing commander was not captured'))<br>assert(warReportText(g.s,row).includes('opposing ruler was set free')) |
| 4. [Rice shortage and unpaid wages are local, numbered, independently deduplicated province reports](../tests/v29-tests.mjs#L8-L8) | test('Rice shortage and unpaid wages are local, numbered, independently deduplicated province reports',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.s.provinces.find(p=&gt;p.name==='Jiangxia');g.s.month=3;g.s.lastReport={year:189,month:3,details:[{kind:'month',text:&#96;Rice shortage in ${p.name}. Population and garrison declined.&#96;,province:null},{kind:'month',text:&#96;${p.name} cannot cover wages; officer loyalty fell.&#96;,province:null}]};for(const e of g.s.lastReport.details)g.record(e.text,'month');;;;}) | assert.equal(provinceCurrentEvents(g)[p.id-1].events.length,2)<br>assert(provinceCurrentEvents(g)[p.id-1].events.every(e=&gt;e.province===p.id&amp;&amp;!e.text.startsWith('#')))<br>assert(!acrossChinaChronology(g).some(e=&gt;/shortage\|cannot cover wages/.test(e.text))) |
| 5. [All live event atlas cells exactly match events.webp dimensions and aspect](../tests/v29-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('All live event atlas cells exactly match events.webp dimensions and aspect',async()=&gt;{const base=await loadImage(new URL('assets/artwork/events.webp',root).pathname);for(const [key,n]of [['disasters',2],['omens',2],['stories',2],['history',4]]){const im=await loadImage(new URL('assets/artwork/'+key+'-v29.webp',root).pathname);;;}const portrait=await loadImage(new URL('assets/artwork/officers-historic-v29.webp',root).pathname),old=await loadImage(new URL('assets/artwork/officers-historic-v16.webp',root).pathname);;;;}) | assert.equal(im.width/n,base.width/2)<br>assert.equal(im.height/n,base.height/2)<br>assert.equal(portrait.width,old.width)<br>assert.equal(portrait.height,old.height)<br>assert.notDeepEqual(fs.readFileSync(new URL('assets/artwork/officers-historic-v29.webp',root)),fs.readFileSync(new URL('assets/artwork/officers-historic-v16.webp',root))) |
| 6. [Guan Yu and Zhang Fei join as ordinary captives; loyalty/family protection applies to serving recruitment only](../tests/v29-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Guan Yu and Zhang Fei join as ordinary captives; loyalty/family protection applies to serving recruitment only',()=&gt;{for(const name of ['Guan Yu','Zhang Fei']){const g=fresh(),o=g.s.officers.find(o=&gt;o.name===name);;;takeCaptive(g,o.id,0,9,1,'battle','General');;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrain);}}) | assert.equal(o.loyalty,100)<br>assert.equal(recruitmentProtection(g.s,o),'absolute loyalty')<br>assert.equal(g.decideCaptive(o.id,'recruit'),true)<br>assert.equal(o.owner,0)<br>assert.equal(o.prisonerOf,undefined) |
| 7. [Every hostile recruit method honours zero, including large gold bonuses; free recruitment remains separate](../tests/v29-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Every hostile recruit method honours zero, including large gold bonuses; free recruitment remains separate',()=&gt;{for(const method of ['attention','horse','letter','gold']){const g=fresh(),o=g.s.officers.find(o=&gt;o.name==='Zhang Fei'),p=g.province(9);p.horses=5;p.gold=30000;;fidelityOrder(g,'recruitMethod',{province:9,officer:33,target:o.id,method,amount:30000});;g.officer(33).acted=false;o.loyalty=99;;}const g=fresh(),o=g.s.officers.find(o=&gt;o.name==='Guan Yu');o.owner=255;;}) | assert.equal(recruitMethodChance(g,p,g.officer(33),o,method),0)<br>assert.equal(o.owner,1)<br>assert(recruitMethodChance(g,p,g.officer(33),o,method)&gt;0)<br>assert.equal(recruitmentProtection(g.s,o),null) |
| 8. [Shared blood blocks recruitment at low loyalty; native immunity is attribute-based](../tests/v29-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Shared blood blocks recruitment at low loyalty; native immunity is attribute-based',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),o=g.s.officers.find(o=&gt;o.name==='Zhang Fei');o.loyalty=20;o.blood=g.officer(g.ruler(1).leader).blood;;o.blood=0;;}) | assert.equal(recruitmentProtection(g.s,o),'shared family allegiance')<br>assert.equal(recruitmentProtection(g.s,o),null) |
| 9. [Battlefield bribes cannot switch a fully loyal subordinate on a perfect roll](../tests/v29-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Battlefield bribes cannot switch a fully loyal subordinate on a perfect roll',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),target=8;g.invade({province:9,target,officers:[0,34],governor:33,food:20000,gold:1000});while(g.s.battle.phase==='deployment')aiBattle(g,60);const b=g.s.battle,u=b.units.find(u=&gt;u.id===0),v=b.units.find(u=&gt;u.side==='defend'&amp;&amp;u.id!==b.rulers.defend);;g.officer(v.id).loyalty=100;b.side='attack';const gold=b.gold.attack;g.battleAction('bribe',{unit:u.id,target:v.id,gold:100});;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrain);}) | assert(v)<br>assert.equal(v.side,'defend')<br>assert.equal(b.gold.attack,gold-100) |
| 10. [Actual monthly shortages retain province IDs in council playback and after save/load](../tests/v29-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Actual monthly shortages retain province IDs in council playback and after save/load',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);g.s.month=2;p.gold=0;p.food=0;g.s.completed=g.s.rulers.map(r=&gt;r.id).filter(id=&gt;id!==0);g.finishFactionTurn();const local=g.s.lastReport.details.filter(e=&gt;e.province===p.id&amp;&amp;/Rice shortage\|cannot cover wages/.test(e.text));;;const loaded=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrain),{},terrain);;;}) | assert(local.length&gt;=2)<br>assert(local.every(e=&gt;e.province===p.id))<br>assert(!acrossChinaChronology(loaded).some(e=&gt;/Rice shortage\|cannot cover wages/.test(e.text)))<br>assert(provinceCurrentEvents(loaded)[8].events.some(e=&gt;e.text.includes('Rice shortage'))) |

<a id="tests-v30-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v30-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v30-tests.mjs](../tests/v30-tests.mjs). Purpose: Typhoon reports actual land/control losses and zero resource casualties using native coefficients; Native flood retains stores, scales casualties with control and reports exact six-field deltas; Native locust changes food and land without inventing gold, population or soldier losses; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`, `../assets/artwork/${name}-v30.webp`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `@napi-rs/canvas`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/monthly-events.mjs`, `../mjs/event-impact.mjs`, `../mjs/events.mjs`, `../mjs/event-chronicle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Typhoon reports actual land/control losses and zero resource casualties using native coefficients](../tests/v30-tests.mjs#L5-L5) | test('Typhoon reports actual land/control losses and zero resource casualties using native coefficients',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(28);Object.assign(p,{population:100000,food:30000,gold:1000,land:80,flood:40,loyalty:80});const before=provinceSnapshot(g,p),r=report(g);disaster(g,p,'typhoon',r);;;;;for(const k of ['people','soldiers','food','gold']);for(const k of IMPACT_KEYS);;}) | assert.equal(p.land,58)<br>assert.equal(p.flood,24)<br>assert.equal(p.loyalty,72)<br>assert.deepEqual(r.details[0].impact,provinceImpact(g,p,before))<br>assert.equal(r.details[0].impact[k],0)<br>assert(r.details[0].text.includes(k==='flood'?'flood control':k))<br>assert(!/[#\u3400-\u9fff]/.test(r.details[0].text)) |
| 2. [Native flood retains stores, scales casualties with control and reports exact six-field deltas](../tests/v30-tests.mjs#L6-L6) | test('Native flood retains stores, scales casualties with control and reports exact six-field deltas',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(8);Object.assign(p,{population:100000,food:30000,gold:1000,land:80,flood:40,loyalty:80});for(const id of p.officers)g.officer(id).soldiers=1000;const before=provinceSnapshot(g,p),r=report(g);disaster(g,p,'flood',r);;;;;;;;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert.equal(p.population,83100)<br>assert.equal(p.land,52)<br>assert.equal(p.flood,30)<br>assert.equal(p.loyalty,58)<br>assert(p.officers.every(id=&gt;g.officer(id).soldiers===800))<br>assert.deepEqual(r.details[0].impact,provinceImpact(g,p,before))<br>assert.equal(p.food,30000)<br>assert.equal(p.gold,1000)<br>assert.equal(p.ricePrice,40) |
| 3. [Native locust changes food and land without inventing gold, population or soldier losses](../tests/v30-tests.mjs#L7-L7) | test('Native locust changes food and land without inventing gold, population or soldier losses',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),before=provinceSnapshot(g,p),r=report(g);disaster(g,p,'locust',r);;;;;;}) | assert.equal(p.food,Math.floor(before.food/2))<br>assert.equal(p.land,Math.floor(before.land*.5))<br>assert.equal(p.gold,before.gold)<br>assert.equal(p.population,before.people)<br>assert.equal(r.details[0].impact.soldiers,0) |
| 4. [Illness onset and recovery are separate local events, preserved through save/load](../tests/v30-tests.mjs#L8-L8) | test('Illness onset and recovery are separate local events, preserved through save/load',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);g.random=()=&gt;.25;for(const id of p.officers){g.officer(id).sick=false;g.officer(id).injured=false;}const r=report(g);disaster(g,p,'epidemic',r);const illnesses=r.details.filter(e=&gt;e.title.startsWith('Illness'));;;g.s.lastReport=r;const loaded=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains),{},terrains);;;loaded.random=()=&gt;.99;loaded.s.month=2;const next=report(loaded);runMonthlyEvents(loaded,next);;validateSave(loaded.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert.equal(illnesses.length,p.officers.length)<br>assert(illnesses.every(e=&gt;e.province===9&amp;&amp;e.text.includes('fell ill')&amp;&amp;e.text.includes('1 month')))<br>assert.equal(provinceCurrentEvents(loaded)[8].events.filter(e=&gt;e.text.includes('fell ill')).length,p.officers.length)<br>assert(!acrossChinaChronology(loaded).some(e=&gt;e.text.includes('fell ill')))<br>assert(next.details.some(e=&gt;e.province===9&amp;&amp;e.text.includes('recovered'))) |
| 5. [Good local events explicitly report unchanged resources and legacy messages lose duplicate prefixes](../tests/v30-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Good local events explicitly report unchanged resources and legacy messages lose duplicate prefixes',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),r=report(g);;;;const e=presentEvent(g,{kind:'month',province:8,title:'#8 · 房子泡湯了 · Flood',text:'#8 · 房子泡湯了 · Flood struck Linzi. 3,062 people lost.'});;;}) | assert(triggerEvent(g,'medical',r,{officer:34}))<br>assert.deepEqual(r.details[0].impact,Object.fromEntries(IMPACT_KEYS.map(k=&gt;[k,0])))<br>assert(r.details[0].text.includes('food 0 unchanged'))<br>assert.equal(e.title,'Flood')<br>assert.equal(e.text,'Flood struck Linzi. 3,062 people lost.') |
| 6. [Native uprising population displacement is reported as a gain in its connected destination](../tests/v30-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Native uprising population displacement is reported as a gain in its connected destination',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),r=report(g);const before=provinceSnapshot(g,p);disaster(g,p,'uprising',r);;const arrival=r.details.find(e=&gt;e.title.startsWith('Refugees'));;;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert.equal(p.population,(Math.floor(Math.floor(before.people/100)*.6)+1)*100)<br>assert(arrival)<br>assert(p.neighbors.includes(arrival.province))<br>assert.equal(arrival.impact.people,-r.details[0].impact.people)<br>assert(arrival.text.includes('gained')) |
| 7. [Battle injuries record their onset at the final province without replacing the victory announcement](../tests/v30-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Battle injuries record their onset at the final province without replacing the victory announcement',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.s.settings.battleView='all';g.invade({province:9,target:8,officers:[0,34],governor:33,food:20000});const b=g.s.battle,u=b.units.find(u=&gt;u.id===34);u.soldiers=Math.floor(u.startSoldiers/3);b.outcome={winner:'attack',reason:'Test victory'};g.resolveBattle();;const injury=g.s.log.find(e=&gt;e.event?.officer===34&amp;&amp;e.text.includes('was injured'));;;;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert(g.officer(34).injured)<br>assert(injury)<br>assert.equal(injury.province,8)<br>assert(injury.text.includes('3 months'))<br>assert(!g.s.lastEvent.text.includes('was injured'))<br>assert(provinceCurrentEvents(g)[7].events.some(e=&gt;e.text.includes('was injured'))) |
| 8. [New event ledgers reject malformed deltas without rejecting legacy reports](../tests/v30-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('New event ledgers reject malformed deltas without rejecting legacy reports',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),r=report(g);disaster(g,g.province(8),'flood',r);g.s.lastReport=r;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);const bad=structuredClone(g.s);bad.lastReport.details[0].impact.people='unknown';;delete bad.lastReport.details[0].impact;validateSave(bad,scenarios,terrains);}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;validateSave(bad,scenarios,terrains)) |
| 9. [Every replacement event cell fills its frame instead of containing a flat matte strip](../tests/v30-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Every replacement event cell fills its frame instead of containing a flat matte strip',async()=&gt;{for(const [name,n]of [['history',4],['disasters',2],['omens',2],['stories',2]]){const image=await loadImage(new URL(&#96;../assets/artwork/${name}-v30.webp&#96;,import.meta.url).pathname);;;const c=createCanvas(100*n,40*n),ctx=c.getContext('2d');ctx.drawImage(image,0,0,c.width,c.height);for(let row=0;row&lt;n;row++)for(let col=0;col&lt;n;col++)for(const side of [0,99]){const d=ctx.getImageData(col*100+side,row*40+3,1,34).data,colors=new Set();for(let i=0;i&lt;d.length;i+=4)colors.add(&#96;${d[i]&gt;&gt;3},${d[i+1]&gt;&gt;3},${d[i+2]&gt;&gt;3}&#96;);;}}}) | assert.equal(image.width/n,991.5)<br>assert.equal(image.height/n,396.5)<br>assert(colors.size&gt;5,&#96;${name} ${col},${row} has a flat side strip&#96;) |

<a id="tests-v31-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v31-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v31-tests.mjs](../tests/v31-tests.mjs). Purpose: Execution with surviving realm installs a blood-related successor and hostility100 only toward killer; Human successor choice keeps vengeance at100 and persists the pending council; No heir execution emits exactly one clan event, while freeing dispossessed ruler permits exile; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`, `-v31.webp`, `config/release.json`, `assets/artwork/omens-v30.webp`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `node:crypto`, `@napi-rs/canvas`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/campaign-decisions.mjs`, `../mjs/campaign-completion.mjs`, `../mjs/monthly-events.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-battle.mjs`, `../mjs/battle-summary.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/events.mjs`, `../mjs/event-chronicle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Execution with surviving realm installs a blood-related successor and hostility100 only toward killer](../tests/v31-tests.mjs#L4-L4) | test('Execution with surviving realm installs a blood-related successor and hostility100 only toward killer',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),r=g.ruler(1),old=r.leader,home=g.province(r.home),candidate=home.officers.find(id=&gt;id!==old);;g.officer(candidate).blood=g.officer(old).blood\|\|1;g.officer(old).blood=g.officer(candidate).blood;takeCaptive(g,old,0,9,1,'battle','Ruler');g.decideCaptive(old,'behead');;;;const event=g.s.log.find(x=&gt;x.event?.art==='heir');;;;valid(g);}) | assert(candidate!==undefined)<br>assert(g.officer(old).dead)<br>assert.equal(r.leader,candidate)<br>assert.equal(r.relations[0],100)<br>assert(event)<br>assert.equal(eventFrame(event.event).atlas,'heir')<br>assert(acrossChinaChronology(g).some(x=&gt;x.text===event.text)) |
| 2. [Human successor choice keeps vengeance at100 and persists the pending council](../tests/v31-tests.mjs#L5-L5) | test('Human successor choice keeps vengeance at100 and persists the pending council',()=&gt;{const g=fresh([0,1]),r=g.ruler(1),old=r.leader;takeCaptive(g,old,0,9,1);g.decideCaptive(old,'behead');;const pendingState=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;const s=valid(g),loaded=new Game(s,{},terrains),x=loaded.s.successorDecisions.find(x=&gt;x.owner===1);loaded.chooseSuccessor(1,x.candidates.at(-1));;;valid(loaded);}) | assert(g.s.successorDecisions.some(x=&gt;x.owner===1))<br>assert.equal(g.aiStep(),false)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),pendingState)<br>assert.equal(loaded.ruler(1).relations[0],100)<br>assert.equal(loaded.s.successorDecisions.length,0) |
| 3. [No heir execution emits exactly one clan event, while freeing dispossessed ruler permits exile](../tests/v31-tests.mjs#L6-L6) | test('No heir execution emits exactly one clan event, while freeing dispossessed ruler permits exile',()=&gt;{for(const action of ['behead','free']){const g=fresh(),r=g.ruler(1),old=r.leader;for(const p of g.s.provinces.filter(p=&gt;p.owner===1)){for(const id of p.officers){g.officer(id).owner=255;p.unclaimed.push(id);}p.officers=[];p.owner=255;p.governor=null;}takeCaptive(g,old,0,9,1);g.decideCaptive(old,action);g.reconcileControl();g.reconcileControl();const events=g.s.log.filter(x=&gt;x.event?.art==='clan');;if(action==='free');valid(g);}}) | assert.equal(events.length,action==='behead'?1:0)<br>assert(g.s.roaming.some(x=&gt;x.owner===1)) |
| 4. [Diplomatic surrender retires realm and reports clan destruction; natural succession does not invent a killer](../tests/v31-tests.mjs#L7-L7) | test('Diplomatic surrender retires realm and reports clan destruction; natural succession does not invent a killer',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),r=g.ruler(1);surrenderRealm(g,r,g.officer(0));g.normalize();;;valid(g);const h=fresh(),rr=h.ruler(1),relations=structuredClone(rr.relations),report={events:[],details:[]};;;;h.normalize();valid(h);}) | assert.equal(r.extinct,true)<br>assert(g.s.log.some(x=&gt;x.event?.art==='clan'))<br>assert(officerDeath(h,rr.leader,report))<br>assert.deepEqual(rr.relations,relations)<br>assert(report.details.some(x=&gt;x.title==='A new ruler')) |
| 5. [Human incoming alliance and marriage always pause, save and obey choice rather than rerolling](../tests/v31-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Human incoming alliance and marriage always pause, save and obey choice rather than rerolling',async()=&gt;{for(const mode of ['alliance','marriage'])for(const accept of [false,true]){const g=await proposal(mode,accept);;valid(g);}}) | assert.equal(mode==='alliance'?g.ruler(0).alliances.includes(1):g.ruler(0).daughterGivenTo===1,accept) |
| 6. [Human joint invasion pauses before assent and then requires a separate force commitment](../tests/v31-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Human joint invasion pauses before assent and then requires a separate force commitment',async()=&gt;{const declined=await proposal('joint',false);;;valid(declined);const accepted=await proposal('joint',true);;;;valid(accepted);accepted.answerAlly(false);;;valid(accepted);}) | assert.equal(declined.s.allyDecisions.length,0)<br>assert.equal(declined.s.jointPlans.length,0)<br>assert.equal(accepted.s.allyDecisions.length,1)<br>assert.equal(accepted.s.allyDecisions[0].owner,1)<br>assert.equal(accepted.s.jointPlans.length,0)<br>assert.equal(accepted.s.allyDecisions.length,0)<br>assert.equal(accepted.s.jointPlans.length,0) |
| 7. [An actual battlefield death announces extinction or surviving succession without treating army depletion as death](../tests/v31-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('An actual battlefield death announces extinction or surviving succession without treating army depletion as death',()=&gt;{for(const survives of [false,true]){const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),target=g.province(8),owner=target.owner,r=g.ruler(owner),old=r.leader;if(survives){const q=g.province(16);q.owner=owner;for(const id of q.officers)g.officer(id).owner=owner;q.governor=q.officers[0];}g.normalize();const b=spawnBattle(g,p,target,[0,34],20000,terrains[7]);b.gold={attack:0,defend:0};b.outcome={winner:'attack',reason:'The opposing commander was killed.'};g.s.battle=b;const leader=b.units.find(u=&gt;u.id===old);;leader.killed=true;leader.soldiers=0;g.resolveBattle();;;;;if(survives){;;}else ;v … (complete input/procedure at linked L14–14) | assert(leader)<br>assert(g.officer(old).dead)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(old).deathReason,'battle')<br>assert(!g.s.captiveDecisions.some(x=&gt;x.officer===old))<br>assert(g.s.log.some(x=&gt;x.event?.art===(survives?'heir':'clan')))<br>assert.notEqual(r.leader,old)<br>assert.equal(r.relations[0],100)<br>assert(r.extinct) |
| 8. [Human surrender demand requires a response; spectator alliances auto-resolve](../tests/v31-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Human surrender demand requires a response; spectator alliances auto-resolve',async()=&gt;{const g=await proposal('threat',false);;;const h=fresh([]),p=h.province(9);h.random=()=&gt;0;h.execute('diplomaticMission',{province:9,officer:33,target:1,mode:'alliance'});let n=0;while(h.s.journey){if(h.s.journey.interception)h.interceptJourney('free');else h.advanceJourney();;;};valid(h);}) | assert(g.s.provinces.some(p=&gt;p.owner===1))<br>assert(!g.ruler(1).extinct)<br>assert(!h.s.journey?.proposal)<br>assert(++n&lt;100)<br>assert(h.ruler(0).alliances.includes(1)) |
| 9. ## tests/v32-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v32-tests.mjs](../tests/v32-tests.mjs). Purpose: All 1300 isolated native vectors agree with production domestic, power and casualty arithmetic; All 186 directed approaches in 41 provinces have exactly their native five cells; defenders use twenty; Water attacking palace pays native assault penalty; counterattack uses intact pre-exchange force; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`, `docs/native-equation-vectors-v32.json`, `docs/deployment-v32.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/native-combat.mjs`, `../mjs/original-deployment.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/geography.mjs`, `../mjs/event-chronicle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [All 1300 isolated native vectors agree with production domestic, power and casualty arithmetic](../tests/v32-tests.mjs#L11-L17) | test('All 1300 isolated native vectors agree with production domestic, power and casualty arithmetic',()=&gt;{ const vectors=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('docs/native-equation-vectors-v32.json')),g=fresh(); for(const v of vectors.development){g.s.difficulty=v.difficulty;for(const field of ['land','flood']);} for(const v of vectors.power); for(const v of vectors.casualties); ; }) | assert.equal(g.development({[field]:v.value},v,v.gold,field),v.gain)<br>assert.equal(combatPower(v),v.power)<br>assert.equal(nativeCasualty(v.ownPower,v.otherPower,v.otherMen,v.palaceAssault?7:v.terrain,v.factor,v.random300,v.random30),v.loss)<br>assert.equal(vectors.development.length+vectors.power.length+vectors.casualties.length,1300) |
| 2. [All 186 directed approaches in 41 provinces have exactly their native five cells; defenders use twenty](../tests/v32-tests.mjs#L18-L21) | test('All 186 directed approaches in 41 provinces have exactly their native five cells; defenders use twenty',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh(),audit=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('docs/deployment-v32.json'));; for(const p of g.s.provinces){;for(const source of p.neighbors){const entry=provinceDirection(p,g.province(source)),b={target:p.id,terrain:terrains[p.id-1],fire:{},units:[],entryDirection:entry};const actual=placementCells(b,'attack').map(c=&gt;[c.q,c.r]);;;;}} }) | assert.equal(audit.approaches.length,186)<br>assert.equal(DEFENDER_ZONES[p.id-1].length,20)<br>assert.deepEqual(actual,ATTACKER_ZONES[p.id-1][entry])<br>assert.deepEqual(actual,audit.approaches.find(a=&gt;a.target===p.id&amp;&amp;a.source===source).slots)<br>assert.equal(placementCells(b,'defend').length,20) |
| 3. [Water attacking palace pays native assault penalty; counterattack uses intact pre-exchange force](../tests/v32-tests.mjs#L23-L27) | test('Water attacking palace pays native assault penalty; counterattack uses intact pre-exchange force',()=&gt;{ const a=unit('attack'),d=unit('defend');const hit=meleeExchange(a,d,4,6,()=&gt;.5); ; const land=meleeExchange(a,d,0,0,()=&gt;.5);;;;; }) | assert.deepEqual(hit,{loss:75,reply:300,attackerWeapons:300,defenderWeapons:75})<br>assert.equal(land.loss,150)<br>assert.equal(land.reply,150)<br>assert.equal(a.soldiers,5000)<br>assert.equal(d.soldiers,5000) |
| 4. [A native single exchange can wipe 1000 weak men in water without a fixed 1000-man minimum](../tests/v32-tests.mjs#L28-L32) | test('A native single exchange can wipe 1000 weak men in water without a fixed 1000-man minimum',()=&gt;{ const strong={...unit('attack'),war:100,combatWarBonus:20},weak={...unit('defend',1000),war:0,training:0,weapons:0}; ; ; }) | assert.equal(meleeExchange(strong,weak,0,4,()=&gt;.999).loss,1000)<br>assert.equal(meleeExchange(unit('attack'),unit('defend'),0,0,()=&gt;0).loss,1) |
| 5. [Equipment coverage and casualties update both armies; legacy morale alone cannot end a battle](../tests/v32-tests.mjs#L33-L38) | test('Equipment coverage and casualties update both armies; legacy morale alone cannot end a battle',()=&gt;{ ; const b={units:[unit('attack'),unit('defend')],phase:'battle',food:{attack:1000,defend:1000},leaders:{attack:0,defend:1},rulers:{attack:0,defend:1},terrain:Array(156).fill(0),palace:{q:12,r:11}}; ;; b.food.defend=0;;;; }) | assert.equal(equipmentPercent({soldiers:1000,weapons:300}),30)<br>assert.equal(outcome(b),null)<br>assert.equal(living(b).length,2)<br>assert.equal(outcome(b).winner,'attack')<br>assert.match(b.outcome.reason,/defending.*food/)<br>assert.equal(b.units[1].soldiers,5000) |
| 6. [Cultiv and Flood use the same native gain, spend gold and saturate at100](../tests/v32-tests.mjs#L39-L41) | test('Cultiv and Flood use the same native gain, spend gold and saturate at100',()=&gt;{ for(const type of ['develop','flood']){const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),o=g.officer(34),field=type==='develop'?'land':'flood';p[field]=99;p.gold=1000;Object.assign(o,{acted:false,int:100,charm:100});const gain=g.preview(type,9,o.id,100);g.execute(type,{province:9,officer:o.id,amount:100});;;;;} }) | assert.equal(p[field],99+gain)<br>assert.equal(p.gold,900)<br>assert(o.acted)<br>assert.equal(g.development({[field]:100},o,100,field),0) |
| 7. [All-battles spectator outcomes survive tactical-log eviction and save/load with a retained internal reason](../tests/v32-tests.mjs#L42-L44) | test('All-battles spectator outcomes survive tactical-log eviction and save/load with a retained internal reason',()=&gt;{ for(const winner of ['attack','defend']){const g=fresh([]);g.invade({province:9,target:8,officers:[0,34],governor:33,food:20000});const b=g.s.battle;;while(b.phase==='deployment')aiBattle(g);b.outcome={winner,reason:winner==='attack'?'The defending army exhausted its food.':'The attacking commander fled.'};g.resolveBattle();for(let i=0;i&lt;200;i++)g.record(&#96;Day ${i%30+1}: Defending army’s orders.&#96;,'war');const rows=acrossChinaChronology(g).filter(x=&gt;x.text.includes('attacked'));;;;;;const restored=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains),{},g.terrains);;} }) | assert(g.battleDetailed())<br>assert.equal(rows.length,1)<br>assert(rows[0].text.includes(winner==='attack'?'Won.':'Defeated.'))<br>assert(!rows[0].text.includes('Result:'))<br>assert.equal(g.s.warChronicle.find(x=&gt;x.war===b.chronicleWar).reason,b.outcome.reason)<br>assert.equal(g.s.lastBattle.reason,b.outcome.reason)<br>assert(acrossChinaChronology(restored).some(x=&gt;x.text===rows[0].text)) |
| 8. [An invading main contingent at five cannot pull extra units from its source or a friendly neighbor](../tests/v32-tests.mjs#L45-L47) | test('An invading main contingent at five cannot pull extra units from its source or a friendly neighbor',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);while(p.officers.length&lt;7){const q=g.s.provinces.find(q=&gt;q.id!==9&amp;&amp;q.officers.length&gt;1),id=q.officers.find(id=&gt;id!==q.governor);q.officers=q.officers.filter(x=&gt;x!==id);p.officers.push(id);Object.assign(g.officer(id),{owner:p.owner,soldiers:1000,acted:false});}g.normalize();g.invade({province:9,target:8,officers:p.officers.filter(id=&gt;id!==p.governor).slice(0,5),food:20000});while(g.s.battle.phase==='deployment')aiBattle(g);;; }) | assert.equal(reinforcementOptions(g,'attack').length,0)<br>assert.equal(living(g.s.battle).filter(u=&gt;u.side==='attack').length,5) |
| 9. [Old broad-zone deployment and unsupported morale routes migrate without altering a fighting army’s coordinates](../tests/v32-tests.mjs#L48-L50) | test('Old broad-zone deployment and unsupported morale routes migrate without altering a fighting army’s coordinates',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh();g.invade({province:9,target:8,officers:[0,34],governor:33,food:20000});while(g.s.battle.phase==='deployment')aiBattle(g);const raw=structuredClone(g.s),u=raw.battle.units[0];raw.battle.rulesVersion=5;u.morale=0;u.routed=true;const position=[u.q,u.r],restored=validateSave(raw,scenarios,g.terrains);;;; }) | assert.equal(restored.battle.rulesVersion,6)<br>assert.equal(restored.battle.units[0].routed,false)<br>assert.deepEqual([restored.battle.units[0].q,restored.battle.units[0].r],position) |

Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/native-governance-priority.mjs`, `../mjs/native-combat.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/campaign-decisions.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [812 controlled native vectors agree with ambush, flight, Train, Give and hex trigger geometry](../tests/v33-tests.mjs#L11-L18) | test('812 controlled native vectors agree with ambush, flight, Train, Give and hex trigger geometry',()=&gt;{ for(const v of vectors.ambush){const rolls=[(v.random300+.1)/300,(v.random30+.1)/30];;} for(const v of vectors.flight){;;} for(const v of vectors.training); for(const v of vectors.give); for(const v of vectors.geometry); ; }) | assert.deepEqual(ambushExchange(v.mover,v.ambusher,v.terrain,()=&gt;rolls.shift(),v.factor),{loss:v.loss,weapons:v.weapons})<br>assert.equal(fleeChance({...v.unit,mobility:v.mobility},v.enemies,v.ruler,v.random10),v.chance)<br>assert.equal(v.unit.hasHorse\|\|v.roll100&lt;v.chance,v.escaped)<br>assert.equal(trainingGain({war:v.war},v.armies),v.gain)<br>assert.equal(giveFoodGain(v.population,v.governorCharm,v.officerCharm,v.food,v.difficulty),v.gain)<br>assert.deepEqual(ambushCells({q:v.fromCell[0],r:v.fromCell[1]},{q:v.toCell[0],r:v.toCell[1]}).map(p=&gt;[p.q,p.r]),v.cells)<br>assert.equal(Object.values(vectors).reduce((n,x)=&gt;n+x.length,0),812) |
| 2. [Native local governance table selects ambition policy and rotates with original retry counts](../tests/v33-tests.mjs#L19-L26) | test('Native local governance table selects ambition policy and rotates with original retry counts',()=&gt;{ ;;; ; ; ; ; const attempts=[];;; }) | assert.equal(nativePolicy(79,()=&gt;{throw Error('Unneeded policy RNG');}),0)<br>assert.equal(nativePolicy(80,()=&gt;0),1)<br>assert.equal(nativePolicy(100,()=&gt;.999),2)<br>assert.deepEqual(NATIVE_POLICIES[0],['domestic','personnel','domestic','military','movement'])<br>assert.deepEqual(NATIVE_GROUPS.domestic,['relief','develop','flood','sellFood','buyHorses'])<br>assert.deepEqual(nativeGroupOrders('military',3,()=&gt;.75),['buyWeapons','trainArmy','hireArmy'])<br>assert.deepEqual(nativeGroupOrders('personnel',3,()=&gt;.5),['search','rewardWritings','reward','recruit'])<br>assert(runNativePriority(1,3,()=&gt;0,x=&gt;{attempts.push(x);return x==='trainArmy';}))<br>assert.deepEqual(attempts,['relief','develop','flood','sellFood','trainArmy']) |
| 3. [Rulers have two captive options; execution announces an heir and 100 hostility toward the killer](../tests/v33-tests.mjs#L27-L29) | test('Rulers have two captive options; execution announces an heir and 100 hostility toward the killer',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh(),r=g.ruler(0),old=r.leader;takeCaptive(g,old,12,8,0,'battle','Ruler');const c=g.s.captiveDecisions[0];;;const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;g.decideCaptive(old,'behead');;;;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains); }) | assert(g.s.provinces.some(p=&gt;p.owner===0))<br>assert.deepEqual(captiveActions(g,c),['free','behead'])<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.decideCaptive(old,'recruit'))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.notEqual(r.leader,old)<br>assert.equal(r.relations[12],100)<br>assert.equal(r.enemy,12)<br>assert(g.s.log.some(e=&gt;e.event?.art==='heir'))<br>assert(g.s.successorDecisions.some(d=&gt;d.owner===0)) |
| 4. [Moving beside a waiting forest defender automatically ambushes; INT 90 evades without consuming RNG](../tests/v33-tests.mjs#L31-L34) | test('Moving beside a waiting forest defender automatically ambushes; INT 90 evades without consuming RNG',()=&gt;{ for(const intelligence of [89,90]){const {g,b,u,v}=arena();u.int=intelligence;v.ordered=true;v.mobility=0;const before=u.soldiers,mp=v.mobility;g.battleAction('move',{unit:u.id,q:6,r:6});;;;;;} let calls=0;const a={soldiers:1000,weapons:1000,war:80,int:90,training:50};;; }) | assert.equal(u.soldiers&lt;before,intelligence===89)<br>assert(b.events.some(t=&gt;t.includes('ambushed')))<br>assert.equal(v.mobility,mp)<br>assert(v.ordered)<br>assert.equal(v.soldiers,5000)<br>assert.equal(ambushExchange(a,a,0,()=&gt;{calls++;return .5;}).loss,0)<br>assert.equal(calls,0) |
| 5. [Zero-men melee victims leave the field immediately and emit a capture status](../tests/v33-tests.mjs#L35-L35) | test('Zero-men melee victims leave the field immediately and emit a capture status',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,v}=arena();Object.assign(v,direction(u.q,u.r,4),{soldiers:1,int:90});g.battleAction('charge',{unit:u.id,target:v.id});;;;;;}) | assert.equal(v.soldiers,0)<br>assert(!living(b).some(x=&gt;x.id===v.id))<br>assert(v.captured)<br>assert(b.messages.some(m=&gt;m.text.includes('army depleted to 0; captured')))<br>assert.deepEqual({q:u.q,r:u.r},{q:v.q,r:v.r}) |

| 7. [Flight restores training mobility, can fail even without contact, and horses guarantee success](../tests/v33-tests.mjs#L37-L37) | test('Flight restores training mobility, can fail even without contact, and horses guarantee success',()=&gt;{for(const mounted of [false,true]){const {g,b}=arena(),u=b.units.find(x=&gt;x.side==='attack'&amp;&amp;x.id!==b.leaders.attack);Object.assign(u,{war:0,training:0,mobility:0,soldiers:100});g.officer(u.id).hasHorse=mounted;g.random=()=&gt;.99;g.battleAction('flee',{unit:u.id,province:b.source});;;;if(!mounted);}}) | assert.equal(u.fled,mounted)<br>assert.equal(u.captured,!mounted)<br>assert(b.messages.some(m=&gt;m.text.includes(mounted?'fled successfully':'tried to flee — captured')))<br>assert.equal(u.mobility,2) |
| 8. [Watched results retain the field, pause AI and survive save/load until acknowledgement](../tests/v33-tests.mjs#L38-L38) | test('Watched results retain the field, pause AI and survive save/load until acknowledgement',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u}=arena({reviewBattleResult:true});for(const x of b.units)g.officer(x.id).hasHorse=true;g.battleAction('flee',{unit:u.id,confirmArmy:true});;;;const h=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains),{},g.terrains,{reviewBattleResult:true});;;;;;}) | assert.equal(g.s.battle,b)<br>assert(b.awaitingResult)<br>assert.equal(g.aiStep(),false)<br>assert(h.s.battle.awaitingResult)<br>assert.equal(h.acknowledgeBattleResult(),true)<br>assert.equal(h.s.battle,null)<br>assert.equal(h.s.lastBattle.winner,'defend')<br>assert.equal(h.acknowledgeBattleResult(),false) |
| 9. [Unwatched and headless results settle immediately without introducing a result queue](../tests/v33-tests.mjs#L39-L39) | test('Unwatched and headless results settle immediately without introducing a result queue',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u}=arena();for(const x of b.units)g.officer(x.id).hasHorse=true;g.battleAction('flee',{unit:u.id,confirmArmy:true});;const a=arena({reviewBattleResult:true});a.g.s.humanRulers=[];a.g.s.settings.battleView='personal';a.b.outcome={winner:'attack',reason:'Unwatched result'};a.g.finishBattle();;}) | assert.equal(g.s.battle,null)<br>assert.equal(a.g.s.battle,null) |
| 10. [Train excludes full-training armies and includes zero-men officers in the resulting training update](../tests/v33-tests.mjs#L40-L40) | test('Train excludes full-training armies and includes zero-men officers in the resulting training update',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);for(const id of p.officers)Object.assign(g.officer(id),{soldiers:0,training:20,acted:false});const trainer=g.officer(p.officers[0]),trained=g.officer(p.officers[1]);trained.soldiers=10000;trained.training=100;const trainee=g.officer(p.officers[2]);trainee.soldiers=101;trainer.war=50;g.execute('trainArmy',{province:9,officer:trainer.id});;;;}) | assert.equal(trainer.training,100)<br>assert.equal(trainee.training,100)<br>assert.equal(trained.training,100) |

## tests/v34-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v34-tests.mjs](../tests/v34-tests.mjs). Purpose: Defeated humans choose individually after result; cancellation/invalid destinations do not roll or mutate; Compulsory field retreat can fail without adjacent enemies, using native flight rather than INT capture chance; No exit causes capture without RNG, even for a mounted officer; friendly neighbours permit retreat; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/monthly-events.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Defeated humans choose individually after result; cancellation/invalid destinations do not roll or mutate](../tests/v34-tests.mjs#L8-L8) | test('Defeated humans choose individually after result; cancellation/invalid destinations do not roll or mutate',()=&gt;{const {g,b}=battle();g.finishBattle();;;;const first=g.defeatedWithdrawal()[0];;const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;g.officer(first.id).hasHorse=true;g.retreatDefeatedUnit(first.id,b.source);;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains);const h=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains),{},g.terrains,{reviewBattleResult:true});const next=h.defeatedWithdrawal()[0];h.officer(next.id).hasHorse=true;h.retreatDefeatedUnit(next.id,b.source);;;;;validateSave(h.s,scenarios,h.terrains);}) | assert(b.awaitingResult)<br>assert.equal(g.acknowledgeBattleResult(),false)<br>assert(b.resultReviewed)<br>assert(first)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;g.retreatDefeatedUnit(first.id,b.target))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert(first.fled)<br>assert(first.retreatProvince===b.source)<br>assert(g.defeatedWithdrawal().length===1)<br>assert(!h.defeatedWithdrawal().length)<br>assert(h.acknowledgeBattleResult())<br>assert(h.province(b.source).officers.includes(first.id))<br>assert(h.province(b.source).officers.includes(next.id)) |
| 2. [Compulsory field retreat can fail without adjacent enemies, using native flight rather than INT capture chance](../tests/v34-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Compulsory field retreat can fail without adjacent enemies, using native flight rather than INT capture chance',()=&gt;{const {g,b}=battle();g.s.humanRulers=[];for(const u of b.units.filter(u=&gt;u.side==='attack'))Object.assign(u,{war:0,training:0,mobility:0,soldiers:100,int:100});g.random=()=&gt;.99;g.finishBattle();;;;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains);}) | assert(b.units.filter(u=&gt;u.side==='attack').every(u=&gt;u.captured&amp;&amp;!u.fled))<br>assert(b.messages.some(x=&gt;x.text.includes('tried to flee — captured')))<br>assert(g.acknowledgeBattleResult()) |
| 3. [No exit causes capture without RNG, even for a mounted officer; friendly neighbours permit retreat](../tests/v34-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('No exit causes capture without RNG, even for a mounted officer; friendly neighbours permit retreat',()=&gt;{const {g,b}=battle();for(const id of g.province(b.target).neighbors){const p=g.province(id);p.owner=b.defender;for(const x of p.officers)g.officer(x).owner=b.defender;}g.normalize();for(const u of b.units.filter(u=&gt;u.side==='attack'))g.officer(u.id).hasHorse=true;g.random=()=&gt;{throw Error('No escape roll without destination');};g.finishBattle();;;}) | assert(b.units.filter(u=&gt;u.side==='attack').every(u=&gt;u.captured))<br>assert(b.messages.some(x=&gt;x.text.includes('no adjacent'))) |
| 4. [Automatic field withdrawal prefers the original friendly province and does not reroll on repeated finish/save-load](../tests/v34-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Automatic field withdrawal prefers the original friendly province and does not reroll on repeated finish/save-load',()=&gt;{const {g,b}=battle();g.s.humanRulers=[];for(const u of b.units.filter(u=&gt;u.side==='attack'))g.officer(u.id).hasHorse=true;g.finishBattle();;const before=JSON.stringify(b);g.random=()=&gt;{throw Error('Disposed troops must not roll again');};g.finishBattle();;new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains),{},g.terrains,{reviewBattleResult:true});}) | assert(b.units.filter(u=&gt;u.side==='attack').every(u=&gt;u.fled&amp;&amp;u.retreatProvince===b.source))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(b),before) |
| 5. [Governor rebellion cannot recycle the ruler ID of an active exile party](../tests/v34-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Governor rebellion cannot recycle the ruler ID of an active exile party',()=&gt;{const {g}=battle(false);g.s.battle=null;const retired=g.ruler(12);for(const q of g.s.provinces.filter(p=&gt;p.owner===retired.id)){q.owner=255;q.officers=[];q.governor=null;}for(let id=0;id&lt;16;id++)if(!g.ruler(id))g.s.rulers.push({id,leader:1,name:'Retired',alliances:[]});g.s.roaming.push({owner:retired.id,province:9,officers:[retired.leader],gold:0,food:0});const leader=retired.leader,p=g.province(9);p.governor=33;disaster(g,p,'rebellion',{events:[],details:[]});;;}) | assert.equal(g.ruler(retired.id).leader,leader)<br>assert.notEqual(p.owner,retired.id) |

| 2. [Defender F/R retains field units and undeployed local reserves](../tests/v35-tests.mjs#L22-L25) | test('Defender F/R retains field units and undeployed local reserves',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh();g.invade({province:9,target:8,officers:[0],governor:33,food:20000});const b=g.s.battle,info=armySummary(g,b,'defend'); ;; }) | assert.equal(info.generals,b.units.filter(u=&gt;u.side==='defend').length)<br>assert.equal(info.remaining,g.province(8).officers.filter(id=&gt;!b.units.some(u=&gt;u.id===id)).length) |

| 7. [Wake lock coalesces requests, releases when dimming is enabled and reacquires after visibility loss](../tests/v35-tests.mjs#L47-L55) | test('Wake lock coalesces requests, releases when dimming is enabled and reacquires after visibility loss',async()=&gt;{ const document={visibilityState:'visible'},locks=[],messages=[];let requests=0; const navigator={wakeLock:{async request(type){;requests++;const lock={released:false,listener:null,addEventListener(_,fn){this.listener=fn;},async release(){this.released=true;this.listener?.();}};locks.push(lock);return lock;}}}; const wake=createScreenWakeLock({navigator,document,onChange:x=&gt;messages.push(x)}); await Promise.all([wake.setEnabled(true),wake.setEnabled(true)]);;; document.visibilityState='hidden';await wake.setEnabled(true);;; document.visibilityState='visible';await wake.setEnab … (complete input/procedure at linked L47–55) | assert.equal(type,'screen')<br>assert.equal(requests,1)<br>assert(wake.active)<br>assert(locks[0].released)<br>assert(!wake.active)<br>assert.equal(requests,2)<br>assert(wake.active)<br>assert(locks[1].released)<br>assert(!wake.active)<br>assert.equal(messages.at(-1),'Screen dimming allowed.') |
| 8. [A pending wake request is released if game exits before it resolves](../tests/v35-tests.mjs#L56-L60) | test('A pending wake request is released if game exits before it resolves',async()=&gt;{ let resolve;const lock={released:false,async release(){this.released=true;},addEventListener(){}}; const wake=createScreenWakeLock({navigator:{wakeLock:{request:()=&gt;new Promise(r=&gt;resolve=r)}},document:{visibilityState:'visible'}}); const request=wake.setEnabled(true);await wake.setEnabled(false);resolve(lock);await request;;; }) | assert(lock.released)<br>assert(!wake.active) |
| 9. [Unsupported or refused wake locks fail gracefully and permit a later gesture retry](../tests/v35-tests.mjs#L61-L65) | test('Unsupported or refused wake locks fail gracefully and permit a later gesture retry',async()=&gt;{ const messages=[],document={visibilityState:'visible'};const unsupported=createScreenWakeLock({navigator:{},document,onChange:x=&gt;messages.push(x)});await unsupported.setEnabled(true);;; let requests=0;const wake=createScreenWakeLock({navigator:{wakeLock:{async request(){requests++;throw Error('Battery saver');}}},document,onChange:x=&gt;messages.push(x)}); await wake.setEnabled(true);await wake.setEnabled(true);;; }) | assert(!unsupported.active)<br>assert(messages.at(-1).includes('unavailable'))<br>assert.equal(requests,2)<br>assert(messages.at(-1).includes('declined')) |

## tests/v38-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v38-tests.mjs](../tests/v38-tests.mjs). Purpose: AI domestic reports survive short-log eviction, monthly rollover and reload; only one completed round is retained; A real AI governance action is logged nationally with its actual faction; Li Su treasure is national, raises loyalty to 100 and INT by the actual native capped gain; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `config/scenarios.json`, `config/province-terrain.json`, `./assets/sounds/audiomass-horse_gallop.mp3`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/event-chronicle.mjs`, `../mjs/audio.mjs`.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [AI domestic reports survive short-log eviction, monthly rollover and reload; only one completed round is retained](../tests/v38-tests.mjs#L8-L14) | test('AI domestic reports survive short-log eviction, monthly rollover and reload; only one completed round is retained',()=&gt;{ const g=fresh();g.s.log=[];g.s.currentReports=[];g.s.player=1;g.random=()=&gt;.99;const p=g.s.provinces.find(p=&gt;p.owner===1),o=g.officer(p.governor);o.acted=false;p.land=50;const message=g.execute('develop',{province:p.id,officer:o.id,amount:100}); ;; for(let i=0;i&lt;250;i++)g.record('Day '+(i%30+1)+': orders.','war');;; g.endMonth();;;; const h=new Game(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrain),{},terrain);;h.endMonth();;;; }) | assert(acrossChinaChronology(g).some(e=&gt;e.text===message))<br>assert(provinceCurrentEvents(g)[p.id-1].events.some(e=&gt;e.text===message))<br>assert(!g.s.log.some(x=&gt;x.text===message))<br>assert(acrossChinaChronology(g).some(e=&gt;e.text===message))<br>assert.equal(g.s.month,2)<br>assert(acrossChinaChronology(g).some(e=&gt;e.text===message&amp;&amp;e.month===1))<br>assert(provinceCurrentEvents(g)[p.id-1].events.some(e=&gt;e.text===message))<br>assert(acrossChinaChronology(h).some(e=&gt;e.text===message))<br>assert(!acrossChinaChronology(h).some(e=&gt;e.text===message))<br>assert(!provinceCurrentEvents(h)[p.id-1].events.some(e=&gt;e.text===message))<br>assert(!JSON.stringify(h.s).includes(message)) |
| 2. [A real AI governance action is logged nationally with its actual faction](../tests/v38-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('A real AI governance action is logged nationally with its actual faction',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();g.s.player=1;g.s.ai.aggression=0;for(let i=0;i&lt;40&amp;&amp;!acrossChinaChronology(g).some(e=&gt;e.ai);i++)g.aiStep();;}) | assert(acrossChinaChronology(g).some(e=&gt;e.ai&amp;&amp;e.actor===1)) |
| 3. [Li Su treasure is national, raises loyalty to 100 and INT by the actual native capped gain](../tests/v38-tests.mjs#L16-L19) | test('Li Su treasure is national, raises loyalty to 100 and INT by the actual native capped gain',()=&gt;{ for(const roll of [0,.999]){const g=fresh();g.s.player=8;g.s.humanRulers=[8];const o=g.s.officers.find(x=&gt;x.name==='Li Su'),p=g.s.provinces.find(p=&gt;p.officers.includes(o.id)),before=o.int;g.s.spoils=[{owner:8,province:p.id,item:'heavenbook'}];g.random=()=&gt;roll;const text=g.decideSpoils('protect',o.id);;;;;;const s=validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrain);;;} const g=fresh(),o=g.officer(34);o.int=98;g.s.spoils=[{owner:0,province:9,item:'heavenbook'}];g.random=()=&gt;.999;g.decideSpoils('protect',34);;; }) | assert.equal(o.loyalty,100)<br>assert.equal(o.int,before+(roll===0?5:9))<br>assert(text.includes('INT +'+(o.int-before)))<br>assert(acrossChinaChronology(g).some(e=&gt;e.text===text))<br>assert(!provinceCurrentEvents(g)[p.id-1].events.some(e=&gt;e.text===text))<br>assert.equal(s.officers[o.id].int,o.int)<br>assert.equal(s.items[0].gain,o.int-before)<br>assert.equal(o.int,100)<br>assert.equal(g.s.items[0].gain,2) |
