<a id="tests-v39-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v39-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v39-tests.mjs](../tests/v39-tests.mjs). Purpose: Inspection chooses the next eligible unit and preserves campaign bytes and RNG; Jungle diagnostics use real weighted lanes and mark the preferred middle forest; Hidden invaders cannot appear in forest forecasts or inflate lane scores; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: inline fixtures and helper arguments defined in this script. Dependencies/entry points: `node:assert/strict`, `@napi-rs/canvas`, `../mjs/ai-diagnostics.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-parameters.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Inspection chooses the next eligible unit and preserves campaign bytes and RNG](../tests/v39-tests.mjs#L15-L18) | test('Inspection chooses the next eligible unit and preserves campaign bytes and RNG',()=&gt;{ const g=fixture(),before=JSON.stringify(g.s),d=battleAIDiagnostics(g);;; g.s.battle.units[2].ordered=true;;; }) | assert.equal(d.unit.id,3)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.equal(battleAIDiagnostics(g).unit.id,4)<br>assert.equal(battleAIDiagnostics(g,'1').unit.id,1) |
| 2. [Jungle diagnostics use real weighted lanes and mark the preferred middle forest](../tests/v39-tests.mjs#L19-L22) | test('Jungle diagnostics use real weighted lanes and mark the preferred middle forest',()=&gt;{ const d=battleAIDiagnostics(fixture(),3),middle=d.jungles.find(c=&gt;c.q===6&amp;&amp;c.r===5),off=d.jungles.find(c=&gt;c.q===1&amp;&amp;c.r===5); ;;;;; }) | assert(middle.lane&gt;.6)<br>assert.equal(off.lane,0)<br>assert.deepEqual(d.goal,{q:6,r:5})<br>assert(d.route.path.length)<br>assert(d.affordable.length&lt;=d.route.path.length) |
| 3. [Hidden invaders cannot appear in forest forecasts or inflate lane scores](../tests/v39-tests.mjs#L23-L26) | test('Hidden invaders cannot appear in forest forecasts or inflate lane scores',()=&gt;{ const g=fixture(),u=g.s.battle.units[1];u.q=11;u.r=0;g.s.battle.terrain[u.r*13+u.q]=1; const d=battleAIDiagnostics(g,3);; }) | assert.deepEqual(d.forecasts.map(x=&gt;x.unit.id),[1]) |
| 4. [Route overlay follows a distant mountain gap and highlights only affordable hexes](../tests/v39-tests.mjs#L27-L33) | test('Route overlay follows a distant mountain gap and highlights only affordable hexes',()=&gt;{ const g=fixture(),b=g.s.battle;b.side='attack';b.terrain=Array(156).fill(0);b.palace={q:10,r:2};b.terrain[2*13+10]=6; Object.assign(b.units[0],{q:3,r:4});Object.assign(b.units[1],{q:2,r:0});Object.assign(b.units[2],{q:11,r:8});Object.assign(b.units[3],b.palace); for(let r=0;r&lt;11;r++)b.terrain[r*13+5]=3; const d=battleAIDiagnostics(g,1);;;; ;; }) | assert(d.route)<br>assert(d.route.path.some(c=&gt;c.q===5&amp;&amp;c.r===11))<br>assert(d.route.path.every(c=&gt;b.terrain[c.r*13+c.q]!==3))<br>assert(d.route.path.length&gt;d.affordable.length)<br>assert(d.affordable.reduce((n,c)=&gt;n+terrainCost(b.terrain[c.r*13+c.q]),0)&lt;=6) |
| 5. [Palace holding and adjacent attacks are described instead of claiming a move](../tests/v39-tests.mjs#L34-L38) | test('Palace holding and adjacent attacks are described instead of claiming a move',()=&gt;{ const g=fixture();let d=battleAIDiagnostics(g,4);;; Object.assign(g.s.battle.units[0],{q:6,r:6});; d=battleAIDiagnostics(g,3);;; }) | assert.equal(d.route,null)<br>assert.match(d.reason,/Palace guard/)<br>assert.equal(distance(g.s.battle.units[0],g.s.battle.units[2]),1)<br>assert.equal(d.route,null)<br>assert.match(d.reason,/Adjacent attack/) |
| 6. [Inspection is unavailable during placement/results, and blocked forests remain identified](../tests/v39-tests.mjs#L39-L43) | test('Inspection is unavailable during placement/results, and blocked forests remain identified',()=&gt;{ const g=fixture();g.s.battle.fire[5*13+6]=2;; g.s.battle.phase='deployment';;; g.s.battle.phase='battle';g.s.battle.outcome={winner:'attack'};; }) | assert(battleAIDiagnostics(g,3).jungles.find(c=&gt;c.q===6&amp;&amp;c.r===5).blocked)<br>assert.deepEqual(diagnosticUnits(g),[])<br>assert.equal(battleAIDiagnostics(g),null)<br>assert.equal(battleAIDiagnostics(g),null) |
| 7. [Real canvas draws independent overlays at landscape sizes without changing diagnostic data](../tests/v39-tests.mjs#L44-L51) | test('Real canvas draws independent overlays at landscape sizes without changing diagnostic data',()=&gt;{ const d=battleAIDiagnostics(fixture(),3),before=JSON.stringify(d); for(const [width,height]of [[640,393],[426,270],[1100,700]])for(const [showPaths,showJungles]of [[true,false],[false,true],[true,true]]){ const canvas=createCanvas(width,height),ctx=canvas.getContext('2d'),scale=Math.min((width-32)/20,(height-62)/22),screen=p=&gt;({x:width/2+p.x*scale,y:height/2+p.z*scale}); drawAIDiagnostics(ctx,{...d,showPaths,showJungles},{screen,scale,width,height});; } ; }) | assert(canvas.toBuffer('image/png').length&gt;1000)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(d),before) |

## tests/v42-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v42-tests.mjs](../tests/v42-tests.mjs). Purpose: A spent human order selects the next ready general; inspection preserves every order and mobility; Normal, Charge and simultaneous cues retain all involved units at their pre-combat positions; A defeated target remains in the visual cue without resolving combat again; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`, `../docs/native-fire-v42.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-diagnostics.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-battle.mjs`.

Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [A spent human order selects the next ready general; inspection preserves every order and mobility](../tests/v42-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('A spent human order selects the next ready general; inspection preserves every order and mobility',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena();g.battleAction('wait',{unit:u.id});;const orders=b.units.map(x=&gt;[x.id,x.ordered,x.mobility,x.orderDay]);for(const target of [u.id,f.id,v.id])g.battleAction('view',{target});;;g.battleAction('wait',{unit:f.id});;}) | assert.equal(b.selected,f.id)<br>assert.equal(b.selected,f.id)<br>assert.deepEqual(b.units.map(x=&gt;[x.id,x.ordered,x.mobility,x.orderDay]),orders)<br>assert(f.ordered) |
| 2. [Normal, Charge and simultaneous cues retain all involved units at their pre-combat positions](../tests/v42-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Normal, Charge and simultaneous cues retain all involved units at their pre-combat positions',()=&gt;{for(const action of ['attack','charge','simultaneous']){const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena(),before=b.units.map(x=&gt;({id:x.id,q:x.q,r:x.r,side:x.side,soldiers:x.soldiers}));g.battleAction(action,{unit:u.id,target:v.id});const ids=action==='simultaneous'?[u.id,f.id,v.id]:[u.id,v.id];;validateSave(g.s,scenarios,g.terrains);}}) | assert.deepEqual(b.lastAction.combatUnits,ids.map(id=&gt;before.find(x=&gt;x.id===id))) |
| 3. [A defeated target remains in the visual cue without resolving combat again](../tests/v42-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('A defeated target remains in the visual cue without resolving combat again',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,v}=arena();v.soldiers=1;g.battleAction('attack',{unit:u.id,target:v.id});;;;}) | assert(v.routed)<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.combatUnits.find(x=&gt;x.id===v.id).soldiers,1)<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.combatUnits.length,2) |
| 4. [Hidden jungle positions do not alter routes, movement previews or route forecasts](../tests/v42-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Hidden jungle positions do not alter routes, movement previews or route forecasts',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,v,king}=arena();Object.assign(u,{q:0,r:0});Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:1});Object.assign(king,{q:12,r:11});b.terrain[4*13]=b.terrain[8*13+8]=1;Object.assign(v,{q:0,r:4});;const route=planBattleRoute(b,u),moves=reachable(b,u),forecast=projectedAttackRoutes(b,[v]),diagnostic=battleAIDiagnostics(g,u.id);Object.assign(v,{q:8,r:8});;;;;;;}) | assert(!visibleUnit(b,v,u.side))<br>assert(!visibleUnit(b,v,u.side))<br>assert.deepEqual(planBattleRoute(b,u),route)<br>assert.deepEqual(reachable(b,u),moves)<br>assert.deepEqual(projectedAttackRoutes(b,[v]),forecast)<br>assert.deepEqual(battleAIDiagnostics(g,u.id),diagnostic)<br>assert.deepEqual(planBattleRoute(b,u,[v]),route) |
| 5. [An attempted route stops when movement reveals a jungle enemy; it never walks over that enemy](../tests/v42-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('An attempted route stops when movement reveals a jungle enemy; it never walks over that enemy',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,v,king}=arena();Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:0});Object.assign(king,{q:12,r:11});Object.assign(v,{q:6,r:4});b.terrain[v.r*13+v.q]=1;u.int=100;;const move=reachable(b,u).find(c=&gt;c.q===6&amp;&amp;c.r===3);;g.battleAction('move',{unit:u.id,q:move.q,r:move.r});;;;}) | assert(!visibleUnit(b,v,u.side))<br>assert(move)<br>assert.equal(distance(u,v),1)<br>assert(visibleUnit(b,v,u.side))<br>assert.notDeepEqual({q:u.q,r:u.r},{q:move.q,r:move.r}) |
| 6. [A revealed jungle enemy is attacked from equal/better cover and avoided from water](../tests/v42-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('A revealed jungle enemy is attacked from equal/better cover and avoided from water',()=&gt;{for(const terrain of [0,2,4,5,6]){const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena();Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:0});b.terrain[u.r*13+u.q]=terrain;b.terrain[v.r*13+v.q]=1;;v.revealedTo=[u.side];;const held={q:u.q,r:u.r},men=v.soldiers;aiBattle(g,60);;;;}}) | assert(!visibleUnit(b,v,u.side))<br>assert(visibleUnit(b,v,u.side))<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,terrain===4?'wait':'attack')<br>assert.deepEqual({q:u.q,r:u.r},held)<br>assert.equal(v.soldiers===men,terrain===4) |
| 7. [Recovered fire priorities escape a burning palace, approach a dry palace, or finish a weak commander](../tests/v42-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Recovered fire priorities escape a burning palace, approach a dry palace, or finish a weak commander',()=&gt;{const native=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(new URL('../docs/native-fire-v42.json',import.meta.url)));for(const c of native.cases){const {b,u,f,v,king}=arena();Object.assign(u,{q:c.at[0],r:c.at[1],soldiers:c.men});Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:0});Object.assign(king,{q:12,r:11});Object.assign(v,c.enemy?{q:c.enemy.at[0],r:c.enemy.at[1],soldiers:c.enemy.men}:{q:11,r:10});const side=c.nativeArmySlot===0?'defend':'attack',other=side==='attack'?'defend':'attack';u.side=f.side=b.side=side;v.side=king.side=other;b.leaders[other]=v.id;b.leaders[side]=u.id;b.terrain=Array(156).fill(0);b.palace={q:c.p … (complete input/procedure at linked L15–15) | assert.equal(order.action,c.fireFallback?'wait':c.target?'attack':'move',c.name)<br>assert.equal(distance(u,order),1)<br>assert(!b.fire[order.r*13+order.q])<br>assert.equal(distance(order,b.palace),c.name.includes('empty')?0:1)<br>assert.equal(order.target,v.id) |
| 8. [Fire escape precedes ordinary palace holding and jungle wait decisions](../tests/v42-tests.mjs#L16-L16) | test('Fire escape precedes ordinary palace holding and jungle wait decisions',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena();Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:0});b.fire={[u.r*13+u.q]:3};b.terrain[u.r*13+u.q]=4;b.terrain[v.r*13+v.q]=1;aiBattle(g,60);;;const defended=arena();defended.b.side='defend';defended.b.selected=defended.king.id;defended.king.war=100;defended.b.palace={q:defended.king.q,r:defended.king.r};defended.b.terrain[155]=0;defended.b.terrain[defended.king.r*13+defended.king.q]=6;defended.b.fire={[defended.king.r*13+defended.king.q]:3};aiBattle(defended.g,60);;;;}) | assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,'move')<br>assert(!b.fire[u.r*13+u.q])<br>assert.equal(defended.b.lastAction.unit,defended.king.id)<br>assert.equal(defended.b.lastAction.type,'move')<br>assert(!defended.b.fire[defended.king.r*13+defended.king.q]) |
| 9. [HUD explains blocked fire escape instead of replacing it with a generic route message](../tests/v42-tests.mjs#L17-L17) | test('HUD explains blocked fire escape instead of replacing it with a generic route message',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena();Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:0});Object.assign(v,{q:11,r:10});b.fire={[u.r*13+u.q]:3};for(const n of neighbors(u.q,u.r))b.fire[n.r*13+n.q]=3;;}) | assert.equal(battleAIDiagnostics(g,u.id).reason,'No affordable dry neighbour; waiting in fire.') |

## tests/v43-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v43-tests.mjs](../tests/v43-tests.mjs). Purpose: Native fire ignition is below 5%, later burning orders below 30%, equally for both controllers; Inspection and army handoff never trigger fire damage; Wait on burning ground does; Rain at day change extinguishes fire before spread; preceding orders still suffer fire; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`, `../docs/native-turns-v43.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-battle.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-diagnostics.mjs`, `../mjs/battle-presentation.mjs`.

Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Native fire ignition is below 5%, later burning orders below 30%, equally for both controllers](../tests/v43-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Native fire ignition is below 5%, later burning orders below 30%, equally for both controllers',()=&gt;{;;for(const human of [[],[0],[1]]){const {g,b,u,v}=arena();g.s.humanRulers=human;u.int=100;let ignitionRolls=[.5,.5,0,.5];g.random=()=&gt;ignitionRolls.shift()??.5;g.battleAction('fireball',{unit:u.id,q:v.q,r:v.r});;;;g.battleAction('end');;g.battleAction('wait',{unit:v.id});;;}}) | assert.equal(fireLoss(5000,.5,1),125)<br>assert.equal(fireLoss(5000,.5),750)<br>assert.equal(v.soldiers,4875)<br>assert.equal(v.weapons,4875)<br>assert(b.fire[v.r*13+v.q])<br>assert.equal(v.soldiers,4875)<br>assert.equal(v.soldiers,4144)<br>assert(b.messages.some(m=&gt;m.text.includes('after the order'))) |
| 2. [Inspection and army handoff never trigger fire damage; Wait on burning ground does](../tests/v43-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Inspection and army handoff never trigger fire damage; Wait on burning ground does',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u}=arena();b.fire[u.r*13+u.q]=3;const before=u.soldiers;g.battleAction('view',{target:u.id});;g.battleAction('wait',{unit:u.id});;g.battleAction('end');;}) | assert.equal(u.soldiers,before)<br>assert.equal(u.soldiers,4250)<br>assert.equal(u.soldiers,4250) |
| 3. [Rain at day change extinguishes fire before spread; preceding orders still suffer fire](../tests/v43-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Rain at day change extinguishes fire before spread; preceding orders still suffer fire',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,v}=arena();b.fire[u.r*13+u.q]=b.fire[v.r*13+v.q]=3;g.random=()=&gt;.9;g.battleAction('end');;;g.battleAction('end');;;;g.battleAction('wait',{unit:u.id});;}) | assert.equal(u.soldiers,3650)<br>assert.equal(v.soldiers,5000)<br>assert.equal(v.soldiers,3650)<br>assert.equal(b.weather,'rain')<br>assert.deepEqual(b.fire,{})<br>assert.equal(u.soldiers,3650) |
| 4. [Five banked mobility points traverse plain and jungle in one order; four cannot](../tests/v43-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Five banked mobility points traverse plain and jungle in one order; four cannot',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,v}=arena();Object.assign(v,{q:12,r:10});b.terrain[6*13+6]=1;u.mobility=4;;u.mobility=5;const move=reachable(b,u).find(p=&gt;p.q===6&amp;&amp;p.r===6);;;g.battleAction('move',{unit:u.id,q:6,r:6});;;}) | assert(!reachable(b,u).some(p=&gt;p.q===6&amp;&amp;p.r===6))<br>assert.equal(move.cost,5)<br>assert.equal(move.path.length,2)<br>assert.equal(u.mobility,0)<br>assert.equal(u.r,6) |

<a id="tests-v44-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v44-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v44-tests.mjs](../tests/v44-tests.mjs). Purpose: Without extensions, governance and battlefield dispatch preserve Legacy state and random draws exactly; Importing weights never activates them; global/ruler overrides and Legacy rollback are independent; Schema, unknown domains, executable-looking data, weight bounds and obsolete rules cannot be imported; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-governance.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-battle.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-profile.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-extensions.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-simulation.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-planner-governance.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-planner-battlefield.mjs`, `../tools/ai-training-engine.mjs`, `../tools/ai-training-game.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Without extensions, governance and battlefield dispatch preserve Legacy state and random draws exactly](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L17-L17) | test('Without extensions, governance and battlefield dispatch preserve Legacy state and random draws exactly',()=&gt;{for(const domain of ['governance','battlefield']){const g=domain==='governance'?fresh():arena().g,a=forkAI(g),b=forkAI(g);if(domain==='governance')aiGovernance(a);else aiBattle(a,90);runAIExtension(b,domain,90);;;}}) | assert.deepEqual(a.s,b.s)<br>assert(!g.s.aiExtensions) |
| 2. [Importing weights never activates them; global/ruler overrides and Legacy rollback are independent](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L18-L18) | test('Importing weights never activates them; global/ruler overrides and Legacy rollback are independent',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();importAIProfiles(g,{schema:1,profiles:[profile('governance'),profile('battlefield')]});;setAIMode(g,'governance','trained');;setAIMode(g,'governance','legacy',0);;;setAIMode(g,'governance','inherit',0);;setAIMode(g,'governance','legacy');;}) | assert.equal(aiMode(g,'governance'), 'legacy')<br>assert.equal(aiMode(g,'governance',0),'trained')<br>assert.equal(aiMode(g,'governance',0),'legacy')<br>assert.equal(aiMode(g,'battlefield',0),'legacy')<br>assert.equal(aiMode(g,'governance',0),'trained')<br>assert.equal(aiMode(g,'governance',0),'legacy') |
| 3. [Schema, unknown domains, executable-looking data, weight bounds and obsolete rules cannot be imported](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L19-L19) | test('Schema, unknown domains, executable-looking data, weight bounds and obsolete rules cannot be imported',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),before=JSON.stringify(g.s);for(const edit of [p=&gt;p.rulesId='0'.repeat(64),p=&gt;p.schema=99,p=&gt;p.domain='__proto__',p=&gt;p.weights.food='run()',p=&gt;p.weights.food=Infinity,p=&gt;p.weights.food=13,p=&gt;p.weights.extra=1]){const p=profile('governance');edit(p);;;}}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;importAIProfiles(g,p))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before) |
| 4. [Outdated profiles in a saved campaign fall back to Legacy without losing the campaign](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L20-L20) | test('Outdated profiles in a saved campaign fall back to Legacy without losing the campaign',()=&gt;{const g=fresh();importAIProfiles(g,profile('governance'));setAIMode(g,'governance','trained');g.s.aiExtensions.profiles.governance.rulesId='0'.repeat(64);const save=validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);;;;}) | assert.equal(save.aiExtensions.defaults.governance,'legacy')<br>assert.equal(save.provinces[8].gold,g.s.provinces[8].gold)<br>assert.equal(save.aiExtensions.profiles.governance.rulesId,'0'.repeat(64)) |
| 5. [Shadow comparisons and planner previews preserve all campaign bytes, order flags and live randomness](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L21-L21) | test('Shadow comparisons and planner previews preserve all campaign bytes, order flags and live randomness',()=&gt;{for(const domain of ['governance','battlefield']){const g=domain==='governance'?fresh():arena().g;importAIProfiles(g,profile(domain));const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);let calls=0;g.random=()=&gt;{calls++;return .1;};const c=compareAI(g,domain);;aiPlan(g,domain);;;g.s.aiExtensions.shadow=true;const a=forkAI(g),b=forkAI(g);delete a.s.aiExtensions;runAIExtension(a,domain,90);runAIExtension(b,domain,90);delete b.s.aiExtensions;;}}) | assert(c.trained&amp;&amp;c.legacy)<br>assert.equal(calls,0)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.deepEqual(b.s,a.s) |
| 6. [Governance lookahead buys scarce food rather than spending the reserve on unrelated development](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L22-L22) | test('Governance lookahead buys scarce food rather than spending the reserve on unrelated development',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),model=profile('governance');p.neighbors=[];p.food=1000;p.gold=1000;p.merchant=true;p.land=p.flood=p.loyalty=100;p.unclaimed=[];for(const id of p.officers){const o=g.officer(id);o.training=o.loyalty=100;o.weapons=o.soldiers;}model.weights.food=12;model.weights.gold=.05;const plan=planGovernance(g,model);;;const before=p.food;g.execute(plan.order.action,plan.order.args);;;}) | assert.equal(plan.order.action,'trade')<br>assert.equal(plan.order.args.mode,'buy')<br>assert(p.food&gt;before)<br>assert(p.gold&lt;1000) |
| 7. [Governance selects the officer/order pair with the greater actual province-wide training gain](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L23-L23) | test('Governance selects the officer/order pair with the greater actual province-wide training gain',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);p.neighbors=[];p.gold=0;p.merchant=false;p.food=10000;p.unclaimed=[];p.land=p.flood=p.loyalty=100;for(const id of p.officers){const o=g.officer(id);o.loyalty=100;o.training=20;o.acted=id!==0&amp;&amp;id!==34;}Object.assign(g.officer(0),{int:100,war:1,charm:1});Object.assign(g.officer(34),{int:1,war:100,charm:100});const plan=planGovernance(g,profile('governance'));;;}) | assert.equal(plan.order.action,'trainArmy')<br>assert.equal(plan.order.args.officer,34) |
| 8. [A low-WAR unit can take an immediate palace win before a stronger teammate; highlight follows the planned actor](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L24-L24) | test('A low-WAR unit can take an immediate palace win before a stronger teammate; highlight follows the planned actor',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f}=arena();Object.assign(f,{q:12,r:10,war:1,mobility:3});u.war=100;importAIProfiles(g,profile('battlefield'));setAIMode(g,'battlefield','trained');const plan=aiPlan(g,'battlefield');;;;runAIExtension(g,'battlefield',90);;;}) | assert.equal(plan.order.action,'move')<br>assert.equal(plan.order.args.unit,f.id)<br>assert.equal(policyBattleUnit(g).id,f.id)<br>assert.equal(g.s.lastBattle.winner,'attack')<br>assert.equal(g.s.lastBattle.reason,'The palace was occupied.') |
| 9. [Concealed positions and previous hidden action coordinates cannot change the trained plan or its sample seed](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L25-L25) | test('Concealed positions and previous hidden action coordinates cannot change the trained plan or its sample seed',()=&gt;{const {g,b,v}=arena();Object.assign(v,{q:0,r:10});b.terrain[10*13]=b.terrain[10*13+12]=1;b.lastAction={type:'move',unit:v.id,q:0,r:10};const p=profile('battlefield'),a=battlefieldObservation(g),plan=planBattlefield(g,p);Object.assign(v,{q:12,r:10});b.lastAction.q=12;;;}) | assert.deepEqual(battlefieldObservation(g),a)<br>assert.deepEqual(planBattlefield(g,p),plan) |
| 10. [A trained battle order applies the identical engine outcome once, including simultaneous participant costs](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L26-L26) | test('A trained battle order applies the identical engine outcome once, including simultaneous participant costs',()=&gt;{const {g}=arena();importAIProfiles(g,profile('battlefield'));setAIMode(g,'battlefield','trained');const plan=aiPlan(g,'battlefield'),expected=forkAI(g);expected.battleAction(plan.order.action,plan.order.args);runAIExtension(g,'battlefield',90);;}) | assert.deepEqual(g.s,expected.s) |
| 11. [The game-independent trainer is reproducible, learns from fixed outcome feedback and reports small samples honestly](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L27-L27) | test('The game-independent trainer is reproducible, learns from fixed outcome feedback and reports small samples honestly',()=&gt;{const adapter={weights:{a:1,b:1},evaluate:(w,e)=&gt;({score:-(((w?.a??1)-e.target)**2)})},episodes=[{target:2},{target:2.5}],options={seed:123,generations:4,population:4,episodes};const a=trainWeights(adapter,options),b=trainWeights(adapter,options);;;const report=comparisonReport(a.evaluation,evaluateWeights(adapter,null,episodes));;;}) | assert.deepEqual(a,b)<br>assert(a.evaluation.score&gt;evaluateWeights(adapter,adapter.weights,episodes).score)<br>assert.equal(report.approximateMean95,null)<br>assert(report.interpretation.includes('Small')) |
| 12. [Reusable adapters construct real, reproducible scenario episodes and paired battlefield roles](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L28-L28) | test('Reusable adapters construct real, reproducible scenario episodes and paired battlefield roles',()=&gt;{for(const domain of ['governance','battlefield']){const options={seed:987,count:2,months:1},a=buildTrainingEpisodes(domain,options),b=buildTrainingEpisodes(domain,options);;;;;}}) | assert.deepEqual(a,b)<br>assert.equal(a.length,domain==='battlefield'?4:2)<br>assert(a.every(e=&gt;e.state.humanRulers.length===0))<br>assert(TRAINING_ADAPTERS[domain].evaluate) |
| 13. [Trained shadow execution retains both recommendations and no extra live order](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L29-L29) | test('Trained shadow execution retains both recommendations and no extra live order',()=&gt;{const {g}=arena();importAIProfiles(g,profile('battlefield'));setAIMode(g,'battlefield','trained');g.s.aiExtensions.shadow=true;const expected=forkAI(g),plan=aiPlan(g,'battlefield');expected.battleAction(plan.order.action,plan.order.args);runAIExtension(g,'battlefield',90);;;;}) | assert(g.aiComparison.legacy)<br>assert(g.aiComparison.trained)<br>assert.deepEqual(g.s,expected.s) |
| 14. [Paired battlefield roles count once for uncertainty and never manufacture a narrow per-match interval](../tests/v44-tests.mjs#L30-L30) | test('Paired battlefield roles count once for uncertainty and never manufacture a narrow per-match interval',()=&gt;{const trained={score:2,results:Array.from({length:40},(_,i)=&gt;({seed:Math.floor(i/2),score:2,result:i%2?0:1}))},legacy={score:1,results:trained.results.map(r=&gt;({...r,score:1}))},report=comparisonReport(trained,legacy);;;;;}) | assert.equal(report.episodes,40)<br>assert.equal(report.independentSeeds,20)<br>assert.equal(report.approximateMean95,null)<br>assert.equal(report.battles.decisiveWinRate95,null) |

## tests/v45-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v45-tests.mjs](../tests/v45-tests.mjs). Purpose: Backward finish and expansion states are valid, repeatable, cover all six scenarios and preserve source data; Opening curriculum retains precisely the existing campaign scenarios and their shortage variants; Governance simulations ignore All battles, never call detailed orders and preserve the input save and RNG; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`, `config/ai-profiles/battlefield-v44.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `node:child_process`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-profile.mjs`, `../tools/ai-training-game.mjs`, `../tools/governance-conquest.mjs`, `../tools/ai-training-engine.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Backward finish and expansion states are valid, repeatable, cover all six scenarios and preserve source data](../tests/v45-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Backward finish and expansion states are valid, repeatable, cover all six scenarios and preserve source data',()=&gt;{const original=JSON.stringify(scenarios);for(const [stage,count]of [['finish',38],['expand',24]]){const options={stage,seed:11001,count:6,months:1},a=buildConquestEpisodes(options),b=buildConquestEpisodes(options);;for(const e of a){;;;validateSave(e.state,scenarios,terrains);;;}};}) | assert.deepEqual(a,b)<br>assert.equal(e.state.provinces.filter(p=&gt;p.owner===e.owner).length,count)<br>assert.equal(e.state.provinces.filter(p=&gt;p.owner===255).length,1)<br>assert(e.state.provinces.some(p=&gt;p.owner!==e.owner&amp;&amp;p.owner!==255))<br>assert.equal(e.state.settings.battleView,'personal')<br>assert.equal(e.state.humanRulers.length,0)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(scenarios),original) |
| 2. [Opening curriculum retains precisely the existing campaign scenarios and their shortage variants](../tests/v45-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Opening curriculum retains precisely the existing campaign scenarios and their shortage variants',()=&gt;{const options={seed:13001,count:6,months:1},old=buildTrainingEpisodes('governance',options),next=buildConquestEpisodes({...options,stage:'opening'});;}) | assert.deepEqual(next.map(({stage,...e})=&gt;e),old) |
| 3. [Governance simulations ignore All battles, never call detailed orders and preserve the input save and RNG](../tests/v45-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Governance simulations ignore All battles, never call detailed orders and preserve the input save and RNG',()=&gt;{const e=buildConquestEpisodes({stage:'expand',seed:11001,count:1,months:1})[0];e.state.settings.battleView='all';const original=JSON.stringify(e),a=evaluateConquest(null,e),b=evaluateConquest(null,e);;;for(const r of [a,b])delete r.meanDecisionMs;;;}) | assert.equal(a.detailedActions,0)<br>assert(a.abstractBattles&gt;0)<br>assert.deepEqual(a,b)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(e),original) |
| 4. [An invasion of a zero-soldier garrison is an instant result, not a detailed battlefield](../tests/v45-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('An invasion of a zero-soldier garrison is an instant result, not a detailed battlefield',()=&gt;{const e=buildConquestEpisodes({stage:'opening',seed:33566239,count:1,months:1})[0];for(const p of e.state.provinces.filter(p=&gt;p.owner!==e.owner))for(const id of p.officers)e.state.officers[id].soldiers=0;const r=evaluateConquest(AI_WEIGHTS.governance,e);;;;}) | assert(r.instantBattles&gt;0)<br>assert.equal(r.detailedActions,0)<br>assert.equal(conquestSummary([r]).instantBattles,r.instantBattles) |
| 5. [All 41 provinces must be united to claim success; a strong unfinished empire receives no win](../tests/v45-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('All 41 provinces must be united to claim success; a strong unfinished empire receives no win',()=&gt;{const e=buildConquestEpisodes({stage:'finish',seed:10001,count:1,months:1})[0],unfinished=evaluateConquest(null,e);;;const s=structuredClone(e.state);for(const p of s.provinces){p.owner=e.owner;delete p.delegation;p.delegate='manual';if(!p.officers.length){const donor=s.provinces.find(q=&gt;q.officers.length&gt;1&amp;&amp;q.id!==p.id),id=donor.officers.find(id=&gt;id!==s.rulers[0].leader);donor.officers=donor.officers.filter(n=&gt;n!==id);p.officers=[id];p.governor=id;}for(const id of p.officers)s.officers[id].owner=e.owner;}for(const r of s.rulers)if(r.advisor!==null&amp;&amp;s.officers[r.advisor].owner!==r.id)r.ad … (complete input/procedure at linked L13–13) | assert.equal(unfinished.won,false)<br>assert.equal(unfinished.cappedMonths,2)<br>assert.equal(result.won,true)<br>assert.equal(result.lost,false)<br>assert.equal(result.metrics.provinces,41)<br>assert.equal(result.elapsedMonths,0)<br>assert(result.score&gt;unfinished.score) |
| 6. [Early elimination cannot masquerade as a quick conquest](../tests/v45-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Early elimination cannot masquerade as a quick conquest',()=&gt;{const e=buildConquestEpisodes({stage:'opening',seed:11001,count:1,months:3})[0];for(const p of e.state.provinces.filter(p=&gt;p.owner===e.owner)){p.owner=1;for(const id of p.officers)e.state.officers[id].owner=1;}e.state.rulers[0].advisor=null;e.state.rulers[0].extinct=true;const r=evaluateConquest(null,e);;;;;}) | assert.equal(r.won,false)<br>assert.equal(r.lost,true)<br>assert.equal(r.cappedMonths,4)<br>assert.equal(r.score,-1000) |
| 7. [Conquest ranking prioritizes success, then capped completion time, ahead of any supporting score](../tests/v45-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Conquest ranking prioritizes success, then capped completion time, ahead of any supporting score',()=&gt;{const row=(won,months,score)=&gt;({won,lost:false,elapsedMonths:months,cappedMonths:won?months:13,score,metrics:{provinces:won?41:38},shortageMonths:0,abstractBattles:0,detailedActions:0}),a={score:-1000,results:[row(true,12,-1000)]},b={score:999999,results:[row(false,0,999999)]};;;;;}) | assert(compareConquest(a,b)&gt;0)<br>assert(compareConquest({score:-100,results:[row(true,2,-100)]},a)&gt;0)<br>assert.equal(conquestSummary(b.results).meanMonthsToVictory,null)<br>assert.equal(conquestSummary(b.results).unfinished,1) |
| 8. [The shared numerical optimizer obeys conquest ranking without changing its default scalar behavior](../tests/v45-tests.mjs#L16-L16) | test('The shared numerical optimizer obeys conquest ranking without changing its default scalar behavior',()=&gt;{const row=(wins,score)=&gt;({won:wins,lost:false,elapsedMonths:1,cappedMonths:wins?1:4,score,metrics:{provinces:wins?41:1},shortageMonths:0,abstractBattles:0,detailedActions:0}),adapter={weights:{army:1},evaluate:(w)=&gt;row(w.army!==1,w.army===1?9999:-9999),compare:compareConquest},r=trainWeights(adapter,{seed:1,generations:1,population:1,episodes:[{}]});;;}) | assert.notEqual(r.weights.army,1)<br>assert.equal(r.evaluation.results[0].won,true) |
| 9. ## tests/v47-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v47-tests.mjs](../tests/v47-tests.mjs). Purpose: all 16 ownership fills match the native ruler table, without modulo colour collisions; captured, released, executed, recruited, killed, returned and unknown commander fates, including Chinese names and follow-up reviews.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`, `../docs/native-display-v47.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle-summary.mjs`, `../mjs/raster-world.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [all 16 ownership fills match the native ruler table, without modulo colour collisions](../tests/v47-tests.mjs#L1-L7) | const scenarios=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(new URL('../config/scenarios.json',import.meta.url))),terrains=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(new URL('../config/province-terrain.json',import.meta.url))); const native=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(new URL('../docs/native-display-v47.json',import.meta.url))); ;;;; | assert.deepEqual(ownershipPalette,native.colors.map(row=&gt;row.solidColor))<br>assert.equal(new Set(ownershipPalette).size,16)<br>assert.equal(ownershipColor(255),'#a0aaa4')<br>assert.equal(ownershipColor(16),'#a0aaa4') |
| 2. [captured, released, executed, recruited, killed, returned and unknown commander fates, including Chinese names and follow-up reviews](../tests/v47-tests.mjs#L7-L18) | ; for(const human of [[],[0]])for(const view of ['personal','all'])for(const winner of ['attack','defend']){ const s=createCampaign(scenarios[0],human);s.seed=1;const g=new Game(s,{},structuredClone(terrains));g.s.settings.battleView='all';g.invade({province:9,target:8,governor:33,officers:[0,34],food:20000});const b=g.s.battle;g.s.settings.battleView=view;g.random=()=&gt;0;b.outcome={winner,reason:'Controlled display regression'};g.resolveBattle(); const row=pendingBattleResult(g);;const before=JSON.stringify(g.s),copy=battleResultCopy(g,row);;;;;if(g.s.triumph); reviewBattleResult(g,row);;; if(g.s.triumph)g.acknowledgeTriumph(); console.log(&#96;PASS ${human.length?'human':'spectator'} / ${view}  … (complete input/procedure at linked L7–18) | assert(row)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert(copy.outcome.includes(row.attacker.name)&amp;&amp;copy.outcome.includes(row.defender.name))<br>assert(copy.commander.includes(row.commanders[winner==='attack'?'defend':'attack'].name))<br>assert.equal(copy.title,winner==='attack'?'Attack victorious':'Attack defeated')<br>assert.equal(triumphBattleResult(g),row)<br>assert.equal(pendingBattleResult(g),null)<br>assert.deepEqual(validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains).battleResultReviews,g.s.battleResultReviews)<br>assert.equal(pendingBattleResult(g),row)<br>assert(battleResultCopy(g,row).commander.includes(word))<br>assert.equal(pendingBattleResult(g),null)<br>assert(battleResultCopy(g,row).outcome.includes('曹操'))<br>assert(battleResultCopy(g,row).commander.includes('李傕')) |

## tests/v48-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v48-tests.mjs](../tests/v48-tests.mjs). Purpose: two concealed jungle enemies both apply native ambush loss and produce separate animations in all six directions and both column parities; charge marches through offset hexes, plays eight flashes per charge exchange on the defender tile, returns on failure and follows resolved occupation or breakthrough; a real failed charge retains its source tile and its animation returns to that same tile; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/native-combat.mjs`, `../mjs/battle-presentation.mjs`, `../mjs/events.mjs`.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [two concealed jungle enemies both apply native ambush loss and produce separate animations in all six directions and both column parities](../tests/v48-tests.mjs#L1-L15) | const scenarios=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(new URL('../config/scenarios.json',import.meta.url))),terrains=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(new URL('../config/province-terrain.json',import.meta.url))); function arena(){const g=new Game(createCampaign(scenarios[0],[0]),{},structuredClone(terrains));g.s.settings.battleView='all';g.invade({province:9,target:8,governor:33,officers:[0,34],food:20000});while(g.s.battle.phase==='deployment')aiBattle(g,60);const b=g.s.battle,u=b.units.find(u=&gt;u.id===0),f=b.units.find(u=&gt;u.id===34),enemies=b.units.filter(u=&gt;u.side==='defend');b.units=[u,f,...enemies.slice(0,2)];b.side='attack';b.selected=u.id;b.terrain=Array(156).fill(0);b.terrain[155]=6;b.palace={q:12,r:11 … (complete input/procedure at linked L1–15) | assert(cells.length&gt;=2)<br>assert.equal(visibleUnit(b,v),false)<br>assert.equal(u.soldiers,expected.soldiers)<br>assert.equal(u.weapons,expected.weapons)<br>assert.equal(animations.length,2)<br>assert.deepEqual(new Set(animations.map(a=&gt;a.unit)),new Set(enemies.map(v=&gt;v.id)))<br>assert(enemies.every(v=&gt;!v.ordered&amp;&amp;v.mobility===10))<br>assert.equal(combatAnimationDuration(a),3000)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),state)<br>assert.equal(g.s.seed,seed) |
| 2. [charge marches through offset hexes, plays eight flashes per charge exchange on the defender tile, returns on failure and follows resolved occupation or breakthrough](../tests/v48-tests.mjs#L15-L22) | ; for(const from of [{q:6,r:4},{q:7,r:4}])for(let d=0;d&lt;6;d++){ const target=direction(from.q,from.r,d),action={type:'charge',unit:1,target:2,combatUnits:[{id:1,...from},{id:2,...target}],landing:{...from}}; ;;const middle=chargePosition(action,{id:1},325),a=tilePoint(from.q,from.r),z=tilePoint(target.q,target.r);;; for(let cycle=0;cycle&lt;80;cycle++){;;;} ;action.landing={...target};;action.landing=direction(target.q,target.r,d);; } | assert.equal(combatAnimationDuration(action),7300)<br>assert.deepEqual(chargePosition(action,{id:1},0),{...tilePoint(from.q,from.r),walk:true})<br>assert.equal(middle.x,(a.x+z.x)/2)<br>assert.equal(middle.z,(a.z+z.z)/2)<br>assert.equal(combatAnimationPhase(action,650+cycle*75).cycle,cycle+1)<br>assert.equal(combatAnimationPhase(action,688+cycle*75).frame,1)<br>assert.deepEqual(chargePosition(action,{id:1},650+cycle*75),{...z,walk:false})<br>assert.deepEqual(chargePosition(action,{id:1},7300),{...a,walk:true})<br>assert.deepEqual(chargePosition(action,{id:1},7300),{...z,walk:false})<br>assert.deepEqual(chargePosition(action,{id:1},7300),{...tilePoint(action.landing.q,action.landing.r),walk:true}) |
| 3. [a real failed charge retains its source tile and its animation returns to that same tile](../tests/v48-tests.mjs#L22-L26) | ; { const {g,b,u,enemies}=arena(),target=enemies[0];Object.assign(u,{q:6,r:4,war:0});Object.assign(target,{q:7,r:4,war:0});Object.assign(enemies[1],{q:0,r:11});act(g,'charge',{unit:u.id,target:target.id});;;;const action={...b.lastAction,landing:{q:u.q,r:u.r}};; } | assert(target.soldiers&gt;0&amp;&amp;u.soldiers&gt;0)<br>assert.deepEqual({q:u.q,r:u.r},{q:6,r:4})<br>assert(!b.lastChargeLanding)<br>assert.deepEqual(chargePosition(action,u,7300),{...tilePoint(6,4),walk:true}) |
| 4. [injury presentation keeps recovery information and removes redundant zero impacts without changing the saved event](../tests/v48-tests.mjs#L26-L29) | ; const g=new Game(createCampaign(scenarios[0],[0]),{},terrains),injury={kind:'month',art:'medical',title:'Battle injury · Chenliu',province:9,text:'Cao Hong was injured in battle. Recovery expected in 3 months. Impact: people 0 unchanged; soldiers 0 unchanged; gold 0 unchanged; food 0 unchanged; land 0 unchanged; flood control 0 unchanged.',impact:{people:0,soldiers:0,gold:0,food:0,land:0,flood:0}}; const before=JSON.stringify(injury);;;; | assert.equal(presentEvent(g,injury).text,'Cao Hong was injured in battle. Recovery expected in 3 months.')<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(injury),before)<br>assert(presentEvent(g,{...injury,art:'flood',title:'Flood'}).text.includes('Impact:')) |

| 1. [Both outcomes land remaining field food/gold at the battlefield; winning armies carry their own equipment](../tests/v49-tests.mjs#L19-L19) | test('Both outcomes land remaining field food/gold at the battlefield; winning armies carry their own equipment',()=&gt;{for(const winner of ['attack','defend']){const {g,b,u}=arena(),source=g.province(b.source),target=g.province(b.target),before={gold:source.gold,food:source.food};b.food={attack:12345,defend:54321};b.outcome={winner,reason:'Test settlement'};g.resolveBattle();;;;;if(winner==='attack'){;;;;}validateSave(g.s,scenarios,terrains);}}) | assert.equal(source.gold,before.gold)<br>assert.equal(source.food,before.food)<br>assert.equal(target.gold,579)<br>assert.equal(target.food,66666)<br>assert(target.officers.includes(u.id))<br>assert.equal(g.officer(u.id).weapons,4000)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(u.id).soldiers,5000)<br>assert.equal(g.officer(u.id).training,52) |
| 2. [Native treasury caps apply to combined stores after either result](../tests/v49-tests.mjs#L20-L20) | test('Native treasury caps apply to combined stores after either result',()=&gt;{for(const winner of ['attack','defend']){const {g,b}=arena();b.gold={attack:25000,defend:25000};b.food={attack:2000000,defend:2000000};b.outcome={winner,reason:'Test caps'};g.resolveBattle();;;}}) | assert.equal(g.province(8).gold,30000)<br>assert.equal(g.province(8).food,3000000) |
| 3. [Weapons purchases are INT-independent and allow a final partially filled lot](../tests/v49-tests.mjs#L21-L21) | test('Weapons purchases are INT-independent and allow a final partially filled lot',()=&gt;{for(const int of [0,40,80,100])for(const weapons of [0,9899,9900,9999]){const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9),actor=g.officer(34),recipient=g.officer(33);p.merchant=true;actor.int=int;recipient.weapons=weapons;const before=p.gold,max=100-Math.floor(weapons/100),message=g.execute('trade',{province:9,officer:34,recipient:33,mode:'arms',amount:max});;;;}}) | assert.equal(recipient.weapons,10000)<br>assert.equal(p.gold,before-max)<br>assert(message.includes(String(10000-weapons))) |
| 4. [Merchant availability and trained candidates admit the native final partial weapons lot](../tests/v49-tests.mjs#L22-L22) | test('Merchant availability and trained candidates admit the native final partial weapons lot',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);g.s.monthlyReview=null;p.merchant=true;for(const id of p.officers)Object.assign(g.officer(id),{weapons:10000,soldiers:0});const o=g.officer(34);Object.assign(o,{soldiers:10000,weapons:9999,war:100,training:100});;;o.weapons=10000;;}) | assert.equal(orderUnavailable(g,p,'trade:arms'),null)<br>assert(governanceCandidates(g,AI_WEIGHTS.governance).some(c=&gt;c.action==='trade'&amp;&amp;c.args.mode==='arms'&amp;&amp;c.args.recipient===34&amp;&amp;c.args.amount===1))<br>assert(orderUnavailable(g,p,'trade:arms')) |
| 5. [Give and development advice find the cheapest native capped maximum under the selected actor](../tests/v49-tests.mjs#L23-L23) | test('Give and development advice find the cheapest native capped maximum under the selected actor',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);g.ruler().advisor=33;g.officer(33).int=100;g.s.monthlyAdvice.intelligence[33]=100;p.loyalty=99;p.population=100000;p.food=10000;const best=bestOrderSpend(g,'Give',34);;;for(let amount=1;amount&lt;best.amount;amount++);;p.land=99;const development=bestOrderSpend(g,'Cultivate',34);;;p.loyalty=100;;}) | assert.equal(best.gain,1)<br>assert(best.amount&lt;10000)<br>assert(g.preview('relief',9,34,amount)&lt;best.gain)<br>assert(orderInsight(g,'Give food to the people',34).includes('using '+best.amount.toLocaleString()+' food'))<br>assert.equal(development.gain,1)<br>assert(orderInsight(g,'Cultivate',34,{amount:100}).includes('smaller amount'))<br>assert(orderInsight(g,'Give',34).includes('give no food')) |
| 6. [Gold advice preserves random bonus and cap, and council text refreshes after loyalty changes](../tests/v49-tests.mjs#L24-L24) | test('Gold advice preserves random bonus and cap, and council text refreshes after loyalty changes',()=&gt;{const g=fresh(),p=g.province(9);g.ruler().advisor=33;g.officer(33).int=100;g.s.monthlyAdvice.intelligence[33]=100;for(const id of p.officers)g.officer(id).loyalty=100;g.officer(34).loyalty=99;const best=bestOrderSpend(g,'Reward',34);;;const text=orderInsight(g,'Reward · Gold',34,{amount:100});;;;g.officer(34).loyalty=100;;const spy=adviceTopic(g,p,'spy');;;}) | assert.equal(best.gain,1)<br>assert(best.amount&lt;100)<br>assert(text.includes('99 → 100–100'))<br>assert(text.includes('smaller recommended amount'))<br>assert(!adviceTopic(g,p,'officers').includes('dependable'))<br>assert(adviceTopic(g,p,'officers').includes('all subordinates have loyalty 100'))<br>assert(!spy.includes('loyalty is strong'))<br>assert(spy.includes('does not lower loyalty')) |
| 7. [Jungle relocation cannot reveal a new position to Legacy routes or trained observations/plans](../tests/v49-tests.mjs#L25-L25) | test('Jungle relocation cannot reveal a new position to Legacy routes or trained observations/plans',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,v}=arena();Object.assign(v,{q:7,r:4});b.terrain[4*13+7]=b.terrain[5*13+7]=1;b.lastKnown.attack[v.id]={q:7,r:4};;const model={schema:1,policyVersion:AI_POLICY_VERSION,id:'v49-test',name:'v49',domain:'battlefield',rulesId:AI_RULES_ID,weights:structuredClone(AI_WEIGHTS.battlefield)},before=JSON.stringify(g.s),observation=battlefieldObservation(g),route=planBattleRoute(b,u),moves=reachable(b,u),plan=planBattlefield(g,model);;Object.assign(v,{q:7,r:5});;;;;;;}) | assert(!visibleUnit(b,v,'attack'))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert(!visibleUnit(b,v,'attack'))<br>assert.deepEqual(battlefieldObservation(g),observation)<br>assert.deepEqual(planBattleRoute(b,u),route)<br>assert.deepEqual(reachable(b,u),moves)<br>assert.deepEqual(planBattlefield(g,model),plan)<br>assert.deepEqual(blindAttackOrder(b,u),{action:'attack',q:7,r:4}) |

[Blind jungle attack can miss the remembered hex and spends an order without harming the relocated enemy](../tests/v49-tests.mjs#L26-L26) | test('Blind jungle attack can miss the remembered hex and spends an order without harming the relocated enemy',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,v}=arena();b.terrain[4*13+7]=b.terrain[5*13+7]=1;b.lastKnown.attack[v.id]={q:7,r:4};Object.assign(v,{q:7,r:5});const men=v.soldiers;const message=act(g,'attack',{unit:u.id,q:7,r:4});;;;;;}) | assert(message.includes('no enemy was there'))<br>assert.equal(v.soldiers,men)<br>assert(u.ordered)<br>assert(!visibleUnit(b,v,u.side))<br>assert.equal(b.lastKnown.attack[v.id],undefined) |

[An empty burning palace destroys 30% defender stores daily; rain cancels the penalty](../tests/v49-tests.mjs#L31-L31) | test('An empty burning palace destroys 30% defender stores daily; rain cancels the penalty',()=&gt;{for(const rain of [false,true]){const {g,b}=arena();for(const u of b.units)u.ordered=true;b.fire[155]=3;b.food={attack:200000,defend:200000};b.gold={attack:999,defend:999};const ration=battleDailyFood(battleRationMen(g,b,'defend'));g.random=()=&gt;rain?.9:.1;act(g,'end');;;;;}}) | assert.equal(b.gold.defend,rain?999:699)<br>assert.equal(b.gold.attack,999)<br>assert.equal(b.food.defend,rain?200000-ration:Math.floor((200000-ration)*70/100))<br>assert.equal(g.province(8).population,scenarios[0].provinces[7].population) |
| 14. [A ready adjacent replacement reoccupies the dry palace before attacking; trained and Legacy agree](../tests/v49-tests.mjs#L32-L32) | test('A ready adjacent replacement reoccupies the dry palace before attacking; trained and Legacy agree',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,v,king}=arena();b.side='defend';b.palace={q:6,r:5};b.terrain[5*13+6]=6;Object.assign(v,{q:7,r:4,ordered:false});king.ordered=true;const order=palaceReturnOrder(b);;;;const model={weights:AI_WEIGHTS.battlefield};;aiBattle(g,90);;;;}) | assert.equal(order.unit,v.id)<br>assert.equal(order.q,6)<br>assert.equal(order.r,5)<br>assert.deepEqual(planBattlefield(g,model).order.args,{unit:v.id,q:6,r:5})<br>assert.equal(v.q,6)<br>assert.equal(v.r,5)<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,'move') |
| 15. [Tile description compares actual adjacent jungle, hill and water and explains Charge equivalence](../tests/v49-tests.mjs#L33-L33) | test('Tile description compares actual adjacent jungle, hill and water and explains Charge equivalence',()=&gt;{const {b}=arena();b.terrain[4*13+6]=2;for(const [i,terrain]of [[0,1],[1,2],[2,4]]){const cell=direction(6,4,i);b.terrain[cell.r*13+cell.q]=terrain;}const text=tileCombatInfo(b,6,4);for(const word of ['Jungle','Hill','Water','1–10','same equation']);;}) | assert(text.includes(word))<br>assert(!text.includes('vs plain')) |
| 16. [All historical faces remain unique, iconic exceptions persist, and custom names cannot borrow a historic face](../tests/v49-tests.mjs#L34-L34) | test('All historical faces remain unique, iconic exceptions persist, and custom names cannot borrow a historic face',()=&gt;{const keys=OFFICER_ROSTER.map(o=&gt;{const f=portraitFrame(o);return f.atlas+':'+f.cell;});;;;;for(const choice of CUSTOM_PORTRAITS){const s=structuredClone(scenarios[0]),province=s.provinces.find(p=&gt;p.owner===255).id;const ruler=createCustomRuler(s,{province,name:'Cao Cao',sex:choice.sex,customPortrait:choice.cell});const o=s.officers[s.rulers.find(r=&gt;r.id===ruler).leader];;;;}}) | assert.equal(new Set(keys).size,352)<br>assert.deepEqual(portraitFrame(OFFICER_ROSTER.find(o=&gt;o.name==='Liu Bei')),{atlas:'officersHistoric',cell:2})<br>assert.deepEqual(portraitFrame(OFFICER_ROSTER.find(o=&gt;o.name==='Taishi Ci')),{atlas:'officersCustom',cell:12})<br>assert.deepEqual(portraitFrame(OFFICER_ROSTER.find(o=&gt;o.name==='Cao Pi')),{atlas:'officersCustom',cell:13})<br>assert.deepEqual(portraitFrame(o),{atlas:'officersCustom',cell:choice.cell})<br>assert.equal(s.customOfficers[0].customPortrait,choice.cell)<br>assert.notDeepEqual(portraitFrame(s.customOfficers[1]),portraitFrame(o)) |

<a id="tests-v50-battle-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs](../tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs). Purpose: Simultaneous status identifies each participating and defending general with actual men lost; Visible and concealed jungle targets accept simultaneous attacks with every unit loss reported; A remembered jungle position can be attacked jointly but a hidden move remains unknown and misses; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`, `../docs/native-combat-v50.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-battle.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-planner-battlefield.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-profile.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Simultaneous status identifies each participating and defending general with actual men lost](../tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('Simultaneous status identifies each participating and defending general with actual men lost',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena(),before=new Map([u,f,v].map(z=&gt;[z.id,z.soldiers]));act(g,'simultaneous',{unit:u.id,target:v.id});;for(const row of b.lastAction.lossRows){;;};;}) | assert.equal(b.lastAction.lossRows.length,3)<br>assert.equal(row.loss,before.get(row.unit)-b.units.find(z=&gt;z.id===row.unit).soldiers)<br>assert(b.events[0].includes(row.name+' lost '+row.loss+' men'))<br>assert(!v.ordered)<br>assert.equal(v.orderDay,0) |
| 2. [Visible and concealed jungle targets accept simultaneous attacks with every unit loss reported](../tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Visible and concealed jungle targets accept simultaneous attacks with every unit loss reported',()=&gt;{for(const hidden of [false,true]){const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena();b.terrain[v.r*13+v.q]=1;v.revealedTo=hidden?[]:['attack'];;;if(!hidden);act(g,'simultaneous',{unit:u.id,...(hidden?{q:v.q,r:v.r}:{target:v.id})});;;;;;}}) | assert.equal(jointAttackers(b,u,v).length,2)<br>assert.equal(visibleUnit(b,v,u.side),!hidden)<br>assert(battlefieldCandidates(g,AI_WEIGHTS.battlefield).some(o=&gt;o.action==='simultaneous'&amp;&amp;o.args.target===v.id))<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,'simultaneous')<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.lossRows.length,3)<br>assert(b.lastAction.lossRows.some(row=&gt;row.unit===v.id&amp;&amp;row.loss&gt;0))<br>assert(u.ordered&amp;&amp;!f.ordered)<br>assert(visibleUnit(b,v,u.side)) |
| 3. [A remembered jungle position can be attacked jointly but a hidden move remains unknown and misses](../tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('A remembered jungle position can be attacked jointly but a hidden move remains unknown and misses',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena();b.terrain[v.r*13+v.q]=1;b.lastKnown.attack[v.id]={q:v.q,r:v.r};;const old={...b.lastKnown.attack[v.id]};Object.assign(v,{q:9,r:8});b.terrain[v.r*13+v.q]=1;const men=b.units.map(z=&gt;z.soldiers),seed=g.s.seed;;act(g,'simultaneous',{unit:u.id,...old});;;;;;;}) | assert.equal(blindAttackOrder(b,u).action,'simultaneous')<br>assert.deepEqual(blindAttackOrder(b,u),{action:'simultaneous',...old})<br>assert.deepEqual(b.units.map(z=&gt;z.soldiers),men)<br>assert.equal(g.s.seed,seed)<br>assert(b.events[0].includes('no enemy was there'))<br>assert(u.ordered)<br>assert(!f.ordered)<br>assert(!visibleUnit(b,v,u.side)) |
| 4. [Insufficient support and direct concealed IDs reject without state changes or random draws](../tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Insufficient support and direct concealed IDs reject without state changes or random draws',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena();b.terrain[v.r*13+v.q]=1;const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:0});const solo=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;act(g,'simultaneous',{unit:u.id,target:v.id}),/visible/)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;act(g,'simultaneous',{unit:u.id,q:v.q,r:v.r}),/two friendly/)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),solo) |
| 5. [Initiator attacks first and helpers that never exchange damage retain readiness](../tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs#L14-L14) | test('Initiator attacks first and helpers that never exchange damage retain readiness',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena();b.units=[f,u,v,...b.units.filter(z=&gt;![f,u,v].includes(z))];;v.soldiers=1;act(g,'simultaneous',{unit:u.id,target:v.id});;;;}) | assert.equal(jointAttackers(b,u,v)[0].id,u.id)<br>assert(u.ordered)<br>assert(!f.ordered)<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.lossRows.find(row=&gt;row.unit===f.id).loss,0) |
| 6. [Native local flight decisions match all bounded attacker and defender witness inputs](../tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs#L15-L15) | test('Native local flight decisions match all bounded attacker and defender witness inputs',()=&gt;{for(const row of native.cases.filter(row=&gt;row.rule==='Native AI local flight decision')){const {g,b,u,f,v,king}=arena(),side=row.side&gt;=2?'attack':'defend',actor=side==='attack'?u:v,enemy=side==='attack'?v:u;Object.assign(actor,{q:6,r:4,soldiers:row.men});Object.assign(enemy,{q:7,r:4,soldiers:row.adjacentEnemyMen});Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:0});Object.assign(king,{q:0,r:11});b.leaders[side]=row.commander?actor.id:side==='attack'?f.id:king.id;g.random=()=&gt;row.randomSuccess?0:.999;const bank=g.province(b.target).neighbors.map(id=&gt;g.province(id)).find(p=&gt;p.id!==b.source);bank.owner=b[side==='attack'?'at … (complete input/procedure at linked L15–15) | assert.equal(!!aiFlightOrder(g,actor),row.withdraw,JSON.stringify(row)) |
| 7. [Visible local peril prompts flight; concealed jungle locations cannot alter that choice](../tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs#L16-L16) | test('Visible local peril prompts flight; concealed jungle locations cannot alter that choice',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,v}=arena();u.soldiers=499;b.terrain[v.r*13+v.q]=1;;;v.revealedTo=['attack'];;}) | assert(!visibleUnit(b,v,u.side))<br>assert.equal(aiFlightOrder(g,u),null)<br>assert.equal(aiFlightOrder(g,u)?.action,'flee') |
| 8. [Both battle sides execute voluntary flight instead of repeatedly waiting or attacking](../tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs#L17-L17) | test('Both battle sides execute voluntary flight instead of repeatedly waiting or attacking',()=&gt;{for(const side of ['attack','defend']){const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena(),actor=side==='attack'?f:v;b.side=side;actor.soldiers=99;for(const z of b.units.filter(z=&gt;z.side===side&amp;&amp;z!==actor))z.ordered=true;g.officer(actor.id).hasHorse=true;const bank=g.province(b.target).neighbors.map(id=&gt;g.province(id)).find(p=&gt;p.id!==b.source);bank.owner=b[side==='attack'?'attacker':'defender'];aiBattle(g,90);;;}}) | assert(actor.fled)<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,'flee') |
| 9. [Trained withdrawal previews preserve live save data and random state](../tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs#L18-L18) | test('Trained withdrawal previews preserve live save data and random state',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u}=arena();u.soldiers=99;const before=JSON.stringify(g.s),plan=planBattlefield(g,{weights:AI_WEIGHTS.battlefield});;;}) | assert.equal(plan.order.action,'flee')<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before) |
| 10. [A burning stranded unit withdraws while a unit with an affordable dry exit continues fighting](../tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs#L19-L19) | test('A burning stranded unit withdraws while a unit with an affordable dry exit continues fighting',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,v,f}=arena();u.soldiers=2000;b.fire[u.r*13+u.q]=3;for(const cell of neighbors(u.q,u.r))b.terrain[cell.r*13+cell.q]=3;Object.assign(v,{q:0,r:10});Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:0});;b.terrain[3*13+6]=0;;}) | assert.equal(aiFlightOrder(g,u)?.action,'flee')<br>assert.equal(aiFlightOrder(g,u),null) |
| 11. [Retreat destinations are reserved across simultaneous suspended wars for their recorded owner](../tests/v50-battle-tests.mjs#L20-L20) | test('Retreat destinations are reserved across simultaneous suspended wars for their recorded owner',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u}=arena(),dest=g.province(b.target).neighbors.map(id=&gt;g.province(id)).find(p=&gt;p.id!==b.source);dest.owner=255;dest.officers=[];g.s.wars=[{attacker:b.defender,defender:b.attacker,units:[{id:100,side:'attack',originalSide:'attack',fled:true,retreatProvince:dest.id}]}];;g.s.wars[0].attacker=b.attacker;;}) | assert(!retreatOptions(g,u).some(p=&gt;p.id===dest.id))<br>assert(retreatOptions(g,u).some(p=&gt;p.id===dest.id)) |

<a id="tests-v50-governance-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v50-governance-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v50-governance-tests.mjs](../tests/v50-governance-tests.mjs). Purpose: Native governor self rewards: Gold, Horse and Writings work without a visiting ruler; Self reward uses the governor monthly action; repeating any reward is rejected atomically; Acted and recovering recipients can be rewarded by a ready governor; their action is not spent; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/order-availability.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Native governor self rewards: Gold, Horse and Writings work without a visiting ruler](../tests/v50-governance-tests.mjs#L7-L7) | test('Native governor self rewards: Gold, Horse and Writings work without a visiting ruler',()=&gt;{for(const type of ['rewardGold','rewardHorse','rewardWritings']){const {g,p}=province(),governor=g.officer(p.governor),gold=p.gold,horses=p.horses;;;g.execute(type,{province:p.id,officer:governor.id,amount:40});;;;;;;;}}) | assert(!p.officers.includes(g.ruler().leader))<br>assert.equal(orderUnavailable(g,p,{rewardGold:'reward:gold',rewardHorse:'reward:horse',rewardWritings:'reward:writings'}[type]),null)<br>assert.equal(governor.loyalty,type==='rewardWritings'?50:type==='rewardGold'?58:70)<br>assert.equal(governor.int,type==='rewardWritings'?41:40)<br>assert.equal(p.gold,gold-(type==='rewardGold'?40:0))<br>assert.equal(p.horses,horses-(type==='rewardHorse'?1:0))<br>assert(governor.acted)<br>assert(!g.officer(g.ruler().leader).acted)<br>assert(!g.officer(g.ruler().advisor).acted) |
| 2. [Self reward uses the governor monthly action; repeating any reward is rejected atomically](../tests/v50-governance-tests.mjs#L8-L8) | test('Self reward uses the governor monthly action; repeating any reward is rejected atomically',()=&gt;{for(const type of ['rewardGold','rewardHorse','rewardWritings']){const {g,p}=province();g.execute(type,{province:p.id,officer:p.governor,amount:40});const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;;}}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute(type,{province:p.id,officer:p.governor,amount:40}),/already acted/)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert.match(orderUnavailable(g,p,'reward'),/already acted/) |
| 3. [Acted and recovering recipients can be rewarded by a ready governor; their action is not spent](../tests/v50-governance-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Acted and recovering recipients can be rewarded by a ready governor; their action is not spent',()=&gt;{for(const type of ['rewardGold','rewardHorse','rewardWritings'])for(const condition of ['acted','sick','injured']){const {g,p}=province(),target=g.officer(35);g.ruler().advisor=34;Object.assign(target,{loyalty:50,int:40,acted:false,sick:0,injured:0,[condition]:condition==='acted'?true:2});g.execute(type,{province:p.id,officer:target.id,amount:40});;;;;}}) | assert(g.officer(p.governor).acted)<br>assert.equal(target.acted,condition==='acted')<br>assert.equal(target[condition],condition==='acted'?true:2)<br>assert.equal(target.int,type==='rewardWritings'?41:40) |
| 4. [An unavailable governor blocks the reward despite ready recipients and a ready ruler elsewhere](../tests/v50-governance-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('An unavailable governor blocks the reward despite ready recipients and a ready ruler elsewhere',()=&gt;{for(const condition of ['acted','sick','injured']){const {g,p}=province();g.officer(p.governor)[condition]=condition==='acted'?true:2;const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;;}}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute('rewardGold',{province:p.id,officer:34,amount:40}))<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before)<br>assert(orderUnavailable(g,p,'reward:gold')) |
| 5. [Rulers remain ineligible recipients, including when governing the issuing province](../tests/v50-governance-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Rulers remain ineligible recipients, including when governing the issuing province',()=&gt;{for(const type of ['rewardGold','rewardHorse','rewardWritings']){const g=new Game(createCampaign(scenarios[0],[0]),{},structuredClone(terrains)),p=g.province(9);g.ruler().advisor=33;g.officer(33).int=100;g.officer(0).int=40;const before=JSON.stringify(g.s);;;}}) | assert.throws(()=&gt;g.execute(type,{province:p.id,officer:0,amount:40}),/subordinate/)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before) |
| 6. [Reward Horse removes stock and increases loyalty without silently granting personal equipment](../tests/v50-governance-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Reward Horse removes stock and increases loyalty without silently granting personal equipment',()=&gt;{const {g,p}=province(),target=g.officer(35);target.loyalty=50;delete target.hasHorse;g.execute('rewardHorse',{province:p.id,officer:target.id});;}) | assert.equal(target.hasHorse,undefined) |

<a id="tests-v50-presentation-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v50-presentation-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v50-presentation-tests.mjs](../tests/v50-presentation-tests.mjs). Purpose: simultaneous status shows one readable loss message per friendly and hostile participant; structured ambush animations preserve both participants independently of text or living roster; charge repeats eight native flashes for each actual resolved exchange, with finite march and return; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: inline fixtures and helper arguments defined in this script. Dependencies/entry points: `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/battle-presentation.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [simultaneous status shows one readable loss message per friendly and hostile participant](../tests/v50-presentation-tests.mjs#L1-L7) | const names={0:'Cao Cao',34:'Cao Hong',66:'Zhang Yang'},rows=[{unit:0,side:'attack',loss:23},{unit:34,side:'attack',loss:71},{unit:66,side:'defend',loss:127}]; const battle={phase:'battle',side:'attack',selected:0,units:[],lastAction:{type:'simultaneous',unit:0,messageSerial:1,lossRows:rows},messages:[{serial:1,text:'Cao Cao initiated a simultaneous attack: 127 enemy losses, 94 own losses. Each unit: Cao Cao lost 23 men; Cao Hong lost 71 men; Zhang Yang lost 127 men.'}]}; const messages=battleStatusMessages(battle,0,id=&gt;names[id],false); ; ; | assert.deepEqual(messages.slice(-3).map(m=&gt;m.text),['Cao Cao: 23 men lost (attacker).','Cao Hong: 71 men lost (attacker).','Zhang Yang: 127 men lost (defender).'])<br>assert(!messages[1].text.includes('Each unit')) |
| 2. [structured ambush animations preserve both participants independently of text or living roster](../tests/v50-presentation-tests.mjs#L7-L10) | ; const ambush={unit:66,target:0,combatUnits:[{id:66,q:6,r:4,side:'defend',soldiers:2000},{id:0,q:6,r:5,side:'attack',soldiers:4000}]}; battle.messages=[{serial:4,text:'Localised ambush text',ambush},{serial:5,text:'Localised second ambush text',ambush:{...ambush,unit:34}}]; const before=JSON.stringify(battle),animations=ambushAnimations(battle,{messageSerial:4},id=&gt;names[id]);;;;;;; | assert.equal(animations.length,2)<br>assert.equal(animations[0].target,0)<br>assert.equal(animations[0].combatUnits.length,2)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(battle),before)<br>assert.equal(combatAnimationCycles(animations[0]),8)<br>assert.equal(combatAnimationDuration(animations[0]),3000) |
| 3. [charge repeats eight native flashes for each actual resolved exchange, with finite march and return](../tests/v50-presentation-tests.mjs#L10-L11) | ; for(const exchanges of [1,3,10]){const action={type:'charge',exchanges};;;;} | assert.equal(combatAnimationCycles(action),8*exchanges)<br>assert.equal(combatAnimationDuration(action),600*exchanges+1300)<br>assert.equal(combatAnimationPhase(action,650+600*exchanges).phase,'return') |
| 4. [simultaneous flashes sequential attacker-target pairs only for actual exchanges](../tests/v50-presentation-tests.mjs#L11-L14) | ; const joint={type:'simultaneous',unit:0,target:66,exchanges:2,combatUnits:[{id:0},{id:34},{id:66}]}; const pairs=combatAnimations(joint);;;; | assert.deepEqual(pairs.map(a=&gt;a.combatUnits.map(u=&gt;u.id)),[[0,66],[34,66]])<br>assert.equal(pairs.reduce((n,a)=&gt;n+combatAnimationCycles(a),0),16)<br>assert.equal(combatAnimations({...joint,exchanges:1}).length,1) |

<a id="tests-v52-tests-mjs"></a>
## tests/v52-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v52-tests.mjs](../tests/v52-tests.mjs). Purpose: Move, Wait and Fireball helpers still exchange damage, animate and retain their completed own order; A helper that has not acted keeps its own available order after simultaneous assistance; Human attacking commander targeted by defender simultaneous attack can act when the army changes; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`. Dependencies/entry points: `node:fs`, `node:assert/strict`, `../mjs/strategy.mjs`, `../mjs/battle.mjs`, `../mjs/battle-presentation.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-planner-battlefield.mjs`, `../mjs/ai-profile.mjs`.

Outputs: each listed PASS/group or script summary on success; thrown assertions or collected failures give a failing process exit. All loop iterations in each row must pass. Asset/source assertions inspect files; state/RNG assertions inspect engine outcomes; Audio/JSDOM/Canvas fixtures inspect integration calls.

| Case / what it tests | Inputs and procedure (source excerpt; shared helpers above) | Required output / success criteria |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. [Move, Wait and Fireball helpers still exchange damage, animate and retain their completed own order](../tests/v52-tests.mjs#L9-L9) | test('Move, Wait and Fireball helpers still exchange damage, animate and retain their completed own order',()=&gt;{for(const order of ['move','wait','fireball']){const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena();if(order==='move'){Object.assign(f,{q:5,r:5});act(g,order,{unit:f.id,q:6,r:5});}else act(g,order,{unit:f.id,q:v.q,r:v.r});;const day=f.orderDay;;;act(g,'simultaneous',{unit:u.id,target:v.id});;;;;}}) | assert(f.ordered)<br>assert.equal(jointAttackers(b,u,v).length,2)<br>assert(battlefieldCandidates(g,AI_WEIGHTS.battlefield).some(o=&gt;o.action==='simultaneous'&amp;&amp;o.args.unit===u.id))<br>assert(b.lastAction.lossRows.find(z=&gt;z.unit===f.id).loss&gt;0)<br>assert.deepEqual(combatAnimations(b.lastAction).map(a=&gt;a.unit),[u.id,f.id])<br>assert(f.ordered&amp;&amp;f.orderDay===day)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;act(g,'wait',{unit:f.id}),/already completed/) |
| 2. [A helper that has not acted keeps its own available order after simultaneous assistance](../tests/v52-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('A helper that has not acted keeps its own available order after simultaneous assistance',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,v}=arena();act(g,'simultaneous',{unit:u.id,target:v.id});;act(g,'wait',{unit:f.id});;;}) | assert(u.ordered&amp;&amp;!f.ordered&amp;&amp;!v.ordered)<br>assert(f.ordered)<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;act(g,'wait',{unit:u.id}),/already completed/) |
| 3. [Human attacking commander targeted by defender simultaneous attack can act when the army changes](../tests/v52-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('Human attacking commander targeted by defender simultaneous attack can act when the army changes',()=&gt;{const {g,b,u,f,v,king}=arena();b.side='defend';Object.assign(v,{q:6,r:4});Object.assign(king,{q:6,r:5});Object.assign(u,{q:7,r:4});Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:0});act(g,'simultaneous',{unit:v.id,target:u.id});;;act(g,'end');;;;act(g,'move',{unit:u.id,q:8,r:4});;;}) | assert(!u.ordered)<br>assert(!king.ordered)<br>assert.equal(b.side,'attack')<br>assert.equal(b.day,1)<br>assert.equal(b.selected,u.id)<br>assert.equal(u.q,8)<br>assert(u.ordered) |
| 4. [Automatic ambush preserves both available and previously completed own orders](../tests/v52-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('Automatic ambush preserves both available and previously completed own orders',()=&gt;{for(const already of [false,true]){const {g,b,u,f,v,king}=arena();b.side='defend';const from={q:5,r:5},to=direction(from.q,from.r,2),cell=ambushCells(from,to)[0];Object.assign(v,from,{int:89});Object.assign(u,cell,{ordered:already,orderDay:already?b.day:0});Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:0});Object.assign(king,{q:0,r:11});b.terrain[cell.r*13+cell.q]=1;act(g,'move',{unit:v.id,...to});;;;if(!already){act(g,'end');;act(g,'wait',{unit:u.id});;}}}) | assert.equal(ambushAnimations(b,b.lastAction,id=&gt;g.officer(id).name).length,1)<br>assert.equal(u.ordered,already)<br>assert.equal(u.orderDay,already?b.day:0)<br>assert.equal(b.side,'attack')<br>assert(u.ordered) |

[Own orders renew after both army phases for either native first-side sequence](../tests/v52-tests.mjs#L13-L13) | test('Own orders renew after both army phases for either native first-side sequence',()=&gt;{for(const first of ['attack','defend']){const {g,b}=arena();b.side=b.firstSide=first;act(g,'end');;;act(g,'end');;;;}}) | assert.equal(b.day,1)<br>assert(b.units.filter(z=&gt;z.side!==first).every(z=&gt;!z.ordered))<br>assert.equal(b.day,2)<br>assert.equal(b.side,first)<br>assert(b.units.filter(z=&gt;z.side===first).every(z=&gt;!z.ordered)) |

## tests/v53-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v53-tests.mjs](../tests/v53-tests.mjs). Purpose: BUG-53-03 palace defenders and low-intelligence commanders choose joint attacks when available; BUG-53-04 human display conceals hostile jungle units throughout both army turns; BUG-53-05 forced enemy movement invokes arrival ambush without spending helper or target orders; additional cases below.

[BUG-53-03 palace defenders and low-intelligence commanders choose joint attacks when available](../tests/v53-tests.mjs#L11-L11) | test('BUG-53-03 palace defenders and low-intelligence commanders choose joint attacks when available',()=&gt;{for(const intelligence of [1,60]){const {g,b,u,f,v,king}=arena();b.side='defend';b.day=2;Object.assign(king,{q:6,r:4,war:80});Object.assign(v,{q:6,r:5,war:50});Object.assign(u,{q:7,r:4});Object.assign(f,{q:0,r:0});b.palace={q:6,r:4};b.terrain[4*13+6]=6;aiBattle(g,intelligence);;;;}}) | assert.equal(b.lastAction.type,'simultaneous')<br>assert.equal(b.lastAction.lossRows.length,3)<br>assert(!b.units.find(z=&gt;z.side===b.side&amp;&amp;z.id!==b.lastAction.unit).ordered) |
| 2. ## tests/v54-tests.mjs

Source: [tests/v54-tests.mjs](../tests/v54-tests.mjs). Purpose: BUG-54-01 native captive ruler remains Set free/Behead with zero, one or two provinces at every hostility; BUG-54-02 every native joint loss, equipment mutation and stop branch matches; helper order ignores roster insertion; BUG-54-02 AI joint native equations, human-target difficulty and geometric order match every exchange sum; additional cases below.

Shared inputs: `../config/scenarios.json`, `../config/province-terrain.json`, `../docs/native-joint-witness-v54.json`, `../docs/native-ai-joint-witness-v54.json`. | 1. [BUG-54-01 native captive ruler remains Set free/Behead with zero, one or two provinces at every hostility](../tests/v54-tests.mjs#L5-L5) | test('BUG-54-01 native captive ruler remains Set free/Behead with zero, one or two provinces at every hostility',()=&gt;{for(const count of [0,1,2])for(const hostility of [0,50,80,100]){const g=new Game(createCampaign(scenarios[0],[0]),{},structuredClone(terrains)),former=g.ruler(3),id=former.leader;if(count===2){const destination=g.s.provinces.find(p=&gt;p.owner===255),donor=g.s.provinces.find(p=&gt;p.officers.includes(34));donor.officers=donor.officers.filter(id=&gt;id!==34);destination.owner=3;destination.governor=34;destination.officers=[34];g.officer(34).owner=3;}for(const p of g.s.provinces.filter(p=&gt;p.owner===3).slice(count)){p.owner=0;for(const id of p.officers)g.officer(id).owner=0;}takeCaptive … (complete input/procedure at linked L5–5) | assert.equal(g.s.provinces.filter(p=&gt;p.owner===3).length,count)<br>assert.deepEqual(captiveActions(g,c),['free','behead'])<br>assert.throws(()=&gt;decideCaptive(g,id,'recruit'),/only be set free or beheaded/)<br>assert.equal(JSON.stringify(g.s),before) |
| 2. [BUG-54-02 every native joint loss, equipment mutation and stop branch matches; helper order ignores roster insertion](../tests/v54-tests.mjs#L10-L10) | test('BUG-54-02 every native joint loss, equipment mutation and stop branch matches; helper order ignores roster insertion',()=&gt;{;for(const f of fixtures)for(const shuffled of [false,true]){const r=replay(f,shuffled);;;;;}}) | assert(fixtures.length&gt;0)<br>assert.deepEqual(r.final,f.final,f.name)<br>assert.equal(r.targetLoss,f.targetLossSum.reduce((n,x)=&gt;n+x,0))<br>assert.equal(r.rngIndex,f.rng.length)<br>assert.equal(r.b.lastAction.exchanges,f.exchanges.length) |
