[BUG-54-02 AI joint native equations, human-target difficulty and geometric order match every exchange sum](../tests/v54-tests.mjs#L12-L12) | test('BUG-54-02 AI joint native equations, human-target difficulty and geometric order match every exchange sum',()=&gt;{;for(const f of aiFixtures)for(const shuffled of [false,true]){const r=replay(f,shuffled);;;;;;}}) | assert(aiFixtures.some(f=&gt;f.humanTarget&amp;&amp;f.difficulty===5))<br>assert.deepEqual(r.final,f.final,f.name)<br>assert.equal(r.targetLoss,f.targetLossSum.reduce((n,x)=&gt;n+x,0),f.name)<br>assert.equal(r.b.lastAction.exchangeRows.reduce((n,x)=&gt;n+x.loss,0),r.targetLoss,f.name)<br>assert.equal(r.rngIndex,f.rng.length,f.name)<br>assert.equal(r.b.lastAction.exchanges,f.exchanges.length,f.name) |

a.output.write_text(json.dumps(dict(originalSha256=EXPECTED,cases=results),indent=2)+'\n')
print(len(results),'native AI/remaster casualty, equipment, RNG and exchange counts matched, including shuffled rosters and human-target difficulty')

### Inputs, procedure and expected outputs

Catalogue and execute all 16 native effect sequences against a mocked OPLDRV boundary.
No DOS/synthesizer or sound-driver waveform parity is claimed. Original bytes stay private.

CLI, input identity and report writes (literal source):

p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('main_exe',type=Path);p.add_argument('output',type=Path);a=p.parse_args()
raw=a.main_exe.read_bytes();assert hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()==original.EXPECTED
out={'originalSha256':original.EXPECTED,'unpackedSha256':hashlib.sha256(image).hexdigest(),'imageBytes':len(image),'driverSignature':'OPLDRV','effectCount':16,'musicAcceptedIds':list(range(21)),'effects':entries,'staticFarCallCandidates':refs,'checks':checks,'checkCount':len(checks),'limits':['External OPLDRV and original song data are absent; no original waveforms or BGM melodies were recovered.','Driver interrupt and waits are hooked; the native dispatch and effect sequences execute unchanged.','Effect labels come from call-site strings and controlled native routines; ID15 use remains unresolved.','Static far-call candidates can include data and are not an exhaustive dynamically reachable call graph.']}
a.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);a.output.write_text(json.dumps(out,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps({'effects':len(entries),'checks':len(checks),'originalSha256':out['originalSha256'],'unpackedSha256':out['unpackedSha256']}))

a.output.write_text(json.dumps(out,indent=2)+'\n');print(f"{len(out['cases'])} bounded native battle rule checks passed")

## tools/verify-original.py

Source: [tools/verify-original.py](../tools/verify-original.py). Purpose: bounded original-executable or deployment/geometry verification; the source's hook boundaries are part of the specification.

Shared inputs: `main.exe`. Dependencies/entry points: `argparse, hashlib, json, struct`, `pathlib`.

### Inputs, procedure and expected outputs

Static v34 checks on supplied packed English main.exe and scenario.dat.
Unpacks the binary's own backwards RLE stream in memory. Does not execute DOS,
modify inputs or redistribute executable bytes. Offsets are unpacked load-image offsets.

    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);args.output.write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n')
    print(f'{len(checks)} binary checks passed; Liu Bei 189: {len(names)} officers. {args.output}')

## Build, training and support tools (not independent validation cases)

These scripts are not silently executed by this catalogue. Builders can replace artwork/media/data; optimizer and benchmark commands have their own inputs/outputs and do not substitute for regression tests. Run production training only on explicit instruction. Historical fallback audio generators must not overwrite driver-rendered native exports or the user's supplied clips.

## Keeping this document current

Run `node tools/generate-test-spec.mjs` after changing a test, then review the generated case input excerpts, dynamic matrices and assertions against the source. Cases are identified by their declared labels or PASS checkpoints; one matrix row may expand to many executions. Generation does not run tests, create training profiles or certify a pass. Add every new reported bug to a behavioral case or record the exceptional missing design in pending-test-case-design.md.
